### 253 subject events

- **1912.03** `vienna-life-toddler-first-words/first-word-door` tags: infancy, family, first, comic
  门外有人上楼，Klara忽然指着门说：“爸爸。”Josef Bauer看了看空门口，没有纠正。那天晚上，孩子把家里每个人的名字都叫了一遍，叫错两个，却叫得很认真。
  _effects: skill; mood 0.01999999999999999; flag +first_word_door_

- **1913.01** `vienna-life-infant-first-smile/colic-night` tags: infancy, family, first, warm, injury
  夜里Klara哭了很久，Josef Bauer抱着孩子在屋里来回走，数着地板缝。天快亮时孩子终于打了个嗝，安静下来；Josef Bauer坐在床沿，发现自己还保持着走路的姿势。
  _effects: mood -0.03; health -0.010000000000000009 (lost sleep)_

- **1913.10** `iw18-infancy.ceiling-shadows::ctx-vienna-1912::milieu-tenement/the-coat-is-just-a-coat` tags: infancy, night, wonder, gaslight, fear, family, vienna, urban
  Josef Bauer把Klara抱起来，走到衣钩下面，让孩子伸手去抓那件旧大衣。粗呢的袖口擦过掌心，Klara先愣住，随后皱着鼻子哭了一声。Josef Bauer笑着把大衣掀开，说“看，只有衣服”，灯影也跟着晃了一下。
  _effects: mood 0.03; flag +learned_the_coat_rack_

- **1914.02** `statistical_infection`
  Klara在Bauer-Wohnung, Leopoldstadt病倒了，痢疾让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1914.03** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Klara的痢疾没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1914.06** `statistical_recovery`
  Klara从痢疾中慢慢恢复，能下床喝水，但身体还虚弱。

- **1914.07** `vienna-early-childhood.stairwell-fall/neighbor-carries` tags: childhood, family, home, street, injury
  Anna Moser的煤桶磕在楼梯转角，煤块滚了一地。Klara坐在平台上，膝盖渗出血珠。Anna Moser把桶一放，夹起Klara进了自家门，用温水冲伤口，又拿一块干面包塞过来。Josef Bauer赶来时，Klara正坐在厨房凳上啃面包。
  _effects: health -0.020000000000000018 (膝盖擦破，已冲洗); food_

- **1915.01** `vienna-life-child-family-legend/stolen-inheritance` tags: childhood, family, secret, story
  Josef Bauer说，家里本来有一件东西，后来被“更会说话的人”拿走了。Klara问是什么，Josef Bauer只说是一块表，表盖里刻着姓。孩子记住了“表盖里刻着姓”，却没有记住那个姓。
  _effects: knowledge “家里有一块表盖里刻着姓的表，被一个更会说话的人拿走了。”; flag +family_legend_watch_

- **1916.01** `vienna-life-child-first-friend/shared-toy` tags: childhood, friendship, family, comic
  Emil在楼道里滚一只铁环，Klara站在门口看。Emil把铁环推过来，说“你推一次，我推一次”。两个人从楼梯口推到院子里，铁环倒下的声音把午睡的大人吵醒了一排。
  _effects: relation friend with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912; skill; mood 0.05_

- **1916.07** `p9-childhood-household.half-bed/slept-on-kitchen-bench` tags: childhood, family, household, home, siblings, illness, neighbors, injury
  床上的被子全裹在Emil身上，Klara拽了两下没拽动，索性抱着外套去了厨房。长凳短，Klara把膝盖蜷起来，后背贴住灶台，余温一会儿就散了。清早Josef Bauer进来生火，先看见一双脚露在长凳外，把自己的毯子搭上去，又往灶里添了把煤。
  _effects: health -0.02999999999999997 (睡长凳受了凉); relation kin with person-josef-bauer_

- **1917.01** `vienna-childhood-street.hoop/hoop-win` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  巷子窄，两个孩子并排推着铁环，铁环撞在一起叮当作响。Klara的钩子先绕过最后一块界石。Anton Moser不服，说再比一次，手却已经伸进口袋，摸出三颗彩色弹珠。Klara把弹珠举到眼前看，Anton Moser在旁边踢墙，踢得鞋尖发白。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-24; mood 0.05; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-24_

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.mind-sibling/sibling-carried-afternoon` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, sibling, care
  Maria Bauer哭得打嗝，手指一直朝井台方向指。Klara蹲下把Maria Bauer驮上背，沿着墙根走，数砖缝给Maria Bauer听：一、二、三……数到第四圈，背上的呼吸才匀了。天黑时Klara的胳膊麻得抬不过肩，Josef Bauer回来接过孩子，Klara扶着门框甩了好一会儿手。
  _effects: skill; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000585:child_

- **1918.10** `vienna-childhood-friends.courtyard-race/fell-on-wet-stones` tags: childhood, social, school, street
  晒衣绳上的水一滴一滴落在石板上。Klara跑到第二圈，鞋底一滑，膝盖先着地。Otto Winter从屋里端出半盆清水，蹲下冲掉伤口里的沙，又用手帕扎紧，说今天别跑了。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-otto-winter_

- **1918.11** `vienna-childhood-street.washhouse-fever/fever-recover` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  洗衣房里全是蒸汽和碱水味。Klara靠在墙边等Josef Bauer，湿衣服堆在脚边。夜里Klara开始发烧，Josef Bauer把湿布叠成长条，敷在额头，凉了再换。三天后热度退下去，Klara的嘴唇起了一层白皮，Josef Bauer的手指也裂了口子。
  _effects: condition; relation kin with person-josef-bauer_

- **1919.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-dragged-docked-pay` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  床单太长，Klara拖着一角往绳子走，布边扫过煤渣，立刻黑了一道。Josef Bauer弯腰看了看，脸沉下来，说这角得重洗，工钱扣一半。Klara接过一赫勒，蹲在盆边用清水搓那一道灰，搓到指节发红，水面上还浮着煤末。
  _effects: cash 0.01 (被扣后只剩一赫勒); mood -0.05; relation acquaintance with person-josef-bauer_

- **1920.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.mind-sibling/sibling-shelter-missed-river` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, sibling, care
  河水那边传来同伴的笑声，Klara站在院门口听了听，还是回去把木盆扶正。Hermann Bauer睡醒哭了一声，Klara把最后半块面包掰碎泡进温水，一勺一勺喂。Josef Bauer回来时，Klara正蹲在盆边打瞌睡，手里还攥着喂饭的木勺。
  _effects: mood -0.02; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000690:child; relation kin with person-josef-bauer; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7_

- **1920.08** `p9-childhood-household.fever-night/hospital-deposit` tags: childhood, family, household, home, siblings, illness, neighbors
  围巾从Klara的头一直裹到脚，Josef Bauer抱着人下楼，在电车踏板上差点滑倒。急诊窗口先收押金，Josef Bauer把内袋里的票子一张张抹平，数出六克朗。Klara躺在观察室的窄床上，头顶的灯管嗡嗡响，Josef Bauer的手一直按着被角。
  _effects: cash -6 (医院急诊押金和电车票); health 0.08 (在医院接受处理); mood -0.04_

- **1925.01** `r13-edu.school-transfer::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/old-school-withholds-papers` tags: education, school, transfer, bureaucracy, family, reform, medical, schooling
  Klara等到学期过了一半，转学证还没有下来。原来旧学校不肯放人，说Klara欠着图书费。Klara把借的书一本本找齐还回去，管理员却找不到借书卡，最后Klara自己写了一张收据，按了手印。那张收据按上手印后，管理员才从抽屉里找出借书卡，说早就在这里了。
  _effects: cash -1 (跨区转学的居住证明); mood -0.04999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9_

- **1925.03** `state_reaction:wish-power/power-humiliation` tags: wish-power, wish, wish:power, power, status, ambition, state, state_reaction
  Klara把那句轻视记了很久。它没有变成一个决定，但也没有消失。
  _effects: mood -0.09999999999999998; flag +wish:power:fixated_on_revenge_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:002608_

- **1925.05** `yf18-body.danube-cabin::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/wade-in-first` tags: body, desire, river, swimming, shame, first_love, reform, medical
  多瑙河的浴棚按男女分开，木板缝里能看见对面的人影。Klara穿着借来的毛线泳衣，站在第三间门口不敢下水；Elisabeth Bauer从水里冒出来，头发贴在额头上，说水不冷。Klara走进河里时，满脑子都是自己肋骨上那道疤。
  _effects: state_add yf18-first-awakening state:world-vienna-1912:95; health 0.019999999999999997 (cold river water and a long swim); mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +yf18_first_awakening_active_

- **1925.05** `yf18-runaway.bundle::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/buy-the-ticket-tonight` tags: runaway, escape, family, plan, night_train, reform, medical, schooling
  Klara趁Josef Bauer去上夜班，把储钱罐砸在窗台上。硬币滚进床缝，Klara趴在地上摸到最后一枚，跑去南站买了一张最便宜的单程票；售票员问要不要往返，Klara说不用，声音比自己想象中平静。
  _effects: state_add yf18-runaway-plan state:world-vienna-1912:96; cash -3 (the savings jar was broken for a ticket); flag +yf18_ticket_bought; mood 0.040000000000000036; flag +yf18_runaway_plan_active_

- **1925.05** `state_reaction:yf18-runaway-plan/a-family-question` tags: yf18-runaway-plan, escape, youth, plan, home, travel, risk, state
  饭桌上又为房租吵起来。Klara把面包掰成很小的块，听见父亲说“这个家养不起闲人”；Klara没有回嘴，只在心里把格拉茨的地址又背了一遍。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.suspicion 15 -> 23; state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.resolve 70 -> 68_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:002618_

- **1925.10** `yf18-dare.bridge-rail::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/fall-on-the-ice` tags: dare, bridge, river, friendship, risk, reform, medical, schooling
  Klara走到第四步，鞋底在霜上滑了一下，整个人挂在栏杆外侧。Rosa抓住Klara的手腕，Anton跑去叫桥头卖烤栗子的老人；Klara被拉上来时，膝盖已经湿透，第二天走路一瘸一拐。
  _effects: health -0.019999999999999997 (a fall on the bridge rail); state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 50 -> 56; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 15 -> 5; relation friend with person-phase15-Rosa-world-vienna-1912; flag +yf18_bridge_scare; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1925.11** `yf18-runaway.mother-bundle::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/lie-about-a-school-trip` tags: runaway, family, discovery, lie, plan, reform, medical, schooling
  Klara说包袱是替同学准备的，学校要去瓦豪采葡萄。Josef Bauer问是哪位同学，Klara报了一个班里最有钱的名字；Josef Bauer居然信了，还往包袱里塞了两个苹果。第二天，Klara在车站看见那个同学，才知道谎话已经开始自己走路。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.suspicion 23 -> 31; state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.preparation 35 -> 30; relation kin with person-josef-bauer; flag +yf18_trip_lie; mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1926.01** `state_tick:apprenticeship-contract/workshop-year/learned` tags: apprenticeship-contract, education, craft, apprentice, work, state, state_tick
  Klara又学会了一道工序。师傅没有夸，只把下一块料推到面前。
  _effects: state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.progress 10 -> 30; skill; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1926.01** `state_tick:yf18-first-awakening/half-year-of-glances/answered-glance` tags: yf18-first-awakening, love, youth, first_love, desire, school, awakening, state
  Klara在教堂门口或学校走廊里又遇见Elisabeth Bauer。这一次对方先点了头，Klara准备好的整段话全忘了，只把手里的书换到另一只手上；回家后Klara才发现，自己把书拿倒了。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 56 -> 61; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 5 -> 13; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.reciprocated false -> true; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1926.01** `state_tick:yf18-runaway-plan/three-months-of-preparation/someone-counts-the-savings` tags: yf18-runaway-plan, escape, youth, plan, home, travel, risk, state
  Klara藏在糖罐后面的硬币少了一小叠。父亲说家里这个月煤钱不够，先拿去用了；Klara看着空出来的那圈灰，什么也没说。那天晚上，Klara第一次认真算了一笔账：从维也纳到格拉茨，到底要攒几个冬天。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.suspicion 31 -> 46; state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.resolve 68 -> 62; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1926.01** `p9-childhood-youth-aspiration.ask-a-mentor/weekly-lesson` tags: adolescence, education, aspiration, school, books, club, exam, task
  Anna Moser 2把老花镜往鼻梁上推了推，让Klara用食指在字盘空位上比画。咖啡馆伙计来续水时，老人把字母表折起来，说下星期带旧字样本来看。
  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44; flag +typesetting_mentor; mood 0.06; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44_

- **1926.06** `yf18-firstlook.parish-dance::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/the-second-dance` tags: first_love, dance, church, courtship, jealousy, reform, medical, schooling
  舞卡上第一支曲子被Josef写走了。Klara在教会礼堂的木地板上踩错了两步，Josef说没关系，把手放回Klara背后；第三支曲子响起时，Lena在门口冲Klara比了个手势，Klara假装没看见。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 35 -> 43; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 10 -> 20; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.reciprocated false -> true; mood 0.03999999999999998_

- **1926.08** `vienna-adolescence.brothers-torn-jacket/sew-it-yourself` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  外套腋下裂了一道口子，里布翻出来。Klara把袖口套在桌角上撑开，穿针时试了三次才引上线。针脚一针长一针短，像蜈蚣。Josef Bauer端着汤进来，站在桌边看了一会儿，把碗往Klara那边推了推，自己只留了半碗。
  _effects: skill; food; relation kin with person-josef-bauer_

- **1926.12** `yf18-firstlook.tram-glove::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/let-friend-return-it` tags: first_love, love, tram, glove, youth, courtship, reform, medical
  Klara把手套交给Josef，让Josef替自己去还。Josef回来时说，Rosa问是谁捡到的；Josef顺口报了一个名字，Klara听见那名字不是自己的，笑得比平时响。
  _effects: state_add yf18-first-awakening state:world-vienna-1912:124; mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1927.01** `yf18-runaway.companion::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/stay-and-spend-the-fare` tags: runaway, friendship, companion, plan, betrayal, reform, medical, schooling
  Klara把票退了，和Rosa在普拉特门口的摊子上吃了两碗热汤。汤里有太多盐，Klara喝到一半就放下勺子；Rosa说等明年攒够钱再一起走，Klara点头，没有说自己已经把包袱收进了柜子最下层。
  _effects: state_remove yf18-runaway-plan state:world-vienna-1912:96; cash 2 (the refunded fare); relation friend with person-phase15-Rosa-world-vienna-1912; flag +yf18_stayed_for_friend; mood -0.010000000000000009; flag -yf18_runaway_plan_active_

- **1927.01** `state_tick:yf18-first-awakening/half-year-of-glances/talked-about` tags: yf18-first-awakening, love, youth, first_love, desire, school, awakening, state
  Klara只把Rosa的名字写在一张草稿纸背面，第二天那张纸就在教室后排传了一圈。有人把名字念出来，有人问Klara是不是要请人喝咖啡；Klara说那是表姐的名字，声音大得连自己都吓了一跳。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.talk 10 -> 22; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 20 -> 14; reputation -0.03; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1927.01** `state_tick:apprenticeship-contract/workshop-year/mistake` tags: apprenticeship-contract, education, craft, apprentice, work, state, state_tick
  Klara废掉了一块料，师傅扣了工钱。晚饭时谁也没提这件事。
  _effects: state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.failures 1 -> 2; cash -1 (wasted material); mood -0.03999999999999998_

- **1927.01** `state_expire:yf18-first-awakening` tags: yf18-first-awakening, love, youth, first_love, desire, school, awakening, state

  _effects: state_remove yf18-first-awakening state:world-vienna-1912:95; flag -yf18_first_awakening_active_

- **1927.01** `r13-civ.carry-message::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/searched-at-platform` tags: politics, courier, message, risk, underground, reform, medical, schooling
  站台口临时设了检查，站台上的巡警让乘客把包打开。Klara把课本抱在胸前，轮到时手心全是汗。巡警翻了两页，问为什么去那么远的区，Klara说去给姑妈送药。
  _effects: flag +searched_while_carrying_message; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-47; knowledge “the platform check was looking for printed leaflets, not let”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-28; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-47_

- **1927.02** `yf18-dare.prater-slide::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/ride-in-the-dark` tags: dare, prater, fairground, comic, friendship, reform, medical, schooling
  普拉特的木滑道在闭园后还亮着一盏值班灯。Klara和Josef从围栏底下钻进去，用一块麻袋垫着滑下来，木槽里的蜡味呛得人直笑。滑到底时，Otto Winter提着灯站在出口，问他们要不要再买一张票。
  _effects: mood 0.050000000000000044; cash -1 (a late ticket paid to the watchman); reputation -0.009999999999999995; flag +yf18_prater_stunt_

- **1927.03** `yf18-friend.two-tickets::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/give-them-away` tags: first_love, friendship, betrayal, dance, tickets, reform, medical, schooling
  Klara把两张票送给门口卖花的小女孩，自己在普拉特大街上看橱窗。橱窗里摆着一双和Hedwig一样的白手套；Klara站到商店关门，回家时说票弄丢了，谁也没有再问。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 43 -> 35; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.secret true -> true; flag +yf18_gave_tickets_away; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1927.11** `r1-cul.fan-letter::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/quoted-without-permission` tags: culture, letters, fandom, literacy, ambition, reform, medical, schooling
  Klara的信寄出半年，没有回音。后来Elisabeth Berg在一篇文章里引用了这封信，没有署名，只写一个读者说。Klara在报上读到自己的句子，先是高兴，随后发现文章里的意思和自己写的相反。
  _effects: mood -0.07999999999999996; reputation 0.03; flag +letter_quoted_without_permission; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-25; knowledge “the quoted sentence means the opposite of what was written”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-56_

  Klara把袖口卷到肘上，用麻绳扎紧，替Die Fackel把一叠纸送进压印机。前两百张很顺，第二百零一张时，纸角卡住，Klara伸手去拨，机器没有停；等Mitzi Moser拉开Klara，食指的指甲已经翻了起来。
  _effects: condition; state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.progress 30 -> 22; cash -2 (a doctor and a bandage); flag +yf18_press_injury; mood -0.04999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-49; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-20_

  Klara在教堂门口或学校走廊里又遇见Rosa。这一次对方先点了头，Klara准备好的整段话全忘了，只把手里的书换到另一只手上；回家后Klara才发现，自己把书拿倒了。

  Franz Weiss来了两次，第三次教室门锁着，黑板上写着“课程暂停”。Klara和Josef Moser在走廊里等了一刻钟，最后把绷带卷带回教室，互相缠着玩。
  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; flag +first_aid_course_cancelled; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-61_

- **1928.03** `vienna-youth-threshold.sibling-departure/coins-into-box` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, transition
  Josef的箱子摊在床板上，里面是两件衬衫、一双旧靴和母亲塞的一小袋面粉。Klara把攒了半年的零钱数到第三遍，一共一克朗二十赫勒，全塞进箱角的袜子里。Josef看见了，没说话，只把箱子扣上，铜扣响了一声。
  _effects: cash -1.2 (把攒下的零钱塞给离家的兄姐); relation kin with person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1928.10** `vienna-adolescence.workshop-apprentice/apprenticeship-rejected` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne捏了捏Klara的虎口，又让Klara张开手指，说骨节还没长开，推硬木会伤手。Josef Bauer想再问一句，Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne已经转身去搬木料。Klara退到门槛外，听见刨子重新响起来，木花从门缝里卷到脚边。
  _effects: mood -0.07999999999999996; reputation -0.05; relation kin with person-josef-bauer_

  Klara在工厂后街租了一个床位，和两个车工合住。房东太太每天收一次煤钱，洗脸水要自己下楼打。第一夜，他听着隔壁的机器声睡着，梦里还在数工票上的尺寸。天没亮，隔壁的车工就敲门叫他一起上工。
  _effects: cash -3 (行李放在楼梯口); mood 0.040000000000000036; flag +moved_to_lodging_near_work; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-20; move place-wien-printshop-winter; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-6_

  Gustav Moser说自己眼睛不行了，带不了徒弟，但从帆布袋里抽出一本旧字样本，让Klara拿回去临摹。书皮用麻线缝过三次，扉页上有一行褪色的签名。少年双手接过来，在咖啡馆门口鞠了个躬。
  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-16; flag +typesetting_specimen_book; cash -1 (咖啡馆里请老师傅喝一杯); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-16_

- **1929.07** `vienna-legacy-worklife.hours-ledger/hours-ledger.refuse` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  学徒在旁边小声说，拒绝按手印的人名字会被单独抄在一张纸上。Klara没接话，把八克朗换成一克朗的硬币，数给家里时硬币在桌上滚了两圈才停。屋里的煤油灯芯剪得太短，光只够照亮手边的桌面。
  _effects: cash 8 (追回的拖欠计件工资); reputation -0.03; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-16_

- **1929.12** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/dentist-pull` tags: health, medical, family, money
  钳子夹住牙根时，Klara听见自己太阳穴里咯的一声。医生往牙槽里塞了团浸药棉，叫咬住别松。下楼时Katharina Winter 2扶着胳膊，说嘴里的血味比啤酒苦多了。
  _effects: cash -3 (牙医拔牙费用); health 0.05999999999999999 (拔除病牙解除疼痛); mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41_

- **1929.12** `p9-gap-adult-unemployed.soup-kitchen/soup-pot-empty` tags: adulthood, unemployment, casual_labour, poverty, daily, relief, food, church
  锅底刮出焦味，Gustav Berg说今天就这些了，明天赶早。Klara把空罐子夹在腋下往回走，石板路上结了薄冰，Emil在拐角等着，二话没说把罐子递过来。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912_

- **1929.12** `vienna-illness-household.child-fever/neighbor-help-night` tags: health, family, home
  Klara正没主意，邻居敲门进来，带了热水和退烧药。孩子清晨安静下来，Klara切了半块面包还这份人情；楼道里有人问起，邻居只说孩子没事了。
  _effects: health 0.08000000000000002 (邻居帮助后烧退); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41_

- **1930.01** `p10-family-tasks.find-missing-kin/find-missing-kin.toolbox` tags: adulthood, family, social, marriage, care, inheritance, task, missing_kin
  仓库里一股潮味。Klara蹲下把工具箱打开，凿子用油布包着，刨子柄上缠着哥哥自己的麻绳。老板说人搬走两个月了。Klara把工具卖给旧货商，三克朗，买票时数了两遍。
  _effects: cash 3 (卖掉哥哥留下的工具换路费); mood -0.16000000000000003; health -0.04 (奔波找哥哥，路费和体力都耗尽)_

- **1930.03** `vienna-legacy-worklife.apprentice-wage/apprentice-wage.beer-with-senior` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  十二赫勒换来两杯淡啤酒，找零是四枚一赫勒的铜币。Klara把铜币收好，听师兄讲那年冬天的事，讲到一半师兄停了停，说“你别到处讲”。酒馆里有人拉手风琴，声音盖过了后半句。
  _effects: cash -1 (请师兄喝一杯啤酒); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-73; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-73_

- **1930.03** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-demand-repair` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Klara没有立刻答话，先去敲Gustav Moser的门。两人一起数落墙上的水渍和门框里灌进来的风，楼梯间的窗户修好了，自家墙皮还在剥落。Gustav Moser提议一起把话带给看门人：先修好漏水的墙角，涨租的事再谈。Klara把纸重新折好，请他把话带到门外。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-16; mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +rent_dispute_

- **1930.05** `ad20-census-form::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/the-form-is-correct` tags: census, bureaucracy, household, everyday, public, obligation, reform, medical
  Friedrich Hofer把表从头到尾看了一遍，说“这次对了”。Klara问上次错在哪；Friedrich Hofer说“上次你把出生地写成了住址”。两个人都笑了一下；Klara接过盖好章的表，发现纸还是热的。
  _effects: flag +census_correct; mood 0.010000000000000009; cash -1 (the census fee)_

- **1931.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/clinic-treatment` tags: health, work, family
  Klara在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Klara把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.05 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:004301_

- **1931.01** `absurd15-wish.crown-for-a-day::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/festival-king-only` tags: wish, power, crown, office, irony, festival, reform, medical
  代表团没有带来任命书，只带来一套戏服：市庆游行需要一个“国王”，一天两场，管一顿饭。Klara穿上戏服时，围观的人鼓起掌来。掌声是真的，王冠是假的，晚饭也是真的。
  _effects: state_add stage-sovereign state:world-vienna-1912:241; cash 2 (游行演出的酬劳); flag +hired_as_festival_king; flag +stage_sovereign; reputation 0.04000000000000001_

- **1931.01** `state_reaction:wish-power/power-office` tags: wish-power, wish, wish:power, power, status, ambition, state, state_reaction
  Klara坐在那张有权力的桌子后面，签下自己的名字。愿望没有因此变近，但桌上的纸张、印章和等待回话的人，都变得具体了。
  _effects: reputation 0.06; flag +wish:power:has_tasted_office_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:004518_

- **1931.01** `r13-civ.public-health-campaign::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/crowd-tears-notices` tags: health, public_health, campaign, poster, institution, reform, medical, schooling
  Klara刚把告示刷上墙，就有人围过来说这是拿疫病吓人。一个卖菜的女人伸手把纸撕下来，浆糊蹭了Klara一脸。Hedwig Stein把Klara拉到一边，说这条街上个月才埋了两个孩子。
  _effects: health -0.04 (街角防疫告示的浆糊); reputation -0.03; flag +public_health_notice_torn_down; relation rival with person-hedwig-stein; knowledge “the market crowd blames the notice for frightening customers”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-31_

- **1931.02** `ml18-inheritance.uncles-press::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/refuse-and-let-the-cousin` tags: inheritance, family, business, debt, legal, money, reform, medical
  Klara在公证处坐了很久，最后说自己没有力气再经营一间铺子。Franz Huber接过钥匙，脸上先是惊讶，随后是一种被托付的沉重。半年后，那间印刷所改了招牌，Franz Huber在门口看见Klara，只点了点头，没有提请客吃饭的事。
  _effects: cash -1 (the notary's fee for refusing the inheritance); relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-32; flag +refused_print_shop; knowledge “叔父的印刷所欠着十四克朗，订阅名单是唯一还值钱的东西。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-32_

- **1931.08** `vienna-women-housework.wash-day/wash-day.send-out` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  洗衣妇把工服从报纸里抖出来，翻开领口看了看油垢，点头收下。Klara转身回屋，铜板在围裙口袋里磕出轻响。Maria Hofer还在楼梯上嘟囔，说这钱不如买半斤猪油。
  _effects: cash -0.5 (请洗衣妇代洗工服); mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-47_

- **1931.11** `vienna-family-same-sex-female/public-suspicion` tags: family, intimacy, same_sex, housing, lifecycle, partnership
  楼里的邻居开始议论Klara和Marie Huber。有人把门牌记下来，房东来敲门，说这栋楼不接受“这种住法”；Marie Huber把箱子拖出来又推回床下，搬家花掉五克朗，两人那一夜都没睡稳。Klara在院子里被人盯着看，门上的名字也被人用手指划过。
  _effects: relation partner with person-marie-huber; reputation -0.12; mood -0.030000000000000027; cash -5 (moving and lost rent); role_

- **1932.01** `r15-wish.power-offer::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/puppet-office` tags: politics, power, wish, office, status, reform, medical, schooling
  报纸把Klara称作“新任主席”，照片很大，办公室很漂亮。真正的决定在另一间屋里做出。Klara每天签字，签的都是别人写好的东西。
  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036; reputation 0.08; position 名誉主席 @org-sdap; flag +puppet_office_

- **1932.01** `state_expire:stage-sovereign` tags: stage-sovereign, performance, theatre, pretend, status, irony, power, no-real-authority
  Klara的「舞台上的君主」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove stage-sovereign state:world-vienna-1912:241; flag -stage_sovereign_

- **1932.02** `major-crisis.rent-increase/pay-the-increase` tags: crisis, housing, rent, eviction, family, poverty
  房东没有露面，只让看门人Friedrich Stein把补款单塞进门缝：每月要多十四克朗，先交十八克朗（本月十四加四克朗手续费）才肯谈续租。Klara把准备买冬煤的钱数了两遍，还是把钱交了，收据上写的是“自愿捐助”。Friedrich Stein低声说，不签的人已经有两家搬走了，楼下的煤价牌又换了数字。
  _effects: cash -18 (advance payment demanded with the rent increase); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-76; flag +rent_increase_accepted; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-76_

- **1932.09** `vienna-parish-aid.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.leave` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Klara看见熟人排在前面，帽檐压得低低的，便转身拐进旁边的小巷。Emil追出来问怎么不领了，Klara说人太多，改天再来。回到屋里，灶是冷的，早上剩的半个土豆还在盘子里。Klara把它切成片，就着水咽下去。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912; food_

- **1933.01** `vienna-family-divorce/reconcile-for-now` tags: family, marriage, divorce, housing, lifecycle, partnership
  Klara和Marie Huber在楼道里吵到半夜，最后因为下个月的房租又坐回同一张桌。谁都没有提和好，只把账本重新摊开；Marie Huber把煤钱推过来，Klara接过去数了两遍，手指碰到一起又分开，屋里的灯亮到很晚。
  _effects: relation partner with person-marie-huber_

- **1933.01** `r15-wish.found-faction::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/founded` tags: politics, power, wish, organization, office, status, reform, medical
  Klara和Rosa在咖啡馆后间成立了一个小小的政治俱乐部。桌子是借的，章程是手写的，但签名、印章和一间可以锁门的办公室是真的。Mitzi Lechner 2没有到场，只送来一句“先看看你们能撑多久”。
  _effects: cash -2 (一间借来的办公室); reputation 0.06; organization_found 权力俱乐部 (party); position 发起人 @org:r15-faction:evt:world-vienna-1912:005362; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14_

- **1933.01** `agency:attend_meeting` tags: agency, agency:attend_meeting
  Klara去了Arbeiter-Zeitung im Vorwärts-Haus，坐在后排听完了关于维也纳社会民主党在工人街区、工会和报刊网络中持续活动，争取普选、劳动保护与社会救济的争论；散会时带走一张油印提纲。
  _effects: historical development:seed-vienna-social-democracy-1912 as participant; knowledge “我参加了维也纳社会民主党在工人街区、工会和报刊网络中持续活动，争取普选、劳动保护与社会救济。。”; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1933.03** `r2-law.residency-permit::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/permit-refused` tags: law, residency, papers, police, housing, vienna, urban, crisis
  Friedrich Hofer说Klara的合同没有在区里备案，居留许可不能续。Klara问备案要多久，Friedrich Hofer说先让房东来，再等通知。Helene Berg 2来了，听说要补三个月的税，转身就走；Friedrich Weiss在楼梯口说，这条街上被赶走的人，都是先丢了那张备案纸。
  _effects: cash -6 (居留章盖在旧地址上); condition; flag +residency_permit_denied_for_unregistered_lease; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-84; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23; knowledge “the lease must be registered before the permit can be renewe”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-84; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23_

- **1933.06** `curse15-bargain.borrowed-name::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-price-is-a-name` tags: wish, power, curse, ritual, bargain, office, irony, vienna
  Gustav Berg只要Klara在一张空白委任状上写下真名，说剩下的由办事处去办。一个月过去，职位没有来，Klara的名字却出现在一份领款单上，金额和签名都不属于Klara。Gustav Berg调走了，窗口换了人，没有人愿意查那张纸是从哪一叠里抽出来的。
  _effects: cash -2 (打点办事处的钱); flag +name_used_without_office; reputation -0.04000000000000001; relation acquaintance with person-gustav-berg_

  Klara去警察局报案，Gustav Berg先问有没有借据。Klara说有，Gustav Berg又问利息写没写进借据。听到“五分”时，Gustav Berg把笔放下，说这归民事，不归刑事；Gustav Bauer 2在门外等着，手里提着Klara的缝纫机。
  _effects: flag +reported_shark_lender; role; relation acquaintance with person-gustav-berg; knowledge “police treat the shark loan as a civil matter”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-77_

  俱乐部在一次表决中分裂。Mitzi Lechner 2带走了将近一半的人，留下的那间办公室突然显得很大。Klara把两把没人坐的椅子搬到墙边，没有扔掉。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; reputation -0.05; flag +faction_split_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:005362_

  有人翻出Klara两年前的一次妥协，说那是立场问题。Klara被移出名单，办公室的钥匙当天收回。Mitzi Lechner 2接替了位置，没有看Klara一眼。

  Klara和Friedrich Hofer在咖啡馆后间谈了一个钟头。没有人知道他们交换了什么，但第二天，两个人都投了对方的票。
  _effects: reputation 0.06; relation colleague with person-friedrich-hofer; knowledge “我知道{rival}在关键委员会里真正想要什么，也知道这笔交易的价格。”_

- **1934.07** `p21-mistaken-identity::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/a-cell-for-someone-else` tags: mistaken_identity, police, identity, arrest, public, secret, absurd, vienna
  Gustav Berg没有再看照片，只让Klara把口袋里的东西全放在桌上。银怀表、一张电车票和半包烟；表盖打开时，里面那张小照片上的人不是Klara。Helene Huber在门外想说什么，被另一个警察挡住了。拘留室的门关上时，Klara才发现自己一直攥着那张电车票。
  _effects: state_add detained state:world-vienna-1912:378; state_add p21-scandal-web state:world-vienna-1912:379; reputation -0.03; flag +jailed_under_wrong_name; knowledge “表盖里的小照片上的人不是当事人，拘留室的门关上时，当事人还攥着电车票。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-29; flag +detained; flag +scandal_web_active_

- **1934.11** `vienna-parish-aid.sick-call/sick-call.help-in` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Klara和Karl Kraus一起上楼，门没锁，屋里一股酸味。病人裹着毯子坐在床沿咳嗽，炉子只剩灰。Klara去楼下讨了几块煤核，蹲着把火生起来，又用带来的面粉搅了一小锅稀粥。药粉冲开，碗底沉着白渣，Klara先垫了药钱，说以后再说。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (替病人垫上药粉钱); relation acquaintance with person-karl-kraus; flag +parish_sick_visitor_

  警察在第三个人发言时赶到。人群散得很快，Klara跟着别人拐进巷子，鞋底踩过一堆碎玻璃。第二天没有人被抓，但街角多了一个穿便衣的人。
  _effects: state_add political-suspect state:world-vienna-1912:377_

  纸牌桌上连开三把庄。Klara按自己写在烟盒背面的翻倍法，把最后十五克朗押在闲上。庄家翻开一张四，闲家九点；Marie Huber的手在桌下抓住Klara的袖子；Klara把赢回的三十克朗换成现钱，数出十克朗塞进袜筒，剩下的放进内袋，说今晚到此为止。
  _effects: cash 30 (winnings taken off the card table); state_add ml18-sudden-fortune state:world-vienna-1912:424; relation partner with person-marie-huber; reputation 0.02; flag +left_the_table_ahead; mood 0.07999999999999996; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-55; flag +sudden_fortune; reputation 0.02; mood 0.030000000000000027_

  Klara把Karl Kraus的名字写在“联络员”后面，月薪一克朗，职责是每周来两次、把外面的消息带进来。Gustav Berg说这个岗位没有编制，Klara说先按临时用工算；第一个月，Karl Kraus带来的消息比文件还多。
  _effects: position 联络员 @org:r15-club:evt:world-vienna-1912:006694; relation colleague with person-karl-kraus; flag +appointed_ally; knowledge “联络员不在正式编制里，工资走临时用工。”; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  Klara在宵禁后回家，被街角的巡警叫住。区公所的讯问室里没有窗，桌上只有一盏台灯；问了两个小时，签了名字，按了手印，天亮前被放出来，工票被翻得起了毛边。台灯罩上有一只死苍蝇，没人去碰。
  _effects: move place-wien-magistrat; move place-wien-leopoldstadt-tenement; condition; flag +questioned_by_police; role; cash -2 (tram fare and a replacement work-book cover); knowledge “区公所的讯问室没有窗，签名字按手印后可以天亮前放人；工票会被翻旧。”_

  _effects: reputation 0.04; organization_found 接手后的俱乐部 (party); position 临时主席 @org:r15-club:evt:world-vienna-1912:006694_

  巷口洗衣房的老板娘把几张椅子摆开，叫来半条街的租户。Klara把涨租单摊在桌上，逐条念出上面的数字；Friedrich Hofer站在门边，既没走，也没替房东说话。大家决定先集体迟交一个月，签名纸传到Klara手里时，纸上已经有一块油渍。
  _effects: cash -2 (paper, ink and tram fare for the tenants meeting); role; flag +rent_strike; relation acquaintance with person-friedrich-hofer; reputation 0.03_

- **1936.06** `vienna-legacy-worklife.sick-fund/sick-fund.fill-form` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  衙门走廊的地砖是黑白相间的，Klara站着站着就把重心从一只脚换到另一只脚。窗口里的办事员姓什么没人知道，只叫“三号窗”。表递进去时，里面说“等复核”，声音很平。
  _effects: relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17; skill_

- **1936.09** `r1-cul.letter-writing::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/sent-the-long-letter` tags: culture, letters, longing, literacy, distance, vienna, urban, crisis
  Klara写到第三页，墨水用完了，就用铅笔接着写。信里说今年的煤比去年贵，说楼下新开了一家面包房，说到最后才问对方什么时候回来。Grete Huber的回信只有半页，说别等，先把冬天过完。
  _effects: cash -0.5 (写到一半的信); mood -0.030000000000000027; flag +sent_the_long_letter; role; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13; knowledge “the reply asks not to wait until spring”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13_

  俱乐部从十一人变成四十人。Klara仍然坐主席的位置，但每次开会前都要先和Anna Bauer对一遍名单。有人开始叫Klara“主席”，也有人只在需要签字时才来。
  _effects: reputation 0.08; knowledge “俱乐部扩大后，真正的权力不在主席的椅子上，而在会前对名单的修改。”; flag +faction_grew_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:006694_

  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036; reputation 0.08; position 名誉主席 @org:r15-faction:evt:world-vienna-1912:005362; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-94_

  又过了一年，那几张纸仍锁在Klara的抽屉里。街上的问话没有变多，也没有变少；Klara在咖啡馆里数了数还能信任的人，发现名单比去年短，但每一个名字都还站得住。
  _effects: state_parameter p21-scandal-web.exposure 60 -> 57; state_parameter p21-scandal-web.loyalty 39 -> 43; state_parameter p21-scandal-web.danger 52 -> 49; mood 0.010000000000000009_

  Anton Winter没有接话，先从抽屉里撕下一角纸。Klara把名字、住址和手艺一样样说完，工头边写边应，写完把纸压回抽屉，说月底来问。
  _effects: mood -0.029999999999999916; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-20; flag +seeking_work_

- **1937.10** `vienna-women-housework.wash-day/wash-day.quarrel` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  铁桶磕在石沿上，水晃出来打湿了Klara的裙角。Maria Kral 2的声音拔高，说轮到她就是轮到她。Klara咬着嘴没再喊，把盆往自己门前拖，湿石板上一道水痕。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-55; reputation -0.01999999999999999_

- **1937.12** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-demand-repair` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money

  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-28; mood 0.030000000000000027_

  _effects: role; cash -3 (new permits and stamps); condition_

  _effects: reputation -0.020000000000000004; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13; flag +appointment_list_rejected; knowledge “委员会以“没有相关资历”为由划掉了联络员的名字。”_

  _effects: cash -1 (materials for the window repair); relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912; flag +repair_promised; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1939.06** `vienna-housing-move.sublet-room/sublet-daylight-only` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Klara和Mitzi Lechner 3谈妥只出租白天，夜里不留宿，收两克朗。床仍归Klara家，钥匙只在白天给。Mitzi Lechner 3说不碍事，他住的工棚不许带人，白天有个落脚的地方就够。Josef听了点头，说这样少些闲话；到了傍晚，Klara把床铺重新收好，屋里照旧几口人。
  _effects: cash 2 (白天出租床位的钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-36; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-36_

  报纸在头版登了Klara和承包商在一张桌子上的照片，配文只有一句“谁签的字”。质询会开了三个小时，Klara把合同、比价单和验收记录一页页摊开；记者第二天没有再写，但把照片留在了档案里。
  _effects: reputation -0.03; state_add office-under-siege state:world-vienna-1912:530; flag +scandal_weathered; knowledge “照片是真的，合同也是真的；报纸没有再追，但档案里留了底片。”; flag +office_under_siege_

- **1943.01** `initiative:found_process:org:r15-faction:evt:world-vienna-1912:005362`
  权力俱乐部在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang经过数年的招募、会议和互助，把分散的行动整理成公开纲领；这不是预先写好的历史，而是成员自己累积出来的运动。
  _effects: historical process 权力俱乐部的持续运动; historical development:initiative-org:r15-faction:evt:world-vienna-1912:005362-1943 as organizer; historical development:initiative-org:r15-faction:evt:world-vienna-1912:005362-1943 as organizer; knowledge “权力俱乐部的持续运动不是一次会议，而是我们这些年积累出来的持续组织。”; reputation 0.04000000000000001_

- **1943.01** `historical_phase`
  历史进程《权力俱乐部在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang经过数年的招募、会议和互助，把分散的行动整理成公开纲领；这不是预先》从 emerging 进入 mobilizing。
  _effects: knowledge “我亲历或知晓「权力俱乐部在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang经过数年的招”; knowledge “我亲历或知晓「权力俱乐部在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang经过数年的招”_

- **1943.08** `vienna-adult-social.burial-fund/burial-fund.skipped` tags: adulthood, social, work, family
  Klara对Mitzi Lechner 3说这个月实在挤不出来，房租刚涨了半个克朗。Mitzi Lechner 3把本子翻到另一页，说那就先记着，只是下个月得补齐。午休铃响时，Klara的工具箱上还留着本子压出的印子。
  _effects: relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-36_

- **1943.10** `r15-wish.found-faction::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/nobody-came` tags: politics, power, wish, organization, office, status, vienna, urban
  第一次会议只来了三个人，其中一个是房东，来问房租。Klara把章程念完，没有人反对，也没有人加入。办公室的钥匙还在，但那间屋子比街上更安静。
  _effects: mood -0.06000000000000005; reputation -0.01999999999999999; flag +faction_failed_to_form_

- **1943.11** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_stolen_by_stranger` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Klara记得出门时钥匙还在，回来却没了。Otto Lechner说下午看见一个穿灰外套的男人在楼道里转悠，手里晃着一把铜钥匙。两人下楼查看，房门虚掩，抽屉被拉开，攒着买煤的六克朗不见了。Otto Lechner陪着Klara去街角警察岗亭报了案，警察记下灰外套，说会留意。
  _effects: cash -6 (被盗的买煤钱)_

- **1944.01** `office16-budget.sign-or-block::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/signed-for-a-favor` tags: office, budget, permit, administration, power, economy, vienna, urban
  Luise Hofer在签字前把一张剧院包厢的票根留在桌上，说是“给孩子的”。Klara把票根推回去，却在许可证上签了名；排水图仍缺一页，但窗口当天就盖了章。扩建开工后，第一场雨把后院淹了半尺。
  _effects: organization_funds; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001282:child; flag +permit_for_favor; knowledge “许可证少了一页排水图，盖章时没有补。”; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1944.01** `agency:support_process` tags: agency, agency:support_process
  Klara为Sozialdemokratische Arbeiterpartei Österreich出了力：整理名单、传递消息或替同伴作证，名字被记进参与记录。
  _effects: historical development:initiative-org-sdap-1933 as supporter; historical_influence; reputation 0.014999999999999986_

- **1944.02** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-neighbors-together` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Klara把Josef和楼里另两户人家叫到楼道，把那张纸摆在窗台上传看。看门人在楼梯拐角听着，说房东礼拜三来收钱。几家人合计好，到那天一起等在门厅，先把克朗递过去，再把修补的事一件件摆出来。Klara把纸折进围裙口袋，觉得人凑齐了话也硬气些。
  _effects: mood 0.040000000000000036; flag +tenant_collective_

- **1944.04** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/corner-doctor` tags: health, medical, family, money
  施特劳斯医生用酒精棉擦手，把药粉倒进小纸袋，折口时指甲掐出印子。他说夜里别再喝醋水，那东西烧胃。Mitzi Berg把炉火拨旺，在灶上坐了一壶水，答应第二天替Klara去铺子里告假。
  _effects: cash -4 (医生出诊费和药费); health 0.05 (药粉和糖浆缓解症状); relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000940:child_

- **1944.07** `statistical_infection`
  Klara在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1944.09** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Klara的肺炎没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1944.12** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Klara的肺炎没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1945.01** `statistical_recovery`
  Klara熬过了这场肺炎，能坐起来喝一点汤，但身体还很虚。

- **1945.01** `vienna-life-1945-liberation/postwar-illness` tags: war, liberation, 1945
  Klara在战后第二个月病倒，医院没有足够的药，医生只给了退烧粉。邻居每天送来一碗汤，放在门口就走。
  _effects: cash -4 (medicine); condition_

- **1945.01** `r15-wish.faction-price::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/absorbed` tags: politics, power, wish, organization, intrigue, office, vienna, urban
  一个更大的组织提出合并。Klara保住了“副主席”的头衔，但新的章程把召集权交给了别人。签字那天，Klara把自己的印章留在旧办公室的抽屉里。
  _effects: reputation 0.04999999999999999; position_end 发起人 @org:r15-faction:evt:world-vienna-1912:005362; flag +absorbed_into_larger_org_

  Klara听Friedrich Stein 2报出押金数目，把伸进内袋的手又抽了回来。问清免费阅览处的位置后，Klara穿过两条街，在 Volksbibliothek 的阅览室门口登记了姓名，坐在长桌边读了一下午；一个赫勒没花，只是不能把书带回家，天黑前就得还回去。
  _effects: mood 0.030000000000000027; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-91; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-91_

- **1945.06** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/subject_yields_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Klara看Katharina Winter盆里那几件小衬衫，把自家床单卷起来抱回屋，说等下午再晾。Katharina Winter愣了一下，闷头把衣服挂好，收工回来时在Klara门口放了一小包用旧报纸裹的洗衣碱。Klara打开报纸，碱面里还掺着几粒粗盐。
  _effects: food_

- **1945.10** `vienna-legacy-worklife.union-office/union-office.enroll-first` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  来问入会的是个瘦高的年轻人，帽檐捏在手里，说自己在第十六区的织布厂做了半年。Klara把名册翻到空白页，蘸水笔尖在“住址”那栏停了一下，等他报出街名。老成员在角落里把账本合上，声音很轻。
  _effects: cash 1 (新会员的入会费先记在桌上); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-109; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-109_

- **1945.11** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/drops-and-endure` tags: health, medical, family, money
  夜里疼醒时，Klara摸黑往棉球上又滴药水，手抖得洒在枕头上。Ferdinand Huber第二天一早来敲门，看见肿脸就说别硬撑了，可Klara只是摆摆手，说再熬一天也许就好了。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (牙痛药水); condition_

  _effects: state_add hs18-street-witness state:world-vienna-1912:677; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001282:child; flag +hid_neighbor_coal; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1946.11** `ml18-lottery.same-numbers::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-number-wins-without-them` tags: lottery, luck, money, family, regret, windfall, vienna, urban
  Klara用了十几年同一组号码——父亲当年送奶路线的门牌号。这个月房租涨了，Klara少买了一张；开奖那天，那组号码中了头奖。Marie Huber把报纸摊在桌上，Klara看了很久，最后去厨房把水壶坐上，水开了才发现自己没有拿杯子。
  _effects: cash -2 (the drink and the extra trip to the newsagent); state_add ml18-reversal state:world-vienna-1912:676; relation partner with person-marie-huber; flag +missed_the_numbers; knowledge “父亲送奶路线的门牌号中了头奖，而这一期我没有买。”; mood -0.06000000000000005; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-12; flag +fortune_reversed; mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1946.11** `state_reaction:ml18-reversal/a-second-chance-arrives` tags: ml18-reversal, money, luck, reversal, risk, debt, reputation, loss
  一封没有署名的信里夹着一张彩票，号码是Klara上个月随口说过的。信纸是廉价的学生用纸，字迹却写得很慢。Klara把彩票翻过来，背面只写着一句：这次别再押上房租。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-reversal.swing -20 -> -10; state_parameter ml18-reversal.strain 30 -> 25; mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +reversal_second_chance_

  Hermann Hofer 2把一篮鸡蛋放在Klara家门口，说只要一个不用打卡的位子。Klara把鸡蛋拎回对方手里，说编制表不是自家的抽屉。两个人站在楼梯间，谁也没有再提这件事；第二天见面，还是点了头。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-122; reputation 0.030000000000000027; mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-122_

  半年里没有新的坏消息。Klara把零钱分成三份：房租、还账、留一点在抽屉里。旧债主没有上门，咖啡馆里的人也不再每见一次就提那件赔本的事；日子没有变好，但至少不再往下滑。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-reversal.swing -10 -> -4; state_parameter ml18-reversal.strain 25 -> 21; cash 1 (small economies and an odd job); mood 0.010000000000000009_

  _effects: state_parameter hs18-street-witness.certainty 90 -> 92_

  Klara在第三天回去上工，Gustav Bauer在电话里沉默了很久，说规矩是给活人守的，不是给死人守的。权力俱乐部把考勤卡递过来，什么也没说。那天晚上Klara在厨房点了一根没人祝福的蜡烛。

  Klara又把那天的顺序在脑子里走了一遍：先是门响，再是靴子，最后才是那声喊。细节还在，只是每讲一次，旁边就多出一点别人替你补上的颜色。
  _effects: state_parameter hs18-street-witness.certainty 84 -> 86_

- **1949.01** `state_tick:office-under-siege/pressure-year/held-on` tags: office-under-siege, office, scandal, pressure, politics, legal, risk, state
  质询会开了两个小时，Klara把每一笔签字都念了一遍。没有人被说服，但也没有人再追问；散会时，支持Klara的人比进门时多了两个。
  _effects: state_parameter office-under-siege.support 60 -> 65; state_parameter office-under-siege.exposure 30 -> 26; state_parameter office-under-siege.months 0 -> 1; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1949.01** `state_tick:ad20-private-bargain/the-favor-ages/exposed` tags: ad20-private-bargain, private, bargain, favor, debt, trust, leverage, exit
  第三个人知道了这件事。不是全部，但足够让Klara在解释时多说了一句，又在下一句里少说了一句。对方听完，只说“原来还有这一层”。
  _effects: state_parameter ad20-private-bargain.leverage 48 -> 60; state_parameter ad20-private-bargain.trust 41 -> 33; state_parameter ad20-private-bargain.exit 25 -> 19; reputation -0.020000000000000018; flag +private_bargain_exposed_

- **1949.01** `agency:support_process` tags: agency, agency:support_process
  Klara想帮忙，但组织者不信任新来的人，只让其在外围等消息。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; reputation -0.010000000000000009_

- **1949.04** `vienna-parish-aid.funeral-society/funeral-society.quit` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Klara说互助会先退了，这笔钱要留给煤和面包。Katharina Winter没争辩，在蓝皮本上把名字划了一道，问清楚是不是全家都退，然后把划掉的那行折进本子夹缝。她说以后想再进，要重新排三个月。门关上，Klara听见铅笔在楼梯上轻轻敲了两下栏杆。
  _effects: cash 0.4 (省下本周会费留作煤钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7; flag +funeral_society_left_

- **1949.07** `ad20-favor-called-in::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/do-the-favor` tags: favor, obligation, everyday, trust, debt, risk, vienna, urban
  Emil说只需要Klara在明天下午去一趟车站，把一个箱子交给穿灰外套的人。Klara问箱子里是什么；Emil说“你不需要知道”。Klara还是去了，因为当年Emil替Klara垫过一笔医药费。
  _effects: state_add ad20-private-bargain state:world-vienna-1912:758; cash -1 (the trip to the station); relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912; flag +favor_done; mood -0.010000000000000009; knowledge “箱子里是什么，邻居没有说。”; flag -private_bargain_active; flag +private_bargain_active_

- **1949.07** `state_reaction:ad20-private-bargain/a-debt-returns` tags: ad20-private-bargain, private, bargain, favor, debt, trust, leverage, exit
  新的债没有走正式渠道，而是被带进了同一间厨房。Klara在桌边听完数目，发现对方连还款日期都替自己算好了。
  _effects: state_parameter ad20-private-bargain.debt 55 -> 61; state_parameter ad20-private-bargain.leverage 50 -> 54; mood -0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:012965_

- **1949.09** `vienna-historical-social-movement.public-meeting/speak-with-rent-numbers` tags: history, historical_process, social_movement, organization, politics
  Klara没有喊口号，只问在场谁家的煤还够烧三天。十几只手举起来，连Maria Lechner也举了。书记说这些数字比演说有用，散会时几个人围上来问自己那条街怎么算。
  _effects: reputation 0.06000000000000005; skill; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000659:child_

- **1950.01** `r15-wish.faction-price::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/grew` tags: politics, power, wish, organization, intrigue, office, vienna, urban
  俱乐部从十一人变成四十人。Klara仍然坐主席的位置，但每次开会前都要先和Marie Huber对一遍名单。有人开始叫Klara“主席”，也有人只在需要签字时才来。
  _effects: reputation 0.08000000000000002; knowledge “俱乐部扩大后，真正的权力不在主席的椅子上，而在会前对名单的修改。”_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:012459_

- **1950.01** `office16-rival.removal-motion::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/motion-fails` tags: office, rivalry, politics, removal, power, intrigue, vienna, urban
  会议室里有人念了一份动议，说Klara把预算当成人情。Franz Weiss把签名页传了一圈，最后停在自己手里：支持票比反对票少三张。Klara没有发言，只把下一季度的支出表放在桌上，表上每一笔都有收据。
  _effects: reputation 0.040000000000000036; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-28; flag +survived_removal_motion; knowledge “动议只拿到少数支持，三名原本签名的委员在投票前撤回了。”; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1950.01** `state_tick:hs18-street-witness/memory-drift/blurs` tags: hs18-street-witness, witness, memory, history, testimony, rumor, risk, street
  Klara忽然想不起那个人先迈的是左脚还是右脚。邻居说自己记得清清楚楚，还补了一个细节；Klara听着，竟分不清那是自己看见的，还是后来听来的。
  _effects: state_parameter hs18-street-witness.certainty 86 -> 78_

- **1950.01** `state_tick:ad20-private-bargain/the-favor-ages/kept` tags: ad20-private-bargain, private, bargain, favor, debt, trust, leverage, exit
  那笔私下的安排又过了一年。没有人提起，但Klara在街上遇见对方时，两个人都多停了一拍。Klara知道，沉默本身也是利息。
  _effects: state_parameter ad20-private-bargain.trust 55 -> 59; state_parameter ad20-private-bargain.debt 61 -> 59; state_parameter ad20-private-bargain.exit 20 -> 22; mood 0.010000000000000009_

  窗外有人来问入会，接待的人一时不知道怎么开口，Klara替他说“进来坐”。老成员把账本拿回去，用一块旧布包好，放进抽屉最里面。抽屉关上的声音比平时响。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-86; flag +union_debt_written_off; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-99_

- **1950.11** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.window-argument/window-argument.peek` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, neighbor, overheard
  Klara下楼倒水，在楼梯口看见院子里站着一对夫妻，男人的围裙上还沾着面粉，女的攥着一张工钱条。她没插话，提着壶又上楼，关门时听见那女人说“一星期八克朗怎么够”。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; skill_

- **1950.11** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/compromise_split_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Klara把洗好的床单搭上院子里的公用晾衣绳，占了整条。Anton Bauer 2抱着一盆湿衣服下来，说自家孩子明天要穿干净衬衫上学。两人在绳子两头扯了一阵，最后Klara把床单对折，腾出一半，Anton Bauer 2用木夹子把衬衫一件件排上去。谁也没道歉，但第二天见面都点了头。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-66; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-66_

  代表团没有带来任命书，只带来一套戏服：市庆游行需要一个“国王”，一天两场，管一顿饭。Klara穿上戏服时，围观的人鼓起掌来。掌声是真的，王冠是假的，晚饭也是真的。
  _effects: state_add stage-sovereign state:world-vienna-1912:801; cash 2 (游行演出的酬劳); flag -stage_king; flag +stage_sovereign; reputation 0.040000000000000036_

- **1951.08** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_burns_curtain` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Klara在公用灶台上热猪油，油星子溅到旁边晾着的床单上，火苗顺着床单爬上木隔板。Anna Bauer 2拎起水桶泼过去，火灭了，但床单烧掉半幅，隔板熏黑一片。房东扣了那户人家两克朗押金，那家的女人在楼道里哭了一下午。
  _effects: cash -2 (赔给房东的隔板熏黑损失); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10_

  教堂后院的铁锅冒着白汽，排队的人把各种罐子捧在胸前。Katharina Lechner穿着灰围裙一勺一勺地分，轮到Klara时锅底已经露出来，勺子刮出响声，还是刮了半勺倒进罐里。
  _effects: food; health 0.03 (热汤下肚驱了寒气)_

- **1952.10** `vienna-church-community.tenement-prayer/rent-prayer-help` tags: faith, social, family, home
  楼梯间只点一根蜡烛。Ferdinand Hofer凑过来低声说，楼下老寡妇的喘病又重了，教区明早才能派修女来。Klara把汤碗接过去，碗底还温着。
  _effects: food_

- **1952.10** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/hospital-night` tags: health, medical, family, money
  护士把冰袋按在Klara额上，水顺着鬓角流进衣领。Franz Huber 4坐在床脚，数着窗外渐渐亮起的铺面门板，说回去给炉子添块煤。隔壁床的老头整夜在喊一个女人的名字。
  _effects: cash -2 (医院急诊押金); health 0.08 (急诊观察和冰敷退烧); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-138; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-138_

  Klara和Mitzi Berg在咖啡馆后间成立了一个小小的政治俱乐部。桌子是借的，章程是手写的，但签名、印章和一间可以锁门的办公室是真的。Katharina Lechner没有到场，只送来一句“先看看你们能撑多久”。

  四十七个弗朗茨·穆勒里，只有Klara拿得出干净的房租簿。公证人Franz Weiss把遗嘱念完，遗产是一辆灵车、两匹老马和十四克朗的欠账。Klara没有钱下葬，先把灵车租给搬家的人；夜里跑环城大道，白天替人运钢琴。Maria Berg起初等着看笑话，后来开始给他介绍生意。
  _effects: cash 5 (用灵车替人搬家的收入); role; state_add bc18-ridiculous-fame state:world-vienna-1912:860; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-140; flag +hearse_became_a_moving_van; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-140; flag +ridiculously_famous; reputation 0.020000000000000018; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  Franz Weiss派了一名检查员去看房，回来说厕所漏水、电线裸露，不适合居住。Mitzi Berg 2说可以降租，Franz Weiss说降租也要先修。Klara问修房期间住哪里，Franz Weiss说可以申请临时安置；安置名单排到明年，Franz Huber 3说先住我家阁楼。

- **1954.01** `state_tick:bc18-ridiculous-fame/ridiculous-fame-year/the-audience-gets-bored` tags: bc18-ridiculous-fame, fame, reputation, mistaken_identity, promotion, media, absurd, public
  观众开始厌倦同一个故事。园艺会请了更年轻的发明家，女子学校把Klara的名字从海报上撤下；Klara在咖啡馆听见有人说“那个人还在讲火柴”。同一个月，一张报纸把旧照片配上新标题，问Klara是否已经“过气”。纠正的冲动又回来，但没有人再提问。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.legitimacy 20 -> 12; state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.boredom 6 -> 18; state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.invitations 1 -> 0; reputation -0.020000000000000018; mood -0.030000000000000027_

  Klara把钥匙丢在了回家路上的有轨电车轨道旁，天黑也没找着。房东太太说换锁芯要一克朗五十赫勒，从下月房租里扣。Franz Huber 3借给Klara一把旧挂锁，但锁鼻和门扣对不上，只能用铁丝拧着凑合。那夜Klara睡不踏实，总觉得有人在拧门。
  _effects: cash -1.5 (换锁芯费用); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-117_

- **1954.10** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/sweat-it-out` tags: health, medical, family, money
  醋水的酸味混着汗气，屋子小得转不开身。Friedrich Stein 2把布拧到不滴水，敷上去时Klara打了个激灵。窗外送奶车吱呀碾过石板，她才想起自己家的炉子还灭着。
  _effects: condition; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-91_

  _effects: cash -2 (补办证明的手续与跑腿钱); relation kin with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912_

- **1955.02** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-demand-repair` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Klara没有立刻答话，先去敲Gustav Hofer 2的门。两人一起数落墙上的水渍和门框里灌进来的风，楼梯间的窗户修好了，自家墙皮还在剥落。Gustav Hofer 2提议一起把话带给看门人：先修好漏水的墙角，涨租的事再谈。Klara把纸重新折好，请他把话带到门外。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-129; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-129_

  Maria Lechner看完租约，没有加价，也没有提补充条款。Klara把房租数好，放在桌上；Maria Lechner数了一遍，又从里面拿出一枚硬币，说“上次多收了”。Klara接过硬币，觉得这一年总算有一件事没有变。
  _effects: cash -1 (the rent was paid on time); relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000659:child; flag +rent_renewed; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1912.02** `vienna-life-toddler-first-words/first-word-nonsense` tags: infancy, family, first, comic
  Mitzi说了一长串谁也听不懂的话，最后用力点头，像是在宣布一件已经决定的事。Alois Lechner认真听完，也点了点头；孩子满意地爬走了，留下大人们互相看着，忍不住笑。
  _effects: mood 0.04; skill_

- **1913.05** `vienna-infant-household.croup/steam-kettle` tags: infancy, health, family, home
  Rosa Lechner把铜壶烧开，提起壶嘴对着床头旧毛巾罩出的布篷，让蒸汽灌进去。Mitzi的犬吠样咳嗽慢慢松下来，天亮时睡着。Rosa Lechner用掉一整天份的煤，手背被蒸汽烫红一片。
  _effects: cash -1 (多烧了一天的煤)_

- **1915.01** `vienna-life-child-family-legend/stolen-inheritance` tags: childhood, family, secret, story
  Alois Lechner说，家里本来有一件东西，后来被“更会说话的人”拿走了。Mitzi问是什么，Alois Lechner只说是一块表，表盖里刻着姓。孩子记住了“表盖里刻着姓”，却没有记住那个姓。
  _effects: knowledge “家里有一块表盖里刻着姓的表，被一个更会说话的人拿走了。”; flag +family_legend_watch_

- **1916.01** `vienna-life-child-first-friend/imaginary-friend` tags: childhood, friendship, family, comic
  院子里没有别的孩子，Mitzi给墙上的裂纹起了一个名字，每天吃饭时给它留一个位置。家里的人没有纠正，只在摆碗时多放一只空碗；Rosa从门口经过，问Mitzi在等谁。
  _effects: skill; flag +imaginary_friend; relation friend with person-phase15-Rosa-world-vienna-1912_

- **1916.02** `p9-childhood-household.supper-bowl/caught-adding-ladle` tags: childhood, family, household, home, siblings, illness, neighbors
  锅盖还冒着气，Mitzi听见Rosa Lechner去灶口添煤，勺子立刻伸进锅里，刮起粘在锅边的半个土豆。Rosa Lechner回来时没坐下，只站在桌边看。Mitzi把勺子放回碗里，膝盖并紧，手心在桌沿上蹭出一层汗。
  _effects: mood -0.04; relation kin with person-rosa-lechner_

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.mind-sibling/sibling-carried-afternoon` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, sibling, care
  Anna Berg哭得打嗝，手指一直朝井台方向指。Mitzi蹲下把Anna Berg驮上背，沿着墙根走，数砖缝给Anna Berg听：一、二、三……数到第四圈，背上的呼吸才匀了。天黑时Mitzi的胳膊麻得抬不过肩，Rosa Lechner回来接过孩子，Mitzi扶着门框甩了好一会儿手。
  _effects: skill; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000604:child; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-22_

  Gustav Berg披着外套去街角敲门，老医生过了半个钟头才提着皮包上楼。医生让Mitzi坐起来，把听诊器贴在背上，听完从纸包里抖出三撮药粉。Rosa Lechner数出两克朗放在桌角，医生用铅笔头写：稀粥、温水、不要面包皮。
  _effects: cash -2 (出诊费和药粉); health 0.05999999999999999 (服药后热退了些); relation acquaintance with person-gustav-berg_

  _effects: relation friend with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; skill; flag +school_friend_

- **1919.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-three-heller` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  床单浸了碱水，沉得拽不动。Mitzi站在木凳上，把湿布一点点递过绳，水顺着肘弯滴进袖口。Maria Huber在绳头数夹子，最后一条挂好，从围裙口袋里摸出三枚赫勒，按进Mitzi掌心。晚上洗手时，虎口那道口子被水一蜇，Mitzi才看见。
  _effects: cash 0.03 (帮邻居晾床单挣的三赫勒); health -0.03 (碱水泡手，虎口裂开); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4_

- **1919.10** `vienna-childhood-street.washhouse-fever/fever-scarlet` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  巷子里一家接一家地病倒。Mitzi烧到说胡话，Alois Lechner把阁楼小屋腾出来，在门把手上系了块红布。医生隔着门看了Mitzi的喉咙，留下药粉，收下一克朗二十赫勒。Alois Lechner把家里留着买煤的钱数了两遍，刚好够。
  _effects: cash -1.2 (付医生诊费（其中1.2克朗记入债务）); condition_

- **1920.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-sold-out-potatoes` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  “卖完了。”三个字从窗口里传出来，Mitzi后面的队伍立刻散了一半。Mitzi没走，趴在窗台上看伙计扫面屑，直到人家挥手赶人。旁边摊子的土豆带着泥，Mitzi用围裙兜住，一枚一枚数铜板，回家报账时连找零都说清了。
  _effects: food; mood -0.030000000000000006; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-20_

- **1920.11** `vienna-childhood-friends.courtyard-race/won-the-race-lost-a-button` tags: childhood, social, school, street
  Mitzi跑到第二圈时鞋带松了，索性踩着鞋后跟跑。最后一圈，Mitzi从床单底下钻过去，先拍到墙。Helene Winter喘着气，把两颗弹珠拍进Mitzi手心。Mitzi低头才发现外套少了一颗扣子；窗口的Helene Winter扔下一根别针，说先别着。
  _effects: mood 0.05; relation acquaintance with person-helene-winter_

- **1921.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.fetch-coal/coal-refused-nuggets` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, coal, errand
  Mitzi空手站在窗口，听掌柜说“叫你Alois Lechner来”，耳朵一直红到脖子。后墙的渣堆冻得硬，Mitzi用鞋尖刨开表层，把煤核一颗颗捡进围裙。回家路上碰到同巷的孩子，Mitzi把纸袋往身后藏，进门才交给Alois Lechner。
  _effects: mood -0.04; food; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10_

- **1921.12** `vienna-childhood-street.market-errand/errand-ok` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  市场里的菜摊一字排开，Mitzi把十五克鲁泽排在掌心，先买了土豆，又去盐摊上要了一小包。摊主用旧报纸把盐裹好，Mitzi把它塞进围裙内袋，土豆兜在身前。回家时Rosa Lechner接过铜板一枚枚数，土豆一颗没少。
  _effects: food; cash -0.15 (市场花销); relation kin with person-rosa-lechner_

- **1922.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-three-heller` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  “拽平，别留褶。”Ferdinand Winter在绳子那头喊。Mitzi踮着脚把床单拉直，碱水把指缝泡得发白。收工时天已经黑了，Ferdinand Winter数了三赫勒，又在围裙上擦干手才递过来。Mitzi把钱塞进袜筒，回家路上一直攥着，虎口的裂口沾了汗，火辣辣地疼。
  _effects: cash 0.03 (帮邻居晾床单挣的三赫勒); relation acquaintance with person-ferdinand-winter; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-31_

- **1923.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.fetch-coal/coal-refused-nuggets` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, coal, errand
  Mitzi空手站在窗口，听掌柜说“叫你Rosa Lechner来”，耳朵一直红到脖子。后墙的渣堆冻得硬，Mitzi用鞋尖刨开表层，把煤核一颗颗捡进围裙。回家路上碰到同巷的孩子，Mitzi把纸袋往身后藏，进门才交给Rosa Lechner。
  _effects: mood -0.04; food; skill_

- **1924.01** `state_tick:wish-knowledge/knowledge-pressure/press-on` tags: wish-knowledge, wish, wish:knowledge, knowledge, education, curiosity, state, state_tick
  Mitzi又把那件没实现的事翻出来想了一遍；这一次没有告诉任何人。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000004; flag +wish:knowledge:pursuing_

- **1924.01** `task-vienna-youth-apprenticeship-search/no-vacancy` tags: task, task:work, work, adolescence, apprenticeship
  Mitzi问了三家印刷厂，得到的回答都是“今年不收学徒”。最后一家让他把名字写在纸角上，说有空缺再通知，但谁都知道那张纸会被压在账本下面。
  _effects: mood -0.05; knowledge “今年印刷厂大多不收新学徒。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-16_

- **1924.12** `r3-teen.parent-remarriage::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/conflict` tags: family, remarriage, stepfamily, grief, household, reform, medical, schooling
  Alois Lechner把Mitzi生父的旧大衣拿去当铺，说家里不缺这件碍事的衣服。Mitzi追到巷口，把当票抢回来，撕成两半。Rosa Lechner站在两人中间，最后谁也没有帮。
  _effects: mood -0.1; condition; flag +conflict_over_dead_fathers_coat; relation rival with person-alois-lechner; relation rival with person-rosa-lechner_

- **1925.01** `r13-edu.school-transfer::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/transfer-approved` tags: education, school, transfer, bureaucracy, family, reform, medical, schooling
  Mitzi的转学材料缺一张居住证明，Rosa Moser让Mitzi去区公所补。Mitzi跑了三趟，第三趟正赶上下雨，Rosa Moser看见Mitzi把湿透的纸一张张摊在柜台上，没再挑毛病，盖了章。Mitzi赶在开学前一天把名字写进新学校。那间教室在二楼，Mitzi第一天就找到了靠窗的座位，把旧课本摆好。
  _effects: flag +school_transfer_approved_

- **1925.04** `vienna-adolescence.workshop-apprentice/apprenticeship-accepted` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne先看Mitzi的手，又让Mitzi推两下刨子试试。Mitzi用力过猛，刨刀啃进木头，Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne皱眉，让再来一遍。第二遍木花薄而匀。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne说“留下”，当面点了三克朗给Rosa Lechner，算头一周饭钱，又指了指墙上的钟，说迟到一刻钟扣半天。
  _effects: cash 3 ({subject}学徒首周的膳食预付款); education apprenticeship; reputation 0.1; relation kin with person-rosa-lechner_

- **1925.05** `yf18-body.danube-cabin::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/stay-behind-the-curtain` tags: body, desire, river, swimming, shame, first_love, reform, medical
  Mitzi把浴巾裹到下巴，说自己怕水。Klara在门外笑，说去年还见Mitzi从木塔上跳下去；Mitzi没有解释，只隔着板缝看Elisabeth Bauer把水撩到肩上。回家时，Mitzi的泳衣还是干的，衣角被手攥得起了皱。
  _effects: state_add yf18-first-awakening state:world-vienna-1912:98; flag +yf18_stayed_dry; mood -0.01999999999999999; flag +yf18_first_awakening_active_

- **1925.07** `yf18-friend.two-tickets::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/give-them-away` tags: first_love, friendship, betrayal, dance, tickets, reform, medical, schooling
  Mitzi把两张票送给门口卖花的小女孩，自己在普拉特大街上看橱窗。橱窗里摆着一双和Ferdinand Huber一样的白手套；Mitzi站到商店关门，回家时说票弄丢了，谁也没有再问。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 40 -> 32; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.secret true -> true; flag +yf18_gave_tickets_away; mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-51_

- **1925.07** `state_reaction:wish-knowledge/knowledge-humiliation` tags: wish-knowledge, wish, wish:knowledge, knowledge, education, curiosity, state, state_reaction
  Mitzi把那句轻视记了很久。它没有变成一个决定，但也没有消失。
  _effects: mood -0.09999999999999998_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:002627_

- **1925.11** `r15-education.entry-offer::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/offer-declined-black-humour` tags: education, scholarship, opportunity, reform, medical, schooling, home, family
  Mitzi把入学通知折成纸船放进河里；家里少了一张嘴吃饭，也少了一个读书人。
  _effects: cash 10 (sold the textbooks instead); education none_

- **1926.01** `state_tick:yf18-first-awakening/half-year-of-glances/answered-glance` tags: yf18-first-awakening, love, youth, first_love, desire, school, awakening, state
  Mitzi在教堂门口或学校走廊里又遇见Elisabeth Bauer。这一次对方先点了头，Mitzi准备好的整段话全忘了，只把手里的书换到另一只手上；回家后Mitzi才发现，自己把书拿倒了。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 32 -> 37; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 0 -> 8; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.reciprocated false -> true; relation friend with person-elisabeth-bauer; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1926.01** `r14-edu.tutoring-sibling::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/sibling-resented-drilling` tags: education, tutored_sibling, family, tutoring, reform, medical, schooling, home
  Mitzi Berg把铅笔一摔，说再也不想看见豆子。Mitzi把豆子一颗颗收进碗里，说随你。那天晚上，Mitzi Berg自己把错题改了一遍，改完把本子推到Mitzi面前，不说话。Mitzi看了一遍，在页角画了一个很小的勾。
  _effects: flag +sibling_resented_tutoring; relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000940:child_

- **1926.05** `statistical_infection`
  Mitzi在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1926.06** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Mitzi的肺炎没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1926.07** `r3-teen.police-caution::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::reform-parent-notice/cautioned` tags: police, caution, record, family, work, reform, medical, schooling
  Anton Winter把Mitzi的名字写进一本硬皮簿子，问一句写一句，最后让他按手印。Alois Lechner赶来时，Anton Winter说带回去好好管教，这次不立案。走出门口，Alois Lechner的手一直攥着Mitzi的袖子。
  _effects: reputation -0.04; flag +police_caution_recorded; relation guardian with person-alois-lechner; knowledge “the police opened a caution file in the subject’s name”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18_

- **1926.08** `statistical_recovery`
  Mitzi从肺炎中慢慢恢复，能下床喝水，但身体还虚弱。

- **1926.10** `yf18-runaway.cousin-letter::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/show-the-letter-to-parent` tags: runaway, letter, cousin, work, plan, reform, medical, schooling
  Mitzi把信放在晚饭桌上。Alois Lechner读完，问格拉茨远不远，又说家里的房租下个月要涨；那晚谁也没有说行或不行，信被压在糖罐下面，第二天边角沾了咖啡。
  _effects: state_add yf18-runaway-plan state:world-vienna-1912:125; relation kin with person-alois-lechner; flag +yf18_family_knows_letter; mood -0.010000000000000009; flag +yf18_runaway_plan_active_

- **1926.11** `p9-childhood-adolescence.courtyard-gang/gang-bring-younger-neighbor` tags: adolescence, friendship, identity, work, school, secret, church
  Mitzi拉上隔壁更小的Emil Kraus一起去桥下，回程时那孩子的母亲堵在院门口，说Mitzi带坏了她家孩子，声音大得半条街都听见。
  _effects: reputation -0.04; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000168:child; mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1926.12** `state_reaction:yf18-runaway-plan/a-family-question` tags: yf18-runaway-plan, escape, youth, plan, home, travel, risk, state
  饭桌上又为房租吵起来。Mitzi把面包掰成很小的块，听见父亲说“这个家养不起闲人”；Mitzi没有回嘴，只在心里把格拉茨的地址又背了一遍。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.suspicion 0 -> 8; state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.resolve 35 -> 33_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:002981_

- **1927.01** `yf18-dare.bridge-rail::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/climb-back-down` tags: dare, bridge, river, friendship, risk, reform, medical, schooling
  Mitzi的手已经搭上栏杆，听见桥下有驳船拉笛，忽然把手收回来。Elisabeth Bauer骂了一句，Rosa Kraus却把围巾解下来递给Mitzi；三个人在桥头站了一会儿，谁也没有再提这件事。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 37 -> 40; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 8 -> 3; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000172:child; relation friend with person-elisabeth-bauer; mood -0.010000000000000009_

  Mitzi把去车站的路又走了一遍，记住了哪一段有煤气灯、哪一段住着巡夜的警察。回家后，Mitzi在旧日历背面画了一条线，标出换乘的站名；那条线短得可笑，却比课本上的任何地图都清楚。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.preparation 20 -> 32; state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.suspicion 8 -> 10; mood 0.010000000000000009_

  Mitzi在教堂门口或学校走廊里又遇见Elisabeth Bauer。这一次对方先点了头，Mitzi准备好的整段话全忘了，只把手里的书换到另一只手上；回家后Mitzi才发现，自己把书拿倒了。

  Helene Huber说自己眼睛不行了，带不了徒弟，但从帆布袋里抽出一本旧字样本，让Mitzi拿回去临摹。书皮用麻线缝过三次，扉页上有一行褪色的签名。少年双手接过来，在咖啡馆门口鞠了个躬。
  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-29; flag +typesetting_specimen_book; cash -1 (咖啡馆里请老师傅喝一杯); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-29_

- **1927.01** `r3-teen.foster-family::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/rejected` tags: foster_care, family, placement, belonging, institution, reform, medical, schooling
  寄养父亲数了数橱柜里的面包，说多一张嘴就是多一份配给。Friedrich Hofer来做回访时，他已经把Mitzi的行李放在门口。Mitzi抱着包袱站在雪里，听见屋里Alois Lechner在哭，门没有开。

- **1927.02** `vienna-adolescence.stolen-roll/caught-and-returned` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  面包房后门的筐里堆着隔夜面包。Mitzi拿起半个黑面包，刚要往怀里塞，Maria Stein从晾衣绳后面绕出来，手一伸，掌心朝上。Mitzi把面包放回去，Maria Stein用指节在Mitzi手背上敲了两下，声音不大，巷口的人都听见了。
  _effects: reputation -0.06; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-6; mood -0.10000000000000003; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-6_

  Franz Huber先说试三个月，不给工钱，只管一顿午饭。家里同意了，因为多一张嘴比多一双手更难。Mitzi每天最早到，最晚走，三个月后师傅才把契约拿出来。

  _effects: cash -1 (誊写申请与买信封的花费); education secondary; mood 0.09999999999999998; flag +scholarship_holder_

- **1932.01** `r14-edu.sibling-exam::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/sibling-failed` tags: education, tutored_sibling, family, exam, reform, medical, schooling, home
  Mitzi Berg差三分。回家路上，Mitzi Berg一句话也没说，进了门就把自己关在屋里。Mitzi把饭菜放在门口，敲了两下，没有回应。半夜，Mitzi听见屋里有人在翻书，翻到天亮。第二天清早，Mitzi Berg出来洗脸，眼睛肿着，问Mitzi能不能再教一年。补考费一克朗。
  _effects: cash -1 (弟弟妹妹的准考证（其中1克朗记入债务）); flag +sibling_failed_exam; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000940:child_

  Mitzi在换纸时左手被压板夹住。工友关掉机器，工头拿来急救箱，把伤手包起来送去医院；厂里先垫了十克朗医药费，事故报告写的是“操作不慎”。Mitzi的左手从此握不紧拳，指尖总是凉的，天阴时骨缝里发疼。

  Friedrich Berg在楼道里拦住Mitzi，问家里那张盖红章的纸是不是出了事。Mitzi说只是旧房租收据，回家却把纸从柜底翻出来，和Mitzi Berg一起塞进米缸夹层。两人把米重新铺平，Mitzi Berg的手一直在抖；Mitzi说这事到此为止，谁也别再提。

  Mitzi把新店的招牌挂上去时，街对面已经站了几个人。第一位客人买了一包针，第二位问能不能赊账，第三位什么也没买，只说“看看”。到傍晚，Mitzi发现自己记住了每一张脸，却忘了算今天的账。
  _effects: state_add ad20-public-attention state:world-vienna-1912:341; cash 3 (the first day’s takings); reputation 0.03; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-88; flag +shop_opened; mood 0.040000000000000036; flag +public_attention_active_

- **1933.08** `major-education-university-registration/rejected-documents` tags: education, university, migration, major
  窗口在十二点关上。Friedrich Hofer把材料推回来：拉丁文课时不够，中学毕业证上的印章也模糊。Mitzi问能不能先注册再补，Friedrich Hofer说下一位。走出注册处时，雨把税单打湿了一角；四十五克朗还在口袋里，但名册上没有编号。
  _effects: cash -6 (certified copies and stamps); reputation -0.04000000000000001; flag +matriculation_rejected; role; mood -0.07999999999999996; relation acquaintance with person-friedrich-hofer_

- **1933.10** `p9-work-printshop.printed-secret/ask-for-name` tags: work, printshop, printing, compositor, craft, workshop, secret, handbill
  Mitzi把笔推过去，要来人在单子上写下姓名和地址。Friedrich Berg收起纸，把帽子压低，从后门走了，那晚机器一次也没开。第二天Maria Kral 2问起，Mitzi只说人没来。
  _effects: mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-55_

- **1933.10** `vienna-illness-household.child-fever/home-remedy` tags: health, family, home
  邻居过来看了一眼，教Mitzi用湿布敷额。Helene Lechner天亮时安静睡了，Mitzi却耽误半日活计；一克朗五十赫勒的工钱没了，床边的醋味却留了一整天。
  _effects: health 0.05 (家庭护理后烧略退); cash -1.5 (缺工半日被扣工钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-88_

- **1934.01** `state_tick:ad20-public-attention/the-attention-cycle/warms` tags: ad20-public-attention, public, attention, reputation, media, everyday, risk, opportunity
  Mitzi在街角被人认出来，对方没有要求什么，只说“上次那件事做得不错”。这句客气话被旁边的人听见，下午便有两个陌生人朝Mitzi点头。关注没有减少，但空气松了一点。
  _effects: state_parameter ad20-public-attention.attention 40 -> 36; state_parameter ad20-public-attention.sympathy 45 -> 51; state_parameter ad20-public-attention.opportunity 50 -> 54; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1934.01** `r13-fam.confront-rumour::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::rumour-crisis/denounced-name` tags: task, social, rumor, reputation, conflict, vienna, urban, crisis
  洗衣房里的人压低声音说，Friedrich Berg已经把Mitzi的名字写进了门房的本子，说Mitzi替罢工的人送过饭。Mitzi没有吵，只问本子现在在谁手里。Gustav Winter把最后一件衣服拧干，说昨晚看见本子被送进了分局。
  _effects: reputation -0.12; condition; flag +named_in_porter_book; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71; knowledge “the porter’s book was taken to the district police office”_

- **1934.01** `vienna-history-kraus-lecture/leave-before-the-end` tags: history, culture, karl_kraus, cafe_central, public_lecture, press
  朗读开始不久，Mitzi 发现后排有人在记名字，便提前离开。走到门口时，Karl Kraus 的声音还在背后，句子像钉子一样短。第二天，Mitzi 在报摊上看到同一篇文章被删去了两段。
  _effects: knowledge “Café Central 的公开朗读吸引便衣和报纸记者，现场听众并不全是同情者。”; relation acquaintance with person-karl-kraus; cash -1 (coffee before leaving the reading); mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1934.01** `p9-work-transport.strike-rumor/strike-rumor-sign` tags: work, factory, railway, transport, shift, labour, strike, rumor
  下班后Mitzi沿着玛利亚希尔夫大街走到工会活动室，屋里烟雾腾腾，木椅上坐满了人。Anna Bauer念了几条要求，每小时加两赫勒，每班有固定的休息。Mitzi在名单上签了字，捐了二十赫勒进罢工箱，但没答应站到队伍最前面去。
  _effects: cash -0.2 (捐给罢工箱的钱); skill; relation acquaintance with person-anna-bauer_

- **1934.04** `major-crisis.rent-increase/locks-changed` tags: crisis, housing, rent, eviction, family, poverty, migration, household
  第二个星期，Mitzi下工回来，门锁已经换了。床架、锅和一只装票据的铁盒堆在楼道里，看门人Franz Berg把一张写有“自愿迁出”的纸递过来，说签了就能把东西搬进后院的共用间。Mitzi没有签，先把铁盒抱进怀里，手指被箱角划出一道口子。
  _effects: move place-wien-leopoldstadt-tenement; residence; cash -9 (cart, storage and a replacement lock); condition; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-89; role; flag +evicted; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-89_

- **1934.08** `vienna-medical-treatment.sanatorium/ignore-diagnosis` tags: health, medical, family, money
  抽屉一推就卡住，Mitzi用膝盖顶了一下才合严。上工路上经过药房，橱窗里摆着润肺糖浆的广告画，她别过头去。车间主任点名时，她应声比平时慢了一拍。
  _effects: condition; mood -0.040000000000000036_

- **1935.01** `state_tick:ad20-public-attention/the-attention-cycle/cools` tags: ad20-public-attention, public, attention, reputation, media, everyday, risk, opportunity
  Mitzi发现最近没有人再提那件事。报纸换了头版，邻居也换了话题；本该轻松，Mitzi却在买菜时反复整理衣领，好像还在等一个不会来的目光。
  _effects: state_parameter ad20-public-attention.attention 36 -> 28; state_parameter ad20-public-attention.sympathy 51 -> 48; state_parameter ad20-public-attention.opportunity 54 -> 52; mood -0.010000000000000009_

  Mitzi的房租也到期了，只好把Gustav Berg的缝纫机搬到自己屋里，说等还钱再还机器。Gustav Berg来取针线盒时，看见缝纫机头上盖着布，站了一会儿就走了；第二天，整栋楼都知道这件事。
  _effects: relation rival with person-gustav-berg; role; reputation -0.04000000000000001; flag +seized_sewing_machine; knowledge “缝纫机被扣作抵押，针线盒还留在邻居手里。”_

  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33_

  _effects: cash 0.6 (争执后按降低的工价结算); reputation 0.03; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-55; mood -0.030000000000000027_
