  _effects: cash 3 (按新规定多出的学徒工资交给家里); food; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-48_

- **1938.06** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.gramophone-wall/gramophone-wall.knock-and-bargain` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Mitzi上楼敲了Hedwig的门，说唱针刮在墙上，夜班的人睡不安稳。Hedwig把留声机从墙边挪到窗下，垫了一块毛毡，答应十点以后不再放。下楼时进行曲还在响，但闷了些，Mitzi能听见自家水壶的响声了。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Hedwig-world-vienna-1912; health 0.03 (夜里终于能早些入睡)_

- **1939.01** `hs18-occupation.piano::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-factory/refuse-to-sign` tags: occupation, piano, requisition, witness, neighbor, history, vienna, urban
  Mitzi把笔推回去，说钢琴的主人就住在隔壁。Friedrich Hofer没有争，只让工人把琴盖也搬走，又记下Mitzi的门牌号。卡车开走时，Otto Bauer家的窗子开了又关；Mitzi站在空出来的门洞前，听见自己的呼吸声。
  _effects: state_add hs18-street-witness state:world-vienna-1912:516; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000729:child; reputation 0.020000000000000018; flag +refused_piano_receipt; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1939.01** `state_expire:under-investigation` tags: under-investigation, legal, police, investigation, risk, state, state_tick, state:under-investigation
  Mitzi的「被调查」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove under-investigation state:world-vienna-1912:459_

- **1939.01** `r13-work.collective-bargaining::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-printshop::reform-eight-hour-clause/employer-stalls-on-hours` tags: work, union, bargaining, wages, institution, vienna, urban, crisis
  Maria Kral 2说八小时是给官办工厂的，私人厂子按订单算。Mitzi把合同草案带回去，工人们围着看，没人愿意先签名。拖到第三周，厂里招了几个新工人，合同的事就搁在Ferdinand Bauer的抽屉里，和去年的那张放在一起。
  _effects: flag +work13_hours_clause_stalled; knowledge “the employer is hiring around the eight-hour clause”_

- **1939.01** `state_reaction:mb17-newcomer/workplace-footing` tags: mb17-newcomer, migration, newcomer, language, network, housing, belonging, state
  工头让Mitzi把一箱零件搬到后间，又顺口教了他两个德语词。午休时，一个老工人把多余的汤推过来；Mitzi没有推回去，只把碗底最后一点喝干净，把空碗放回对方手边。
  _effects: state_parameter mb17-newcomer.localNetwork 20 -> 23; state_parameter mb17-newcomer.language 24 -> 25; mood 0.010000000000000009_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:008162_

- **1939.02** `df18-dream.water-in-the-cellar::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-printshop/move-the-crates` tags: dream, omen, home, water, coincidence, eerie, everyday, vienna
  梦里，Mitzi看见地窖的水漫过脚踝，木箱一只只浮起来。第二天一早，Mitzi把箱子和煤筐搬到床上。中午，楼下的水管裂了，水沿着墙根流进地窖，只浸湿了一只空桶。Anton Bauer 3说Mitzi运气好，Mitzi没有解释。
  _effects: cash -1 (搬箱子时打碎了一只罐子); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-87; knowledge “我梦见地窖进水，第二天水管真的裂了。”; flag +df18_moved_crates_before_flood; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-87_

- **1939.04** `ld18-wedding.private-rite::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-factory/rings-in-a-borrowed-room` tags: love, wedding, marriage, queer, secret, ritual, commitment, secret_intimacy
  婚礼在借来的房间里举行，证人是洗衣房的Katharina Berg。戒指是从当铺买的，誓词写在洗衣单背面；Katharina Berg念到“无论贫穷还是疾病”时，楼下有人敲门，三个人同时屏住呼吸。门开了，是来收煤气费的。
  _effects: state_add ld18-impossible-attachment state:world-vienna-1912:531; cash -1 (the pawnshop rings and the gas bill); mood 0.06000000000000005; flag +private_wedding_rite; flag +impossible_attachment_active_

- **1939.04** `state_reaction:ld18-impossible-attachment/a-family-wedding` tags: ld18-impossible-attachment, love, intimacy, queer, unregistered, commitment, secret, desire
  亲戚的婚礼上，Mitzi被安排和伴侣分开坐。司仪问有没有人反对，Mitzi低头喝汤；伴侣在另一桌替别人倒酒，手很稳，酒没有洒出来。散席时，两个人在衣帽间碰见，只说了一句“外面下雨”。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 15 -> 25; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.grief 0 -> 4; mood -0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:008395_

- **1939.05** `vienna-printshop-daily.union-pamphlet/pamphlet-refuse` tags: work, factory, labour, social
  Mitzi把传单推回Mitzi Berg手里，说这活不能在我这台机上印。Mitzi Berg收起纸走了，第二天车间里多了一个不说笑的角落。
  _effects: relation colleague with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000940:child; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1940.01** `hs18-witness.reporter::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/sell-the-account` tags: witness, reporter, memory, money, risk, history, vienna, urban
  Ferdinand Bauer把几张钞票压在咖啡杯底下，说只要一段能上版的细节。Mitzi讲了钢琴、门洞和那件湿外套；对方记得很快，临走前问能不能用真名。Mitzi说用化名，记者笑了一下，说化名也得像真的。
  _effects: cash 4 (sold a witness account to a reporter); state_parameter hs18-street-witness.retellings 0 -> 2; state_parameter hs18-street-witness.danger 60 -> 75; state_parameter hs18-street-witness.certainty 90 -> 93; reputation 0.020000000000000018; flag +sold_witness_account_

- **1940.01** `state_tick:mb17-newcomer/first-year/still-a-guest` tags: mb17-newcomer, migration, newcomer, language, network, housing, belonging, state
  Mitzi又搬了一次房，箱子里多了一把缺齿的梳子和一叠没寄出的信。新住处离电车线更远，邻居说德语很快；他只能点头，等对方走后再把门关上，把今天听错的词一个一个记在旧票据背面。
  _effects: state_parameter mb17-newcomer.language 25 -> 26; state_parameter mb17-newcomer.localNetwork 23 -> 21; state_parameter mb17-newcomer.housingStrain 67 -> 71; cash -1 (the move and a new rent deposit); mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1940.01** `state_tick:hs18-street-witness/memory-drift/holds` tags: hs18-street-witness, witness, memory, history, testimony, rumor, risk, street
  Mitzi又把那天的顺序在脑子里走了一遍：先是门响，再是靴子，最后才是那声喊。细节还在，只是每讲一次，旁边就多出一点别人替你补上的颜色。
  _effects: state_parameter hs18-street-witness.certainty 93 -> 95_

- **1940.01** `state_tick:ld18-impossible-attachment/invented-anniversary/a-form-with-an-empty-line` tags: ld18-impossible-attachment, love, intimacy, queer, unregistered, commitment, secret, desire
  户籍窗口换了一张新表格，配偶一栏不能空着。Mitzi最后写了“同住人”，办事员抬头看了一眼，说这不作数。回家后，Mitzi把表格折成很小的一块，塞进针线盒；伴侣问今天顺不顺利，Mitzi说和平时一样。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 25 -> 31; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.discretion 55 -> 52; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1940.01** `state_tick:wish-knowledge/knowledge-pressure/press-on` tags: wish-knowledge, wish, wish:knowledge, knowledge, education, curiosity, state, state_tick
  Mitzi又把那件没实现的事翻出来想了一遍；这一次没有告诉任何人。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1940.01** `r13-work.union-delegate::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-printshop::crisis-polling-under-police/vote-called-off` tags: work, union, election, delegate, status, vienna, urban, crisis
  Maria Kral 2把巡警领进车间，说今天有人“聚众”。茶叶盒被踢翻，票纸散在油污里。Mitzi蹲下去捡，被巡警用靴尖拨开。选举改到下个月，可下个月车间要停一半机器；Mitzi把湿透的票纸摊在窗台上，一张张晾干。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; flag +work13_delegate_vote_called_off; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-55; knowledge “the foreman used the police to stop the delegate vote”_

- **1940.03** `vienna-legacy-worklife.apprentice-wage/apprentice-wage.beer-with-senior` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  十二赫勒换来两杯淡啤酒，找零是四枚一赫勒的铜币。Mitzi把铜币收好，听师兄讲那年冬天的事，讲到一半师兄停了停，说“你别到处讲”。酒馆里有人拉手风琴，声音盖过了后半句。
  _effects: cash -1 (请师兄喝一杯啤酒（其中1克朗记入债务）); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-48; skill_

- **1940.06** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.rent-note/rent-note.look-for-cheaper-bed` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Mitzi没去事务所，只在洗衣房打听更便宜的床位。有人提到利奥波德城另一条街上有一间合住的屋子，一个月少一克朗半，只是要跟三个人共用一间。Mitzi去看了两趟，量了窗子和床的位置，回来把箱子底下的旧报纸铺开算账。
  _effects: flag +looking_for_cheaper_housing; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13_

- **1941.01** `hs18-witness.reporter::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-factory/refuse-and-warn` tags: witness, reporter, memory, money, risk, history, vienna, urban
  Mitzi把钞票推回去，说那个人还住在这条街上。Ferdinand Bauer收起本子，问是不是有人威胁。Mitzi说没有，只是不想在下一个门洞里再认出谁。出门时，Mitzi绕到Ferdinand Winter家，把记者来过的事说了一遍。
  _effects: state_parameter hs18-street-witness.danger 75 -> 85; state_parameter hs18-street-witness.retellings 2 -> 3; relation acquaintance with person-ferdinand-winter; reputation -0.010000000000000009; flag +refused_witness_payment_

- **1941.01** `state_expire:mb17-newcomer` tags: mb17-newcomer, migration, newcomer, language, network, housing, belonging, state
  Mitzi的「初到维也纳的人」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove mb17-newcomer state:world-vienna-1912:425; flag -mb17_newcomer_active_

- **1941.01** `state_transition:hs18-street-witness/too-dangerous` tags: hs18-street-witness, witness, memory, history, testimony, rumor, risk, street
  Mitzi从「街面上的目击者」转入另一段处境：the witness became more dangerous than the crime
  _effects: state_transition hs18-street-witness -> political-suspect; flag +witness_became_political_suspect_

- **1941.01** `state_expire:ld18-impossible-attachment` tags: ld18-impossible-attachment, love, intimacy, queer, unregistered, commitment, secret, desire
  Mitzi的「无法登记的伴侣」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove ld18-impossible-attachment state:world-vienna-1912:531; flag -impossible_attachment_active_

- **1941.01** `r13-civ.arrange-move::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/neighbors-carried` tags: social, housing, moving, landlord, cash, vienna, urban, crisis
  Mitzi没有雇车，把家什分摊给楼里的几户人家，一趟一趟往新住处搬。Grete Moser用童车推着锅碗，孩子在后面扶着。天黑前搬完，大家在空屋里分了一壶茶。
  _effects: cash -1 (板车与押金收据（其中1克朗记入债务）); reputation 0.04999999999999999; flag +moved_with_neighbor_help_

- **1941.04** `vienna-historical-social-movement.district-list/organize-relief-table` tags: history, historical_process, social_movement, organization, politics
  Anna Bauer 2从围裙里掏出几张揉皱的收据，Mitzi把它们按日期排开。屋外有人上楼，脚步在门口停了一下又走了。Mitzi把新表折成四折塞进衣袋，说先管这条街，别的明天再说。
  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1941.09** `vienna-parish-aid.sick-call/sick-call.door-only` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Mitzi把药包和半块肥皂交给开门的孩子，嘱咐用水冲开、一天两次，就下了楼。楼梯间里孩子一直站在门口看着，手里攥着纸包，没关门。Mitzi走到二层又折回去，帮他把门带上，说自己家里也等着火。
  _effects: health 0.01 (没有久留，避开了病人的屋子); relation acquaintance with person-otto-winter_

- **1941.09** `p9-history-union-political.meeting/meeting-send-delegation` tags: politics, union, party, meeting, leaflet, police, strike, reform
  Mitzi提议先派两个人去见厂主，谈不成再投票。Alois Lechner嫌太软，Ferdinand Bauer却点头，说家里等着月底的工钱。最后表决，派代表的动议多出四票，Mitzi被记在代表名单的第二行。
  _effects: skill; flag +union_delegate_named_

- **1941.12** `hist15-purge.seed::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-printshop/a-name-is-read` tags: purge, repression, organization, surveillance, history, work, vienna, urban
  处里开会时，名单被念到一半。Mitzi听见自己的名字后面跟着“待审查”，也听见Ferdinand Bauer的椅子在地上拖了一下。散会后，走廊尽头多了一张空桌，抽屉里还留着别人的铅笔。
  _effects: state_add institution-purge state:world-vienna-1912:569; mood -0.030000000000000027; flag +saw_the_purge_list_

- **1942.01** `state_tick:political-suspect/police-interest/avoided` tags: political-suspect, politics, repression, legal, surveillance, state, state_tick
  Mitzi换了路线，避开那家咖啡馆和门口的两个便衣。档案里又添了一页，但今年没有传唤。
  _effects: state_parameter political-suspect.heat 0.4 -> 0.35000000000000003_

- **1942.01** `r13-civ.join-union::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-factory/joined-union` tags: politics, union, membership, work, solidarity, vienna, urban, crisis
  Anna Bauer把会员证摊在桌上，说每月一克朗，工伤和失业都有救济。Mitzi数出硬币，Anna Bauer在收据上按了章，又把一张印着“团结”的小卡片塞进Mitzi的口袋。
  _effects: cash -1 (会费收据（其中1克朗记入债务）); flag +joined_union; role; relation colleague with person-anna-bauer; organization_membership; knowledge “the union pays sickness and unemployment benefit”_

- **1942.05** `vienna-social-movement.recruitment/join-and-carry` tags: development, social_movement, organization, politics
  Mitzi在楼梯下面接到一张折成四折的传单。纸上没有署名，只写着下一次集会的地址。Mitzi去了，在昏暗的屋里报上姓名，成为接手后的俱乐部的一员，并答应负责自己所在街区的联络。
  _effects: organization_membership; role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-36_

- **1942.12** `state_reaction:wish-knowledge/knowledge-humiliation` tags: wish-knowledge, wish, wish:knowledge, knowledge, education, curiosity, state, state_reaction

  _effects: flag +wish:knowledge:wounded_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:009597_

- **1943.01** `hs18-blackout.cellar::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-union/trade-for-shelter` tags: blackout, cellar, coal, neighbor, denunciation, history, vienna, urban
  Mitzi没有告发，也没有白帮忙。Alois Moser 2把两桶煤分出一桶，换Mitzi在搜查时把楼上的孩子领到后院。成交时两人都没有提“煤”字，只说“今晚借个火”。
  _effects: state_add hs18-street-witness state:world-vienna-1912:588; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-86; flag +traded_coal_for_shelter; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  _effects: state_parameter political-suspect.heat 0.35000000000000003 -> 0.30000000000000004_

  Mitzi为了赶速度，把一个字母排倒了。校样印出来，主顾把纸摔在柜台上，整版重排的钱从Mitzi的计件里扣。Katharina Winter 2把错字挑出来，钉在Mitzi的字盘上方，说让Mitzi每天先看它一眼。
  _effects: cash -5 (计件单上的小数点（其中5克朗记入债务）); reputation -0.03999999999999998; flag +work13_typesetting_error_

- **1943.04** `vienna-historical-social-movement.district-list/hold-list-and-watch` tags: history, historical_process, social_movement, organization, politics
  Anna Bauer把会费账本推回去，说外头那个组织答应给煤，只要名单。Mitzi把账本合上，用一块旧布包好塞进缝纫机底下，说先看看谁在往外递话。两人都没再提这事。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-anna-bauer; mood -0.030000000000000027; flag +kept_district_list_private_

- **1944.01** `p9-work-jobsearch.negotiate-hours/negotiate-hours.longer-break-only` tags: work, job_search, negotiation, wages, employment, task, hours, saturday
  第二区夜班出租合作社把柜台擦了两遍才开口，说午休可以到三刻钟，收铺的时辰照旧。Mitzi把算好的话咽回去，正午的钟一响，他多坐了会儿，看窗外的车过了一趟又一趟。
  _effects: health 0.020000000000000004 (午休稍长喘了口气); relation employer with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9_

- **1944.04** `p9-history-union-political.leaflets/leaflets-party-printer` tags: politics, union, party, meeting, leaflet, police, strike, reform
  看见巡警在街口来回走，Mitzi把整叠传单原样送回印刷所，油墨味在小屋里压得人发闷。印刷工说星期天集市人多，混在菜摊中间发更稳当，顺手在废纸上记下一个地址给Mitzi。
  _effects: skill; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-48_

- **1944.06** `vienna-church-community.saint-vincent-box/box-request-refused` tags: faith, social, family, home
  登记员翻遍册子，说这户上个月已经领过。楼里的人围着看，谁也没先走开。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-karl-kraus; reputation -0.03999999999999998_

- **1944.08** `vienna-social-movement.aftermath/keep-the-list` tags: development, social_movement, organization, consequence
  Mitzi把普拉特动物园夜班队的名单藏进缝纫机抽屉的夹层。名单上有二十几个名字，其中两个已经搬走，一个被工厂开除。Mitzi没有把最后一行划掉。
  _effects: flag +movement_list_holder; reputation 0.02999999999999997_

- **1945.01** `vienna-life-1945-liberation/survives` tags: war, liberation, 1945
  苏联士兵进城时，Mitzi躲在门后听见坦克履带压过石板路。几天后街口贴出告示，Mitzi去领了一份汤和一块面包。
  _effects: role; food; cash -2 (replacement documents（其中2克朗记入债务）)_

- **1945.01** `state_tick:political-suspect/police-interest/detained` tags: political-suspect, politics, repression, legal, surveillance, state, state_tick
  Mitzi在回家路上被拦住，问话从晚上八点持续到凌晨。没有正式指控，但名字被写进了另一本册子。
  _effects: state_parameter political-suspect.heat 0.25000000000000006 -> 0.4_

- **1945.01** `state_tick:hs18-street-witness/memory-drift/holds` tags: hs18-street-witness, witness, memory, history, testimony, rumor, risk, street
  Mitzi又把那天的顺序在脑子里走了一遍：先是门响，再是靴子，最后才是那声喊。细节还在，只是每讲一次，旁边就多出一点别人替你补上的颜色。
  _effects: state_parameter hs18-street-witness.certainty 85 -> 87_

- **1945.01** `kernel-work.task-workplace-boundary::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-printshop/work-boundary-courtship` tags: task, work, workplace, boundary, romance, vienna, urban, crisis
  Mitzi没有把字条退回去，而是回了一行小字：周日教堂后可以在公园门口见。两人沿着环城路走了半圈，说好不在店里提这件事，也不让Katharina Winter 2替谁排轻活。Emil Berg把字条还给她，纸边已经磨软；周一来上班时，两人只在柜台前点了点头。
  _effects: relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001361:child; role; flag +discreet_workplace_romance; reputation -0.020000000000000018_

- **1945.01** `kernel-culture.cafe-debate::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-union/civic-cafe-lose` tags: culture, cafe, debate, writing, university, vienna, urban, postwar
  她说了三句就被人打断，之后一直听。Katharina Winter 2问她怎么不说话，她说“我在想你们为什么都不提房租”；桌上安静了一会儿，Katharina Winter 2把账单推给她，说“那你想”。她把账单上的数字重新算了一遍，发现少算了自己的咖啡。
  _effects: reputation -0.020000000000000018; knowledge “咖啡馆里的争论可以谈文学和战争，谈房租会让人沉默”; cash -0.5 (替桌上人付的咖啡钱); flag +lost_cafe_debate_

- **1945.02** `state_reaction:hs18-street-witness/retold-in-public` tags: hs18-street-witness, witness, memory, history, testimony, rumor, risk, street
  Mitzi在报上或会上听见自己的话被转述。句子大致没错，只是“我看见”被改成了“大家都看见”；回家的路上，有陌生人朝Mitzi点头。
  _effects: state_parameter hs18-street-witness.retellings 2 -> 4; state_parameter hs18-street-witness.certainty 87 -> 89; reputation 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:010810_

- **1945.03** `p9-history-union-political.meeting/meeting-send-delegation` tags: politics, union, party, meeting, leaflet, police, strike, reform
  Mitzi提议先派两个人去见厂主，谈不成再投票。Anna Bauer嫌太软，Katharina Winter 2却点头，说家里等着月底的工钱。最后表决，派代表的动议多出四票，Mitzi被记在代表名单的第二行。
  _effects: skill_

- **1945.05** `vienna-housing-move.moving-day/moving-day-sell-up` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  收破烂的把两克朗数在门槛上，顺手敲了敲桌板看木料。Mitzi把铁盒扣好，那里面只有照片和几封旧信。Luise Hofer替Mitzi扶着门，说旧床架值不了几个钱，能脱手就不亏。
  _effects: cash 2 (卖掉旧床架和桌子收进的钱); relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001282:child_

- **1946.01** `hs18-witness.reporter::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-union/tell-a-safer-version` tags: witness, reporter, memory, money, risk, history, vienna, urban
  Mitzi把最危险的部分删掉，只说看见一辆卡车和一块掉漆的琴腿。Katharina Winter 2听完，把钞票收回一半，说这样的故事登不出头版。Mitzi收下另一半，觉得口袋比刚才轻了，也比刚才安全。
  _effects: cash 1 (half payment for a safer account); state_parameter hs18-street-witness.retellings 4 -> 5; state_parameter hs18-street-witness.danger 60 -> 55; state_parameter hs18-street-witness.certainty 89 -> 84; flag +gave_safer_witness_version_

- **1946.01** `state_tick:political-suspect/police-interest/detained` tags: political-suspect, politics, repression, legal, surveillance, state, state_tick
  Mitzi在回家路上被拦住，问话从晚上八点持续到凌晨。没有正式指控，但名字被写进了另一本册子。
  _effects: state_parameter political-suspect.heat 0.4 -> 0.55_

- **1946.01** `state_tick:hs18-street-witness/memory-drift/blurs` tags: hs18-street-witness, witness, memory, history, testimony, rumor, risk, street
  Mitzi忽然想不起那个人先迈的是左脚还是右脚。邻居说自己记得清清楚楚，还补了一个细节；Mitzi听着，竟分不清那是自己看见的，还是后来听来的。
  _effects: state_parameter hs18-street-witness.certainty 84 -> 76_

- **1946.01** `r13-edu.typing-course::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-printshop/fired-for-transposed-figures` tags: education, typing, office, work, training, vienna, urban, postwar
  Mitzi把数字键和字母键弄混，一份账单打错了三位数。老板把纸摔在桌上，说打字员连数都认不清。Mitzi当天被辞退，工钱扣了一半，走出门时才想起自己连那台打字机的型号都没记住。Mitzi在门口站了一会儿才走。
  _effects: cash -2 (打字机的字母盘); unemployment (打字机的字母盘)_

- **1946.02** `ml18-gambling.card-system::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-union/stop-while-ahead` tags: gambling, luck, money, casino, risk, absurd, vienna, urban
  纸牌桌上连开三把庄。Mitzi按自己写在烟盒背面的翻倍法，把最后十五克朗押在闲上。庄家翻开一张四，闲家九点；Rosa Moser的手在桌下抓住Mitzi的袖子；Mitzi把赢回的三十克朗换成现钱，数出十克朗塞进袜筒，剩下的放进内袋，说今晚到此为止。
  _effects: cash 30 (winnings taken off the card table); state_add ml18-sudden-fortune state:world-vienna-1912:678; relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9; reputation 0.020000000000000018; flag +left_the_table_ahead; mood 0.07999999999999996; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-140; flag +sudden_fortune; reputation 0.020000000000000018; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1946.06** `vienna-historical-social-movement.district-list/hold-list-and-watch` tags: history, historical_process, social_movement, organization, politics
  Ferdinand Huber把会费账本推回去，说外头那个组织答应给煤，只要名单。Mitzi把账本合上，用一块旧布包好塞进缝纫机底下，说先看看谁在往外递话。两人都没再提这事。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-51_

- **1946.06** `vienna-parish-aid.sick-call/sick-call.help-in` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Mitzi和Friedrich Stein一起上楼，门没锁，屋里一股酸味。病人裹着毯子坐在床沿咳嗽，炉子只剩灰。Mitzi去楼下讨了几块煤核，蹲着把火生起来，又用带来的面粉搅了一小锅稀粥。药粉冲开，碗底沉着白渣，Mitzi先垫了药钱，说以后再说。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (替病人垫上药粉钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-76; flag +parish_sick_visitor; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-76_

- **1946.08** `vienna-women-housework.piecework-bundle/piecework.hide-defects` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  篮底那两件领子卷得很紧，Mitzi用围裙角把篮沿擦了擦。Hermann Kral 3翻了翻面上几件，拿尺子比了比，没再往下。Karl Kraus在走廊里问交了多少，Mitzi含混说了句够买面包。
  _effects: cash 0.7 (交活所得，含未返工的两件); reputation -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-125_

- **1947.01** `state_expire:hs18-street-witness` tags: hs18-street-witness, witness, memory, history, testimony, rumor, risk, street
  Mitzi的「街面上的目击者」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove hs18-street-witness state:world-vienna-1912:588_

- **1947.01** `state_tick:ml18-sudden-fortune/fortune-year/money-keeps-working` tags: ml18-sudden-fortune, money, windfall, sudden_fortune, household, luck, inheritance, lottery
  一年过去，钱没有变多，却也没有一下花完。Mitzi把一部分存进邮局储蓄所，一部分借给楼下的鞋匠；鞋匠每月送来一双补好的鞋，利息就夹在鞋垫下面。街口的杂货商开始把好面粉留到Mitzi来的时候。
  _effects: cash 2 (interest and small repayments on the windfall); state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 1 -> 2; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1947.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.sunday-hill/p9-social-daily-culture.sunday-hill.reach-summit` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:daily
  Mitzi和Otto Lechner按原计划沿葡萄园间的土路往上走，鞋子沾满黄泥，到山顶时风把汗湿的衬衫吹得冰凉。下山在第一个酒棚坐下，两人合点了一壶新酒和一块面包，一共一克朗二十赫勒；Mitzi喝到第二杯时脸颊发烫，把剩下的半块面包包进手帕带回家，第二天腿酸，却睡得很沉。
  _effects: cash -1.2 (卡伦山酒棚的新酒和面包); food; health 0.019999999999999997 (星期天登山远足); mood 0.06000000000000005; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-91_

- **1948.01** `statistical_death/violence` tags: death
  Mitzi在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang卷入一场冲突，头部和胸口受了重伤。旁人把人抬回家，夜里伤势恶化。
  _effects: death (暴力)_

### 251 subject events

- **1913.01** `vienna-life-infant-first-smile/smile-at-the-lamp` tags: infancy, family, first, warm
  Josef Bauer把Josef抱到窗边，煤汽灯罩上有一圈晃动的光。孩子盯着看了很久，忽然笑出声，口水滴在Josef Bauer的袖口上。屋里的人都停了一下，像是听见了什么了不起的消息。
  _effects: mood 0.04000000000000001; health 0.010000000000000009 (a good day)_

- **1914.08** `vienna-life-child-family-legend/lost-relative` tags: childhood, family, secret, story
  Josef Bauer在收拾箱子时翻出一张没有署名的照片，说那是家里一个“去了很远地方”的人。Josef问还回不回来，Josef Bauer把照片翻过去，说先把名字记住，别在饭桌上问。
  _effects: knowledge “家里有一张没有署名的照片，照片上的人去了很远的地方。”; flag +family_legend_photo; mood -0.01_

- **1916.01** `vienna-life-child-first-friend/shared-toy` tags: childhood, friendship, family, comic
  Klara在楼道里滚一只铁环，Josef站在门口看。Klara把铁环推过来，说“你推一次，我推一次”。两个人从楼梯口推到院子里，铁环倒下的声音把午睡的大人吵醒了一排。
  _effects: relation friend with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912; skill; mood 0.05_

- **1916.10** `p9-childhood-household.half-bed/chalk-line` tags: childhood, family, household, home, siblings, illness, neighbors, injury
  粉笔是Josef在讲台底下捡的，短得只剩指甲盖长。Josef趴在床板上，从这头划到那头，线歪了一点，又用唾沫擦掉重划。夜里Emil的膝盖越过线，Josef用胳膊肘顶回去，两个人在黑暗里推来推去，被子滑到地上。
  _effects: health -0.030000000000000027 (夜里推搡没睡踏实); relation kin with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912_

- **1916.12** `vienna-early-childhood.stairwell-fall/arm-bruise` tags: childhood, family, home, street, injury
  楼梯间的灯罩空着，第三级踏板比别处高。Josef一脚踩空，滚到平台，右小臂磕在铁扶手的弯头上。Josef Bauer听见哭声跑出来，把Josef抱回屋，用冷水毛巾敷那块青紫，再用旧布条缠了两圈。夜里Josef哭醒两次。
  _effects: health -0.03999999999999998 (从楼梯滚下，小臂挫伤); mood -0.04999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-6_

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.mind-sibling/sibling-carried-afternoon` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, sibling, care
  Grete Bauer哭得打嗝，手指一直朝井台方向指。Josef蹲下把Grete Bauer驮上背，沿着墙根走，数砖缝给Grete Bauer听：一、二、三……数到第四圈，背上的呼吸才匀了。天黑时Josef的胳膊麻得抬不过肩，Josef Bauer回来接过孩子，Josef扶着门框甩了好一会儿手。
  _effects: skill; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000444:child_

- **1918.05** `vienna-childhood-friends.courtyard-race/won-the-race-lost-a-button` tags: childhood, social, school, street
  晒衣绳下挂着湿床单，孩子们绕着跑，鞋底把水甩到墙上。Josef在第三圈超过Maria Stein，手先碰到墙根。两颗玻璃弹珠进了Josef口袋，外套第二颗纽扣却崩进下水道。Maria Stein从二楼窗口骂了一句，扔下一根别针。
  _effects: mood 0.049999999999999996; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-6_

- **1919.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-sold-out-potatoes` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  木窗“咔”地合上，Josef的布袋还敞着口。伙计隔着板缝说下星期再来。Josef在墙根站了一会儿，把硬币排在掌心数了两遍，走到旁边土豆摊前，挑了几个小的，让摊主当面过秤。家里人晚上把钱和土豆倒在桌上，重新数了一遍。
  _effects: food; mood -0.03; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9_

- **1919.04** `vienna-childhood-street.cigarette-card/card-stolen` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  Josef正低头看卡上的皇帝胡子，一个穿背带裤的男孩从侧面撞过来。卡飞出去，落在马车辙里。Josef扑过去捡，手掌被碎石划开一道口子。Emil追到巷子尽头，只抓下一块袖布。回来时Josef还蹲在地上，把沾泥的卡往袖子上擦。
  _effects: mood -0.08; condition_

- **1920.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.mind-sibling/sibling-falls-at-well` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, sibling, care
  土豆滚进墙角的煤灰里，Josef弯腰去追，再抬头时Hermann Bauer已经坐在井台边，裤腿湿了一片，哭声一抽一抽。Karl Kraus把孩子抱进灶间，Josef跟在后面，手指抠着门框。Josef Bauer回来没问经过，只把Josef的碗收走一半，说“看个人都看不住”。
  _effects: mood -0.06; relation kin with person-josef-bauer_

- **1920.02** `p9-childhood-household.fever-night/called-corner-doctor` tags: childhood, family, household, home, siblings, illness, neighbors
  Hedwig Stein披着外套去街角敲门，老医生过了半个钟头才提着皮包上楼。医生让Josef坐起来，把听诊器贴在背上，听完从纸包里抖出三撮药粉。Josef Bauer数出两克朗放在桌角，医生用铅笔头写：稀粥、温水、不要面包皮。
  _effects: cash -2 (出诊费和药粉); health 0.05999999999999999 (服药后热退了些); relation acquaintance with person-hedwig-stein_

- **1921.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-dragged-docked-pay` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  床单太长，Josef拖着一角往绳子走，布边扫过煤渣，立刻黑了一道。Karl Kraus弯腰看了看，脸沉下来，说这角得重洗，工钱扣一半。Josef接过一赫勒，蹲在盆边用清水搓那一道灰，搓到指节发红，水面上还浮着煤末。
  _effects: cash 0.01 (被扣后只剩一赫勒); mood -0.05; relation acquaintance with person-karl-kraus; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17_

- **1922.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.mind-sibling/sibling-shelter-missed-river` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, sibling, care
  巷口的喊声第二次传来时，Josef正用膝盖压住木盆边沿，给Hermann Bauer挡穿堂风。Hermann Bauer在旧布底下翻了个身，Josef立刻停住，等呼吸又匀了才敢动。Josef Bauer推门进来，先看见盆里睡着的Hermann Bauer，再看见Josef冻红的脚背，把灶上留的汤热了热。
  _effects: mood -0.01999999999999999; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000690:child; relation kin with person-josef-bauer_

- **1922.06** `vienna-childhood-friends.courtyard-race/won-the-race-lost-a-button` tags: childhood, social, school, street
  晒衣绳下挂着湿床单，孩子们绕着跑，鞋底把水甩到墙上。Josef在第三圈超过Helene Winter，手先碰到墙根。两颗玻璃弹珠进了Josef口袋，外套第二颗纽扣却崩进下水道。Helene Winter从二楼窗口骂了一句，扔下一根别针。
  _effects: mood 0.05; relation acquaintance with person-helene-winter_

- **1922.10** `vienna-childhood-street.cigarette-card/card-gift` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  Josef每天放学都在柜台前站一会儿，看玻璃板底下压着的烟画，顺手把歪了的报纸摞齐。Elisabeth Bauer擦完柜台，从抽屉最里面摸出一张揉皱的风景卡，边角还沾着烟丝，塞过来时说了句“别挡着门”。Josef把卡夹进课本，第二天带了一张重复的来给Elisabeth Bauer看。
  _effects: mood 0.06; reputation 0.02_

- **1923.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-bread-with-friend` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  Franz Lechner家的孩子站在绳子另一头，Josef递夹子时故意慢半拍，两个人把床单拉得没有一道褶。收工后Franz Lechner端出半包隔夜面包，掰开时掉了一桌屑。Josef把分到的那块用围裙兜住，回家路上闻着麦味，硬是没咬。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-franz-lechner; skill_

- **1923.08** `vienna-adolescence.brothers-torn-jacket/sew-it-yourself` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  Josef在厨房找到一截粗线，颜色和外套差了一截。缝到一半，线打结，Josef用牙咬断，重新来。Josef Bauer坐在对面，没问外套的事，只把汤碗推过来，说“先吃”。Josef把最后一针打完，汤面已经结了一层薄皮。
  _effects: skill; food; relation kin with person-josef-bauer_

- **1924.01** `state_tick:wish-craft/craft-pressure/put-away` tags: wish-craft, wish, wish:craft, craft, work, mastery, state, state_tick
  Josef暂时把那件事收进抽屉；日子还有别的东西要应付。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000004_

- **1924.01** `vienna-adolescence.workshop-apprentice/apprenticeship-rejected` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  “太小，手也薄。”Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne说完，把Josef推过的木板翻过来，指着上面一道啃痕。Josef Bauer把布包往肩上提了提，说打扰了。Josef在门口多站了一会儿，直到里面的刨子声把话头盖过去，才跟着Josef Bauer下楼。
  _effects: mood -0.08000000000000002; reputation -0.05; relation kin with person-josef-bauer_

- **1924.02** `r2-leisure.cinema-fire::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::cinema-fire-reform/cinema-fire-drill-worked` tags: leisure, cinema, fire, panic, rescue, reform, medical, schooling
  消防检查刚过，电影院换了新的石棉幕布，门口挂着“出口畅通”。放映到一半，胶片又卡了，焦味从放映室飘出来。Katharina Winter 2第一个冲到门口，把两扇门都推开。Rosa Moser在里面喊只是卡片，别慌。观众还是走了一半。
  _effects: reputation 0.03; condition; flag +cinema_fire_drill_worked; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41; knowledge “the new fire doors open outward and were tested during a jam”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41_

- **1924.04** `r15-work.master-takes-on::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/turned-away` tags: work, craft, apprentice, trade, poverty, reform, medical, schooling
  Rosa Moser看了看Josef的手，说太小，拿不稳凿子。Josef回家没有提这件事，第二天去码头找零工。
  _effects: mood -0.08000000000000002; flag +apprenticeship_refused_

- **1925.01** `r13-edu.boarding-school::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/admitted-to-dormitory` tags: education, boarding_school, residence, family, scholarship, reform, medical, schooling
  Josef把铁皮箱绑在自行车后座，骑了三十里到寄宿学校。舍监检查行李，把家里的腌菜罐没收，说会招老鼠。Josef在熄灯后偷偷把罐子从垃圾堆捡回来，藏在床底，第一顿饭就着腌菜吃完了两碗粥。第一个星期Josef把腌菜分给同屋，换回三张邮票和一支不漏水的钢笔。
  _effects: flag +boarding_school_admitted; education secondary_

- **1925.01** `yf18-runaway.bundle::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/buy-the-ticket-tonight` tags: runaway, escape, family, plan, night_train, reform, medical, schooling
  Josef趁Josef Bauer去上夜班，把储钱罐砸在窗台上。硬币滚进床缝，Josef趴在地上摸到最后一枚，跑去南站买了一张最便宜的单程票；售票员问要不要往返，Josef说不用，声音比自己想象中平静。
  _effects: state_add yf18-runaway-plan state:world-vienna-1912:94; cash -3 (the savings jar was broken for a ticket); flag +yf18_ticket_bought; mood 0.03999999999999998; flag +yf18_runaway_plan_active_

- **1925.04** `state_reaction:wish-craft/craft-humiliation` tags: wish-craft, wish, wish:craft, craft, work, mastery, state, state_reaction
  Josef把那句轻视记了很久。它没有变成一个决定，但也没有消失。
  _effects: mood -0.10000000000000003_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:002604_

- **1925.09** `vienna-adolescence.brothers-torn-jacket/sew-it-yourself` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  Josef在厨房找到一截粗线，颜色和外套差了一截。缝到一半，线打结，Josef用牙咬断，重新来。Josef Bauer坐在对面，没问外套的事，只把汤碗推过来，说“先吃”。Josef把最后一针打完，汤面已经结了一层薄皮。
  _effects: skill; food; relation kin with person-josef-bauer_

- **1925.11** `yf18-firstlook.tram-glove::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/return-it-at-the-gate` tags: first_love, love, tram, glove, youth, courtship, reform, medical
  下一站，Josef追下车，把那只手套递还给Elisabeth Bauer。Elisabeth Bauer说了一声谢谢，手指在毛线手套里缩了一下；Josef回到车上才发现自己坐过了两站，只好沿着环城路走回家。
  _effects: state_add yf18-first-awakening state:world-vienna-1912:93; mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +yf18_first_awakening_active_

- **1925.12** `r3-teen.first-friend-group::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/admitted` tags: friendship, roof, belonging, betrayal, class, reform, medical, schooling
  屋顶上的暗号是两声口哨。Josef吹对了一声，第二声卡在喉咙里，Josef Kral笑着替他补上。四个人分吃一袋偷来的栗子，壳扔到楼下，砸在Anna Bauer的雨棚上。
  _effects: mood 0.08000000000000002; flag +joined_roof_group; role; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-53; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-53_

- **1926.01** `state_tick:yf18-first-awakening/half-year-of-glances/answered-glance` tags: yf18-first-awakening, love, youth, first_love, desire, school, awakening, state
  Josef在教堂门口或学校走廊里又遇见Elisabeth Bauer。这一次对方先点了头，Josef准备好的整段话全忘了，只把手里的书换到另一只手上；回家后Josef才发现，自己把书拿倒了。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 45 -> 50; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 25 -> 33; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.reciprocated false -> true; mood 0.03_

- **1926.01** `state_expire:yf18-runaway-plan` tags: yf18-runaway-plan, escape, youth, plan, home, travel, risk, state
  Josef的「床底下的包袱」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove yf18-runaway-plan state:world-vienna-1912:94; flag -yf18_runaway_plan_active_

- **1926.01** `r13-fam.help-homework::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/sums-finished` tags: task, family, school, homework, sibling, literacy, reform, medical
  Josef把煤油灯往桌子中间挪，让Maria Bauer看清算术本上的格子。孩子把七加八数了三遍，Josef没有直接说答案，只把豆子一颗颗排在桌上。最后一页写完时，灯芯已经烧黑，Josef才发现自己的作业还空着半页。
  _effects: flag +helped_sibling_with_homework; role; skill; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000585:child; skill_

- **1926.02** `yf18-runaway.cousin-letter::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/answer-in-secret` tags: runaway, letter, cousin, work, plan, reform, medical, schooling
  格拉茨的表姐来信，说旅馆缺一个叠床单的女孩，管吃住，工钱少但能攒下。Josef把信藏在算术本里，用铅笔在背面写回信；邮票是从Josef Bauer的抽屉里偷的，信封上不敢写自己的名字，只写了“内详”。
  _effects: state_add yf18-runaway-plan state:world-vienna-1912:123; cash -1 (a stolen stamp and writing paper); flag +yf18_cousin_reply; mood 0.03; flag +yf18_runaway_plan_active_

- **1926.02** `state_reaction:yf18-runaway-plan/a-family-question` tags: yf18-runaway-plan, escape, youth, plan, home, travel, risk, state
  饭桌上又为房租吵起来。Josef把面包掰成很小的块，听见父亲说“这个家养不起闲人”；Josef没有回嘴，只在心里把格拉茨的地址又背了一遍。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.suspicion 5 -> 13; state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.resolve 60 -> 58_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:002965_

- **1926.10** `yf18-firstlook.parish-dance::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/the-second-dance` tags: first_love, dance, church, courtship, jealousy, reform, medical, schooling
  舞卡上第一支曲子被Emil写走了。Josef在教会礼堂的木地板上踩错了两步，Emil说没关系，把手放回Josef背后；第三支曲子响起时，Elisabeth Bauer在门口冲Josef比了个手势，Josef假装没看见。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 55 -> 63; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 28 -> 38; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.reciprocated true -> true; mood 0.04000000000000001_

- **1927.01** `vienna-life-apprenticeship/factory-hand` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship, employment
  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne把Josef分到装订间，每天站十个小时，工钱按件算。第一周结束，Josef的指关节肿了，工头说习惯就好。
  _effects: employment Buchbinderhilfe @ org-winter-print; condition_

- **1927.01** `yf18-friend.two-tickets::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/confront-at-the-cloakroom` tags: first_love, friendship, betrayal, dance, tickets, reform, medical, schooling
  Josef在衣帽间堵住Otto Lechner，问那两张票怎么到了Emil手里。Otto Lechner说Josef从来没敢开口，自己只是替Josef把话说了；Josef把票根揉成一团，塞进Otto Lechner的西装口袋，转身时撞翻了侍者的托盘。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 50 -> 55; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.talk 0 -> 10; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 33 -> 28; reputation -0.02; mood -0.05000000000000002_

- **1927.01** `state_tick:yf18-runaway-plan/three-months-of-preparation/route-memorised` tags: yf18-runaway-plan, escape, youth, plan, home, travel, risk, state
  Josef把去车站的路又走了一遍，记住了哪一段有煤气灯、哪一段住着巡夜的警察。回家后，Josef在旧日历背面画了一条线，标出换乘的站名；那条线短得可笑，却比课本上的任何地图都清楚。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.preparation 45 -> 57; state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.suspicion 13 -> 15; mood 0.009999999999999995_

- **1927.01** `state_tick:yf18-first-awakening/half-year-of-glances/answered-glance` tags: yf18-first-awakening, love, youth, first_love, desire, school, awakening, state
  Josef在教堂门口或学校走廊里又遇见Elisabeth Bauer。这一次对方先点了头，Josef准备好的整段话全忘了，只把手里的书换到另一只手上；回家后Josef才发现，自己把书拿倒了。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 63 -> 68; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 38 -> 46; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.reciprocated true -> true; mood 0.03_

- **1927.01** `r13-edu.exam-eve::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/passed-entrance-exam` tags: education, exam, study, poverty, adolescence, reform, medical, schooling
  Josef把煤油灯芯剪短，省下油熬到后半夜。代数题做错三遍，Josef就用冷水拍脸，再把公式抄到手腕内侧。考试那天，监考把袖口翻了一遍，Josef还是把最后一道题解完了。放榜那天，Josef在人群后面站了很久，才敢往前挤。
  _effects: mood 0.06; flag +passed_entrance_exam; skill_

- **1927.01** `state_reaction:yf18-first-awakening/exam-week-distraction` tags: yf18-first-awakening, love, youth, first_love, desire, school, awakening, state
  考试周里，Josef把课本摊在桌上，眼睛却一直看着窗外那条电车线。笔记的页边多了几个没有写完的名字，Josef用橡皮擦掉，纸面起了毛。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 68 -> 65; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.talk 10 -> 12_

- **1927.11** `vienna-apprentice-printshop.lead-type-drop/lead-type-drop.scolded` tags: work, apprentice, factory, social
  Josef在Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne碰翻一整盘8点铅字，字块滚了一地。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne敲着桌沿骂人，扣下两个克朗。Elisabeth Lechner 2蹲在过道里帮忙捡字，只说了一句“下次用两只手托底”。
  _effects: cash -2 (赔偿散落铅字的工钱扣款); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-37; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-37; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-37_

- **1927.12** `r3-teen.police-caution::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::reform-parent-notice/fined` tags: police, caution, record, family, work, reform, medical, schooling
  Anton Winter说按条例罚三克朗，交不出就通知学校。Josef Bauer从内衣口袋里摸出叠好的钞票，一张张数，数到第三张时手停了。回家后，那三克朗被写在厨房的账单上，旁边画了一道红线。
  _effects: cash -3 (派出所里的第一次签字); mood -0.08; flag +fined_by_police; relation rival with person-josef-bauer; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-20_

- **1927.12** `yf18-dare.prater-slide::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/caught-by-the-watchman` tags: dare, prater, fairground, comic, friendship, reform, medical, schooling
  Franz Huber 2的灯从摩天轮那边晃过来时，Rosa已经跑没影了，Josef的鞋带却挂在木槽边。Franz Huber 2把Josef带到园门口，记下住址，说星期一让学校来领人；Josef一路上只担心Rosa会不会把这件事讲给全班。
  _effects: reputation -0.05; mood -0.04000000000000001; flag +yf18_prater_caught; relation friend with person-phase15-Rosa-world-vienna-1912; cash -2 (a fine paid at the Prater office); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33_

- **1928.01** `vienna-life-young-worker/joins-union` tags: adolescence, labour, union
  工会的人在厂门口拦住Josef，说学徒工也该有工票和保险。Josef每月交一克朗会费，把会员证塞进上衣内袋。
  _effects: role; cash -1 (union dues); relation acquaintance with person-anna-bauer_

- **1928.01** `state_expire:yf18-runaway-plan` tags: yf18-runaway-plan, escape, youth, plan, home, travel, risk, state
  Josef的「床底下的包袱」到期；相关安排结束。

  Josef暂时把那件事收进抽屉；日子还有别的东西要应付。
  _effects: mood -0.01999999999999999_

- **1928.01** `r13-civ.first-aid-course::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/certificate-earned` tags: health, first_aid, course, skill, school, reform, medical, schooling
  Franz Huber 2把一条绷带绕在Josef的小臂上，教怎么打结才不会勒住血管。Josef在同桌的手臂上练了三遍，最后一遍得到了点头。课程结束时，Franz Huber 2发了一张油印证书，上面盖着红十字的章。
  _effects: flag +earned_first_aid_certificate; role; skill; knowledge “a bandage must be tight enough to hold but loose enough for ”_

- **1928.07** `vienna-adolescence.brothers-torn-jacket/sell-the-coat` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  巷口的风把外套下摆吹起来。旧货贩子闻了闻领子，说给两克朗。Josef把钱卷进袜筒，回家前在楼梯口站了一会儿。Josef Bauer发现衣钩空了，问了三遍，Josef都说没看见，脚趾在鞋里把硬币越踩越紧。
  _effects: cash 2 (卖掉哥哥旧外套); relation kin with person-josef-bauer; mood -0.04000000000000001_

- **1929.01** `p9-childhood-apprenticeship.leave-bad-workshop/half-measures-stay` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship, labour, training, family, task, task:work
  Josef把窗户指给Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne看，说木板钉死了，味道散不出去。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne让伙计拆掉一半，窗缝里透进风，油墨味淡了一点。清洗剂还是原来那桶，Josef每天下午太阳穴发紧，就用冷水搓手指。工位保住了，日薪照发，工钱袋和平时一样沉。
  _effects: health 0.019999999999999997 (窗户拆掉一半后气味稍减); condition; relation employer with person-ferdinand-winter; cash 1 (保住工位，照常领日薪); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-8_

- **1929.03** `oj18-cinema.reel::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/show-the-reel` tags: cinema, film, projection, night, censorship, work, odd_job, reform
  Josef没有剪，让那卷新闻片继续走。观众先以为这是加映，直到有人站起来喊“这不是我们买的票”。警察在散场前堵住侧门；Wiener Psychoanalytische Vereinigung说是放映员接错了本，Josef被记了名字。
  _effects: reputation 0.020000000000000004; cash -2 (lost wages and paid a fine); condition; state_add oj18-odd-apprenticeship state:world-vienna-1912:215; mood -0.03; flag +showed_banned_reel; knowledge “那卷新闻片里有一张被按下去的脸，散场前警察就堵住了侧门。”; role; flag +odd_job_apprentice_

- **1929.08** `p9-work-transport.strike-rumor/strike-rumor-sign` tags: work, factory, railway, transport, shift, labour, strike, rumor
  下班后Josef沿着玛利亚希尔夫大街走到工会活动室，屋里烟雾腾腾，木椅上坐满了人。Alois Lechner念了几条要求，每小时加两赫勒，每班有固定的休息。Josef在名单上签了字，捐了二十赫勒进罢工箱，但没答应站到队伍最前面去。
  _effects: cash -0.2 (捐给罢工箱的钱); skill; relation acquaintance with person-alois-lechner_

- **1929.10** `vienna-medical-treatment.sanatorium/sanatorium-go` tags: health, medical, family, money
  安娜把钥匙拴在门后的钉子上，又反过来压了压窗栓。车票是硬纸的，打孔时咔嗒一声。Josef在车厢里把诊断书摊在膝盖上，窗外最后几排厂房退远了，松树的味道从窗缝钻进来。
  _effects: cash -12 (疗养院车票和住宿押金); health 0.12000000000000001 (疗养院松林空气和正规休养); relation friend with person-helene-winter_

- **1930.01** `vienna-family-same-sex-male/private-arrangement` tags: family, intimacy, same_sex, persecution, lifecycle, partnership
  Josef和Ferdinand Huber在城边租了一间小屋，对外只说是同事合住。房租四克朗，两人不同时出门，信件用假名，夜里把窗帘拉严；Ferdinand Huber在门后放了一根木棍，Josef把两人的工票分开放，连咳嗽都压着声。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-51; mood 0.04000000000000001; cash -4 (separate room rent); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-51_

- **1930.01** `state_tick:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/strange-year/blamed-for-the-noise` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  楼上住户投诉夜里有人拖椅子，管事的把巡查簿翻到Josef的那一页，说签字太潦草，看不出巡过几趟。Josef想辩解，却想起那晚确实在楼梯间多站了一刻钟；扣掉的工钱刚好够买一双不漏水的鞋。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 20 -> 4; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.reliability 40 -> 30; mood -0.04999999999999999; flag +odd_job_scare_

- **1930.01** `task-vienna-bourgeois-youth.study-funding/loan-with-term` tags: bourgeois, education, culture, salon, university, family, task, task:education
  家里只肯借，不肯给。Josef Bauer拿出一张写明年限的借条，要Josef毕业后第一年收入里还清，月息照银行算。他签了名，把借条和学费一起收进皮夹。
  _effects: cash -50 (借到的学费); relation kin with person-josef-bauer; mood -0.06; flag +family_loan_signed_

- **1930.10** `vienna-parish-aid.funeral-society/funeral-society.quit` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Josef说互助会先退了，这笔钱要留给煤和面包。Anna Moser 2没争辩，在蓝皮本上把名字划了一道，问清楚是不是全家都退，然后把划掉的那行折进本子夹缝。她说以后想再进，要重新排三个月。门关上，Josef听见铅笔在楼梯上轻轻敲了两下栏杆。
  _effects: cash 0.4 (省下本周会费留作煤钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44; flag +funeral_society_left; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44_

- **1930.10** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.breakup/courtyard-quarrel` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family, separation
  话没说完就变成了争。Ferdinand Huber把外套扔在晾衣杆下，Josef说了一句不中听的，声音大得整院都听见。Franz Lechner在三楼把窗开了一条缝，看完了全程，第二天院子里人人都知道；这一架把两个人的关系吵断了，也把Josef的名字在院子里吵薄了一层。
  _effects: relation_end partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-51; reputation -0.07; relation acquaintance with person-franz-lechner; mood -0.10000000000000003_

- **1930.11** `r3-crime.poisoning-case::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::one-microscope/ptomaine-in-the-sausage` tags: crime, medical, poison, marriage, suspicion, reform, schooling, home
  Katharina Berg把剩下的香肠切开，闻了闻，说“是肉坏了，不是人坏了”。Anton Winter不肯信，Katharina Berg把显微镜下的片子推过去，说“你要抓就抓那个卖香肠的”。Ferdinand Huber在病床上笑了一声，Josef却坐在椅子上哭了。
  _effects: mood 0.08000000000000002; flag +poisoning_charge_dropped; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17; knowledge “the sausage was contaminated with ptomaine”_

- **1930.12** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/hospital-night` tags: health, medical, family, money
  护士把冰袋按在Josef额上，水顺着鬓角流进衣领。Friedrich Hofer坐在床脚，数着窗外渐渐亮起的铺面门板，说回去给炉子添块煤。隔壁床的老头整夜在喊一个女人的名字。
  _effects: cash -2 (医院急诊押金); health 0.08 (急诊观察和冰敷退烧); relation friend with person-friedrich-hofer_

- **1931.01** `r13-civ.quarantine::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/observed-quarantine` tags: health, quarantine, epidemic, neighbor, confinement, reform, medical, schooling
  Gustav Berg在门框上画了一道白线，说十天内不许出门，菜和煤会有人送到楼梯口。Josef把线内的鞋一双双收进柜子，数了数米缸，又数了数窗台上的药包。第八天，送来的面包已经发硬。
  _effects: cash -2 (门框上的石灰线); mood -0.04999999999999999; condition; flag +observed_quarantine; knowledge “the quarantine lasts ten days and food is left at the stairh”_

  Anton Winter在铜牌上打了钢印，编号三十七，规定只能在教堂街以南，日落前收工。Josef把铜牌挂在胸前，第一枚硬币落进帽子时，声音比想象中轻。Helene Winter路过，假装系鞋带，把一枚十赫勒压在石子下面。
  _effects: cash 2 (街角的铜牌); flag +licensed_to_beg; role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-20; knowledge “licensed beggars may not cross north of Church Street”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-32_

- **1931.05** `vienna-parish-aid.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.take-and-carry` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  汤是豆子熬的，表面浮着几粒油星，气味把整条巷子都占了。Maria Weiss排在前面，回头替Josef看着位置，嘴里数着还剩几个人。铁罐递过去时烫手，Josef用围裙角垫着。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18; mood 0.02999999999999997; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18_

- **1931.08** `p9-history-union-political.leaflets/leaflets-party-printer` tags: politics, union, party, meeting, leaflet, police, strike, reform
  看见巡警在街口来回走，Josef把整叠传单原样送回印刷所，油墨味在小屋里压得人发闷。印刷工说星期天集市人多，混在菜摊中间发更稳当，顺手在废纸上记下一个地址给Josef。
  _effects: skill; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-76; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-76_

- **1932.01** `vienna-life-work-injury/crushed-hand` tags: adulthood, labour, injury
  Josef在换纸时左手被压板夹住。工友关掉机器，工头拿来急救箱，把伤手包起来送去医院；厂里先垫了十克朗医药费，事故报告写的是“操作不慎”。Josef的左手从此握不紧拳，指尖总是凉的，天阴时骨缝里发疼。
  _effects: condition; cash 10 (employer medical advance)_

- **1932.01** `state_expire:oj18-odd-apprenticeship` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state

  Josef又把那件没实现的事翻出来想了一遍；这一次没有告诉任何人。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +wish:craft:pursuing_

- **1932.01** `kernel-social.reconciliation::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/reconciliation.shared-pot` tags: task, task:social, social, reconciliation, family, apology, conflict, reform
  Josef端着一锅炖肉去敲Elisabeth Bauer的门，说肉炖多了，一个人吃不完。Elisabeth Bauer让他进屋，把两只盘子摆在桌上；两人先谈煤价，再谈母亲的坟，最后才提那笔说不清的钱。Rosa Moser在楼下等消息，等到灯灭才走。
  _effects: relation kin with person-elisabeth-bauer; cash -2 (meat and coal for the reconciliation supper); role; flag +family_reconciled; knowledge “两人把煤价、母亲的坟和那笔钱都谈到了。”_

  登记桌后面，Anton Winter把表格推过来，说这一家人缺一个本地担保人。Klara抱着一只藤箱，箱角用绳子捆了三道。Josef写下自己的名字和门牌号，笔尖顿了一下；Anton Winter盖章时没有抬头，只说“出了事找你”。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912; flag +guaranteed_refugee_family; knowledge “担保人要替被担保的人负责。”; reputation -0.009999999999999995_

- **1932.06** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_no_damage` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Josef刚把锅端下灶，火苗就蹿了起来，Franz Weiss一脚踩灭地上的油布，顺手抄起沙箱里的沙子盖住灶口。两人都没受伤，但Franz Weiss的鞋底烧穿一个洞，那是唯一一双出门的鞋。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-28; mood -0.04999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-28_

- **1932.10** `vienna-market-household.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.sell` tags: home, market, food, social
  鞋匠敲后门问有没有热汤，Josef把剩下的半锅倒进他的罐子里，收下两个十字币。Elisabeth Bauer提着空碗上来时，只看见Josef把硬币塞进围裙口袋。
  _effects: cash 2 (卖出半锅扁豆汤); food_

- **1933.01** `vienna-life-depression/laid-off` tags: adulthood, depression, work, unemployment
  厂里把一张短工名单贴在厂门口。Josef的名字在第三行，旁边写着“下周等通知”。等到月底，通知没有来，工票也停了。
  _effects: unemployment (laid off in the depression); cash -5 (lost wages); condition_

- **1933.01** `task-vienna-bourgeois-youth.concert-outing/church-concert` tags: bourgeois, education, culture, salon, university, family, task, task:culture
  Josef算了一下，四克朗够一个星期午饭。他改约Grete Huber去教堂听免费的管风琴音乐会，捐了两赫勒，坐在后排的长凳上。回去时两人在街角分吃一个烤土豆。
  _effects: cash -0.06 (教堂捐献与烤土豆); food; mood 0.04999999999999999; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13_

  _effects: mood -0.07999999999999999; reputation -0.1; flag +false_accusation; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23; state_add public-scandal state:world-vienna-1912:339; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23_

  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:005793_

- **1933.08** `df18-dream.water-in-the-cellar::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/move-the-crates` tags: dream, omen, home, water, coincidence, eerie, everyday, vienna
  梦里，Josef看见地窖的水漫过脚踝，木箱一只只浮起来。第二天一早，Josef把箱子和煤筐搬到床上。中午，楼下的水管裂了，水沿着墙根流进地窖，只浸湿了一只空桶。Rosa Moser说Josef运气好，Josef没有解释。
  _effects: cash -1 (搬箱子时打碎了一只罐子); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9; knowledge “我梦见地窖进水，第二天水管真的裂了。”; flag +df18_moved_crates_before_flood; mood 0.01999999999999999_

  Josef在电影院后门捡到一叠传单，纸背还印着上周的片名。上面写着罢工和面包，字迹歪斜。Rosa Winter说警察已经到街口，Josef把传单塞进煤箱，只留下一张垫桌脚。
  _effects: knowledge “传单是用电影票纸印的，印的人就在附近。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-12; flag +hid_revolution_leaflets; mood -0.03; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-12_

  Josef看Franz Weiss盆里那几件小衬衫，把自家床单卷起来抱回屋，说等下午再晾。Franz Weiss愣了一下，闷头把衣服挂好，收工回来时在Josef门口放了一小包用旧报纸裹的洗衣碱。Josef打开报纸，碱面里还掺着几粒粗盐。

  Josef和Gustav Lechner在城边租了一间小屋，对外只说是同事合住。房租四克朗，两人不同时出门，信件用假名，夜里把窗帘拉严；Gustav Lechner在门后放了一根木棍，Josef把两人的工票分开放，连咳嗽都压着声。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-31; mood 0.04000000000000001; cash -4 (separate room rent); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-31_

- **1934.01** `r14-soc.confront-rumor::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/made-it-worse` tags: task, rumor, reputation, neighbor_conflict, confrontation, vienna, urban, crisis
  Josef在院子里把Ferdinand Huber堵住，声音大得三层楼都听见。Ferdinand Huber索性把话又说了一遍，还添了两个细节。围观的人越来越多，Josef最后把手里的一篮衣服摔在地上，转身回屋。第二天，Ferdinand Huber把晾衣绳往自家窗边挪了半尺，Josef没有作声。那篮衣服在院子里躺到第二天，被雨淋透了。
  _effects: mood -0.20000000000000007; reputation -0.15; condition; flag +escalated_rumor; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-51_

- **1934.01** `state_reaction:public-scandal/gossip` tags: public-scandal, scandal, reputation, social, state, state_reaction, task, rumor
  Josef走进咖啡馆时，邻桌的声音低了一拍。没有人当面说什么，但每个人都知道该说什么。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; reputation -0.030000000000000027_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:005991_

- **1934.04** `ad20-newspaper-interview::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/story-prints-wrong` tags: media, interview, newspaper, everyday, public, reputation, vienna, urban
  Gustav Berg把Josef的话记成了另一层意思。报纸出来时，标题写着“她说她从不后悔”；Josef记得自己说的是“后悔也没有用”。Josef把报纸折起来，又打开，最后还是把它塞进了炉子。
  _effects: reputation -0.030000000000000027; mood -0.040000000000000036; flag +interview_misquoted; knowledge “报纸把“后悔也没有用”写成了“从不后悔”。”_

- **1934.06** `oj18-zoo.night::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/meat-ledger` tags: zoo, animals, night, keeper, work, odd_job, suspense, vienna
  肉商的账本上少了二十公斤马肉，Gustav Berg要Josef签字认账。Josef知道是夜班门房把肉转卖给了普拉特的香肠摊，但门房是Gustav Berg的小舅子。Josef不签，第二天被调去扫兽栏；臭味洗了三遍还在指甲缝里。
  _effects: reputation -0.030000000000000027; cash -1 (lost the night-shift supplement); mood -0.040000000000000036; state_add oj18-odd-apprenticeship state:world-vienna-1912:376; flag +zoo_night_shift; flag +refused_meat_ledger; knowledge “少掉的二十公斤马肉被夜班门房卖给了香肠摊，门房是园长的亲戚。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-55; role; flag +odd_job_apprentice_

- **1935.01** `p21-duel-at-dawn::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/first-blood` tags: duel, honor, violence, public, scandal, rival, politics, vienna
  子弹擦过Josef的左肩，把外套和衬衫一起撕开。Hedwig没有补第二枪，转身走进雾里；Rosa Moser用领带按住伤口，血从指缝里渗出来。医生在运河桥边缝了六针，警察来问话时，Josef只说是在石头上滑了一跤。
  _effects: condition; state_add under-investigation state:world-vienna-1912:374; state_add p21-scandal-web state:world-vienna-1912:375; relation rival with person-phase15-Hedwig-world-vienna-1912; reputation -0.030000000000000027; flag +wounded_in_duel; knowledge “警察来问话时，伤口还在渗血，当事人说是在石头上滑了一跤。”; mood -0.050000000000000044; flag +scandal_web_active_

- **1935.01** `state_reaction:p21-scandal-web/a-new-scandal` tags: p21-scandal-web, scandal_web, scandal, secret, blackmail, network, leverage, danger
  新的丑闻登出来时，旧的那一页也被重新翻到桌面上。Josef发现两件事共用同一个证人，而那个证人上周还来借过盐。
  _effects: state_parameter p21-scandal-web.exposure 60 -> 66; state_parameter p21-scandal-web.danger 55 -> 59; state_parameter p21-scandal-web.loyalty 30 -> 27; mood -0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:006220_

- **1935.01** `state_tick:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/strange-year/blamed-for-the-noise` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  楼上住户投诉夜里有人拖椅子，管事的把巡查簿翻到Josef的那一页，说签字太潦草，看不出巡过几趟。Josef想辩解，却想起那晚确实在楼梯间多站了一刻钟；扣掉的工钱刚好够买一双不漏水的鞋。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 18 -> 2; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.reliability 55 -> 45; mood -0.050000000000000044_

- **1935.01** `task-vienna-activist.speak-meeting/speak-meeting-shouted-down` tags: politics, activism, union, party, leaflet, meeting, task, task:politics
  他刚说到罢工基金，靠门那排就有人喊“先顾眼前”。话头被截断两三次，后屋分成两片声音，主持人敲桌子也没压住。散会时一个老工人拍拍他肩膀说太急了，他出门才发现手心全是汗。
  _effects: mood -0.08999999999999997; reputation -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-alois-lechner_

- **1935.06** `vienna-parish-aid.funeral-society/funeral-society.defer` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Josef把抽屉拉开又推上，铜币在木板上滚了半圈。Franz Huber 2合上本子说，拖欠的人不少，可规矩就是规矩。她走下两级台阶又回头提醒，发工钱那天她会在教堂侧门等着。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33_

- **1935.10** `bc18-inherit.forty-seven-franz::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/hearse-as-taxi` tags: inheritance, mistaken_identity, debt, funeral, work, absurd, family, vienna
  四十七个弗朗茨·穆勒里，只有Josef拿得出干净的房租簿。公证人Alois Winter 3把遗嘱念完，遗产是一辆灵车、两匹老马和十四克朗的欠账。Josef没有钱下葬，先把灵车租给搬家的人；夜里跑环城大道，白天替人运钢琴。Gustav Moser起初等着看笑话，后来开始给他介绍生意。
  _effects: cash 5 (用灵车替人搬家的收入); role; state_add bc18-ridiculous-fame state:world-vienna-1912:423; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-16; flag +hearse_became_a_moving_van; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-94; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-16; flag +ridiculously_famous; reputation 0.020000000000000018; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1935.10** `state_reaction:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/the-thing-in-the-basement` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  夜班结束前，Josef听见地下室传来拖拽声。下去时，门开着，地上只有一道湿拖把的痕迹；第二天，所有人都说那间屋子锁了十年。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.strangeKnowledge 22 -> 30; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 2 -> 0; mood -0.030000000000000027; knowledge “地下室那扇门后面有东西被搬动过，但巡查簿上没有记录。”_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:006692_

- **1936.01** `vienna-family-divorce/reconcile-for-now` tags: family, marriage, divorce, housing, lifecycle, partnership
  Josef和Gustav Lechner在楼道里吵到半夜，最后因为下个月的房租又坐回同一张桌。谁都没有提和好，只把账本重新摊开；Gustav Lechner把煤钱推过来，Josef接过去数了两遍，手指碰到一起又分开，屋里的灯亮到很晚。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-31; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1936.01** `ml18-family.fortune-split::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/keep-it-for-the-children` tags: family, money, windfall, inheritance, conflict, dark, vienna, urban
  Anna Bauer想把钱拿去盘下街角的小酒馆，Josef却把十五克朗直接交到学校，替孩子续了下一年的学费。Anna Bauer把餐巾摔在桌上，说这家人永远只会把钱埋进土里。Gustav Lechner没有劝，只把汤碗往Josef那边推了推。
  _effects: cash -15 (school fees paid from the windfall); state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.principal 40 -> 25; state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 30 -> 42; relation rival with person-anna-bauer; state_parameter raising-child.careDebt 0 -> 5; flag +fortune_kept_for_child; mood 0.010000000000000009_

  Josef的「被调查」到期；相关安排结束。

- **1936.01** `state_tick:p21-scandal-web/the-web-tightens/held` tags: p21-scandal-web, scandal_web, scandal, secret, blackmail, network, leverage, danger
  又过了一年，那几张纸仍锁在Josef的抽屉里。街上的问话没有变多，也没有变少；Josef在咖啡馆里数了数还能信任的人，发现名单比去年短，但每一个名字都还站得住。
  _effects: state_parameter p21-scandal-web.exposure 66 -> 63; state_parameter p21-scandal-web.loyalty 27 -> 31; state_parameter p21-scandal-web.danger 59 -> 56; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1936.01** `state_tick:bc18-ridiculous-fame/ridiculous-fame-year/the-audience-gets-bored` tags: bc18-ridiculous-fame, fame, reputation, mistaken_identity, promotion, media, absurd, public
  观众开始厌倦同一个故事。园艺会请了更年轻的发明家，女子学校把Josef的名字从海报上撤下；Josef在咖啡馆听见有人说“那个人还在讲火柴”。同一个月，一张报纸把旧照片配上新标题，问Josef是否已经“过气”。纠正的冲动又回来，但没有人再提问。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.legitimacy 20 -> 12; state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.boredom 6 -> 18; state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.invitations 1 -> 0; reputation -0.020000000000000018; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1936.01** `state_tick:wish-craft/craft-pressure/press-on` tags: wish-craft, wish, wish:craft, craft, work, mastery, state, state_tick
  Josef又把那件没实现的事翻出来想了一遍；这一次没有告诉任何人。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1936.01** `r13-fam.negotiate-rent::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/rent-refused` tags: task, commerce, housing, rent, negotiation, vienna, urban, crisis
  Hedwig Stein把收据本合上，说这条街上等着租的人排到了巷口。Josef把准备好的账目念到一半，Hedwig Stein已经转身去敲下一家的门。第二天，门缝里塞进来一张纸条，欠租的数目后面多了一个月。
  _effects: cash -8 (房东的收租日); condition; flag +rent_increase_refused; relation rival with person-hedwig-stein_

- **1936.02** `vienna-legacy-worklife.sick-fund/sick-fund.keep-quiet` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life, injury
  止咳药水是褐色的，瓶口塞着软木塞，摇一摇会起沫。Josef看着工友把表折成小块，折了三折才塞进帽檐。墙上的规章还贴在那里，纸角被风掀起，没人去按。
  _effects: health -0.040000000000000036 (没有正规病假，只能靠药水撑着); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-29; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-29_

- **1936.03** `bc18-ghost.death-certificate::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/accepts-the-file` tags: bureaucracy, legal, death, identity, mistake, paperwork, absurd, vienna
  户籍官Friedrich Hofer为了结掉一桩陈年失踪案，在档案上给Josef盖了“推定死亡”。三天后，Josef收到一张慰问卡和一本作废的配给簿；同一天，追了半年的债主忽然不再上门。旧货商听说后，花两克朗买走一份抄本，说要给一个躲债的朋友看看格式。
  _effects: cash 2 (卖给旧货商的死亡证明抄本); state_add bc18-bureaucratic-ghost state:world-vienna-1912:458; reputation -0.010000000000000009; relation acquaintance with person-friedrich-hofer; flag +declared_dead_while_alive; mood 0.010000000000000009; flag +bureaucratic_ghost_

- **1936.05** `p9-gap-adult-unemployed.dock-morning/dock-picked-coal` tags: adulthood, unemployment, casual_labour, poverty, daily, dock
  多瑙河码头的雾还没散，工头捏着名单念了Josef的名字。煤灰落在汗湿的脖颈上，Otto Bauer在铁门边看着，说这活儿一天顶三天，可肩膀得疼上一个礼拜。
  _effects: cash 4.2 (卸煤半日的工钱)_

- **1936.06** `state_reaction:bc18-ridiculous-fame/reprinted-wrong` tags: bc18-ridiculous-fame, fame, reputation, mistaken_identity, promotion, media, absurd, public
  报纸的周末副刊又把Josef的旧照片翻出来，配上一个更整齐的故事：发明、失败、谦逊的晚年。Josef写信去更正，编辑回了一张便条，说读者喜欢现在这个版本。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.notoriety 25 -> 31; state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.absurdity 65 -> 70; state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.legitimacy 12 -> 8; flag +story_reprinted_wrong; mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1937.01** `bc18-fame.lecture-tour::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/waterworks-question` tags: fame, lecture, travel, absurd, public, invention, reputation, vienna
  Gustav Berg请Josef顺便看看城里的水厂。Josef把一只标着“关闭”的阀门拧向另一边，半小时后，半条街的菜窖进了水。没有人受伤，镇公所赔了菜农六克朗，又给Josef两克朗车马费，请他尽快搭下一班车离开。Josef Hofer在站台上说，这也算一种巡回演讲。
  _effects: cash 2 (镇公所给的车马费); state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.legitimacy 8 -> 2; state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.notoriety 31 -> 36; reputation -0.020000000000000018; condition; flag +flooded_a_cellar_on_tour; mood -0.019999999999999685; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-81_

  又过了一年，那几张纸仍锁在Josef的抽屉里。街上的问话没有变多，也没有变少；Josef在咖啡馆里数了数还能信任的人，发现名单比去年短，但每一个名字都还站得住。
  _effects: state_parameter p21-scandal-web.exposure 63 -> 60; state_parameter p21-scandal-web.loyalty 31 -> 35; state_parameter p21-scandal-web.danger 56 -> 53; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1937.01** `state_tick:bc18-ridiculous-fame/ridiculous-fame-year/the-story-holds-another-year` tags: bc18-ridiculous-fame, fame, reputation, mistaken_identity, promotion, media, absurd, public
  又一年过去，Josef仍在被介绍成那个人物。邀请函从市郊的园艺会、女子学校和退伍军人酒馆寄来；Josef学会在签名后加一句模糊的谦辞，听起来像有许多往事不便细说。每一次应付都带来一点小钱，也让真正的解释更不合时宜。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.notoriety 36 -> 43; state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.invitations 0 -> 1; cash 1 (一场小型演讲的车马费); reputation 0.010000000000000009; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1937.01** `state_tick:bc18-bureaucratic-ghost/paper-trail-year/another-window-opens` tags: bc18-bureaucratic-ghost, bureaucracy, identity, paperwork, death, mistake, legal, absurd
  一个税务员把Josef的死亡记录和一张新房租收据订在一起，寄到了另一个区。三个星期后，Josef收到一封要求“已故纳税人”补缴暖气税的信；去问时，窗口说档案正在路上，让Josef先等通知。通知没有来，房租却涨了。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.fileDepth 25 -> 32; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.risk 20 -> 30; cash -1 (跑窗口的车钱); flag +heating_tax_for_the_dead; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1937.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.sunday-hill/p9-social-daily-culture.sunday-hill.rain-turn-back` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:daily
  走到半路雨点砸下来，山道立刻变得溜滑。Josef和Anna Bauer躲进路边的酒棚檐下，商量了几句，决定改乘电车回家，车票六十赫勒。Josef把带去的面包掰开分给同车躲雨的人，自己站在车尾，湿透的裤脚贴着腿，一路打着哆嗦；远足没走完，倒记住了一车陌生人的谢意。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (改乘电车回家的车票); food; mood -0.010000000000000009; relation friend with person-anna-bauer; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-56_

- **1937.03** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.window-argument/window-argument.peek` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, neighbor, overheard
  Josef下楼倒水，在楼梯口看见院子里站着一对夫妻，男人的围裙上还沾着面粉，女的攥着一张工钱条。她没插话，提着壶又上楼，关门时听见那女人说“一星期八克朗怎么够”。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; skill_

- **1937.05** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/compromise_split_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Josef把洗好的床单搭上院子里的公用晾衣绳，占了整条。Anna Bauer 3抱着一盆湿衣服下来，说自家孩子明天要穿干净衬衫上学。两人在绳子两头扯了一阵，最后Josef把床单对折，腾出一半，Anna Bauer 3用木夹子把衬衫一件件排上去。谁也没道歉，但第二天见面都点了头。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-38; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-38_

- **1937.12** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/sweat-it-out` tags: health, medical, family, money, injury
  醋水的酸味混着汗气，屋子小得转不开身。Friedrich Weiss把布拧到不滴水，敷上去时Josef打了个激灵。窗外送奶车吱呀碾过石板，她才想起自己家的炉子还灭着。
  _effects: health -0.04 (高烧硬熬伤身); condition; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23_

  Josef在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Josef把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.05 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages); condition_

  _effects: state_remove bc18-ridiculous-fame state:world-vienna-1912:423; flag -ridiculously_famous_

- **1938.01** `state_tick:bc18-bureaucratic-ghost/paper-trail-year/kept-the-files-apart` tags: bc18-bureaucratic-ghost, bureaucracy, identity, paperwork, death, mistake, legal, absurd
  这一年，Josef学会了同时带三张纸出门：一张证明活着，一张证明死了，一张证明自己从没搬过家。窗口后面的人各自只看自己那一张，偶尔还会夸Josef的材料齐全。年底，Josef从补发的配给里省下一小袋面粉，觉得自己像个熟练的走私犯。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.fileDepth 32 -> 36; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.risk 30 -> 25; cash 1 (省下的配给和车钱); mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1938.01** `r13-civ.public-health-campaign::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/posters-pasted` tags: health, public_health, campaign, poster, institution, vienna, urban, crisis
  Magistrat der k. k. Reichshaupt- und Residenzstadt Wien的办事员把一叠防疫告示交给Josef，说浆糊要刷匀，别让风揭了角。Josef沿着市场街贴到天黑，手指被纸边割出细口。第二天，告示下面多了一行铅笔字：“水要煮开”。
  _effects: reputation 0.06; flag +pasted_public_health_notices; role; skill; knowledge “the notice was answered with a handwritten instruction to bo”_

- **1938.02** `bc18-crime.inept-blackmail::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/mourning-card` tags: crime, blackmail, photograph, mistake, humiliation, absurd, reputation, vienna
  Luise Moser在葬礼上偷拍了一张Josef靠着棺材睡着的照片，说只要不给四克朗，就把照片寄给Josef的老板。Josef还没有凑钱，照片先被家里人选去做了悼念卡；亲戚们说，只有真正伤心的人才会累成这样。Luise Moser拿着底片站在门口，第一次不知道该威胁谁。
  _effects: cash 3 (相馆支付的悼念卡照片使用费); reputation 0.02999999999999997; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2; condition; flag +blackmail_photo_became_mourning_card; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2_

- **1938.02** `state_reaction:wish-craft/craft-humiliation` tags: wish-craft, wish, wish:craft, craft, work, mastery, state, state_reaction
  Josef把那句轻视记了很久。它没有变成一个决定，但也没有消失。
  _effects: mood -0.08000000000000007_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:007981_

- **1938.10** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.river-walk/river-walk.bridge` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, walk, river
  Josef在桥边站了一会儿，看一艘平底船靠岸卸煤，几个工人用铁锹往岸上甩。风把煤灰吹到她脸上，她抹了一把，往回走时脚步比来时轻，回到巷口还给自己买了块三赫勒的糖。
  _effects: mood 0.06000000000000005; cash -0.03 (买了三赫勒的糖)_

- **1939.01** `vienna-life-anschluss-1938/resists` tags: politics, persecution, 1938
  Josef在登记桌旁说了句“这里以前不是这样”。Friedrich Hofer记下地址，当晚两个穿制服的人来敲门，把Josef带到警局，第二天放回来时脸上有伤。
  _effects: condition; role; cash -5 (fine and lost work)_

- **1939.01** `hs18-occupation.piano::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/refuse-to-sign` tags: occupation, piano, requisition, witness, neighbor, history, vienna, urban
  Josef把笔推回去，说钢琴的主人就住在隔壁。Friedrich Hofer没有争，只让工人把琴盖也搬走，又记下Josef的门牌号。卡车开走时，Luise Berg家的窗子开了又关；Josef站在空出来的门洞前，听见自己的呼吸声。
  _effects: state_add hs18-street-witness state:world-vienna-1912:514; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001143:child; reputation 0.020000000000000018; flag +refused_piano_receipt; mood -0.010000000000000009_

  Josef的「档案里的活鬼」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove bc18-bureaucratic-ghost state:world-vienna-1912:458; flag -bureaucratic_ghost_

- **1939.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.last-standing-ticket/p9-social-daily-culture.last-standing-ticket.got-ticket` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:culture
  Josef挤到窗口前，把一克朗硬币推进凹槽，换回一张印着座号的薄纸片。顶层站席的栏杆被前面的人汗湿，Josef侧身找了个能看见舞台边的位置，倚着烫手的铁栏看完两幕；散场时小腿发麻，嘴里还哼着刚听到的曲调，幕间也和邻座搭上了两句话。
  _effects: cash -1 (约瑟夫施塔特剧院顶层站票); mood 0.06000000000000005; skill_

- **1939.03** `vienna-adult-social.burial-fund/burial-fund.paid` tags: adulthood, social, work, family
  车间里管互助会的Otto Stein在午休时把本子摊在Josef的工具箱上，说这个月还差最后一份月钱。Josef从马甲内袋摸出二十个克罗伊策，Otto Stein当着他的面在本子上画了钩。
  _effects: cash -0.3 (缴纳丧葬互助会的月钱); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-27; reputation 0.03999999999999998_

- **1939.06** `ad20-public-speech::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/speech-lands` tags: speech, public, reputation, everyday, fame, scandal, vienna, urban
  礼堂里坐了一半人，前排还有几个空位。Josef讲到第三段时，后排有人开始点头；讲到第五段，连门口卖票的也转过身来。散场后，Friedrich Hofer握着Josef的手说“下次还请你”，手心里全是汗。
  _effects: state_add ad20-public-attention state:world-vienna-1912:529; reputation 0.03999999999999998; skill; relation acquaintance with person-friedrich-hofer; flag +public_speech_landed; mood 0.040000000000000036; flag +public_attention_active_

- **1939.06** `state_reaction:ad20-public-attention/a-scandal-sticks` tags: ad20-public-attention, public, attention, reputation, media, everyday, risk, opportunity
  旧闻被重新排上版面，标题比上次短，意思却更重。Josef没有买那份报纸，但整条街都知道了标题。
  _effects: state_parameter ad20-public-attention.attention 55 -> 65; state_parameter ad20-public-attention.danger 15 -> 23; state_parameter ad20-public-attention.sympathy 50 -> 45; mood -0.030000000000000027_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:008380_

- **1939.07** `vienna-historical-social-movement.district-list/demand-open-books` tags: history, historical_process, social_movement, organization, politics
  Josef把账本摊在灯下，一页一页念出日期和克朗数，念到买纸那笔时有人 From 门口咳嗽。Anna Moser 2低头拨弄围巾穗子，没有替谁说话。桌子上的旧墨水瓶被碰倒，蓝墨慢慢洇进木纹。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44; reputation 0.03999999999999998; skill_

- **1940.01** `hs18-witness.reporter::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/sell-the-account` tags: witness, reporter, memory, money, risk, history, vienna, urban
  Mitzi Berg把几张钞票压在咖啡杯底下，说只要一段能上版的细节。Josef讲了钢琴、门洞和那件湿外套；对方记得很快，临走前问能不能用真名。Josef说用化名，记者笑了一下，说化名也得像真的。
  _effects: cash 4 (sold a witness account to a reporter); state_parameter hs18-street-witness.retellings 0 -> 2; state_parameter hs18-street-witness.danger 60 -> 75; state_parameter hs18-street-witness.certainty 90 -> 93; reputation 0.020000000000000018; flag +sold_witness_account_

- **1940.01** `state_tick:hs18-street-witness/memory-drift/blurs` tags: hs18-street-witness, witness, memory, history, testimony, rumor, risk, street
  Josef忽然想不起那个人先迈的是左脚还是右脚。邻居说自己记得清清楚楚，还补了一个细节；Josef听着，竟分不清那是自己看见的，还是后来听来的。
  _effects: state_parameter hs18-street-witness.certainty 93 -> 85; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1940.01** `state_tick:ad20-public-attention/the-attention-cycle/cools` tags: ad20-public-attention, public, attention, reputation, media, everyday, risk, opportunity
  Josef发现最近没有人再提那件事。报纸换了头版，邻居也换了话题；本该轻松，Josef却在买菜时反复整理衣领，好像还在等一个不会来的目光。
  _effects: state_parameter ad20-public-attention.attention 65 -> 57; state_parameter ad20-public-attention.sympathy 45 -> 42; state_parameter ad20-public-attention.opportunity 45 -> 43; mood -0.010000000000000009_

  Josef又把那件没实现的事翻出来想了一遍；这一次没有告诉任何人。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1940.01** `r13-work.reference-letter::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/letter-written` tags: work, reference, hiring, favour, reputation, vienna, urban, crisis
  Otto Winter在门房里听完，从抽屉里摸出一张印着厂名的信纸，写Josef“手脚干净，肯学机器”，又签了名。墨水没干，他用吸墨纸按了按，说别拿去给太大的厂，小厂才看这个。Josef把信折成四折，放进内袋最里面。
  _effects: cash 1 (旧工头的一封荐书); flag +work13_obtained_reference_letter; relation friend with person-otto-winter; knowledge “the old foreman still has letterhead from the factory”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-49_

- **1940.03** `vienna-parish-aid.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.help-wash` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Josef喝完汤没走，留下来帮修女把大锅刷净，又把木碗按大小摞好。厨房后头有半块面包和一碗剩汤，修女说是帮忙人的午饭。Josef Winter在门口等着一起回去，说下回还来，洗锅的活总有人要干。
  _effects: food; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-104; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-104_

- **1940.05** `vienna-social-movement.recruitment/join-and-carry` tags: development, social_movement, organization, politics
  Josef在楼梯下面接到一张折成四折的传单。纸上没有署名，只写着下一次集会的地址。Josef去了，在昏暗的屋里报上姓名，成为Sozialdemokratische Arbeiterpartei Österreichs (SDAP)的一员，并答应负责自己所在街区的联络。
  _effects: organization_membership; role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-6_

- **1940.09** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.gramophone-wall/gramophone-wall.cotton-in-ears` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Josef忍到天亮，第二天去药房买了五分钱的棉花，撕成两小团塞在耳里。夜里的进行曲还剩一点闷响，翻个身也就过去了。此后在楼梯间遇见Gustav Bauer只是点头，再没有进过那家的门。
  _effects: cash -0.05 (在药房买棉花塞耳朵); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-22; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-22_

  药粉含在嘴里发苦，Josef就着糖浆咽下去，喉头一阵凉。医生拎包出门时，楼梯间的煤气灯刚好灭了，Gustav Lechner摸黑送他到拐角，回来时手里攥着找回的几枚赫勒。
  _effects: cash -4 (医生出诊费和药费); health 0.05 (药粉和糖浆缓解症状); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-31_

- **1941.01** `r14-soc.club-election::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/won-by-three-votes` tags: task, club_election, club, status, community, vienna, urban, crisis
  合唱团在酒馆后厅改选，票箱是一只装饼干的铁盒。唱票唱到第三遍，Josef比Katharina Winter 2多三票。Katharina Winter 2把账本推过来，说“你接”。散会后，两人在门口抽完同一支烟，谁也没有先提那三票。账本第一页记着上次义卖的收入，数字旁边有一道被擦过的铅笔痕。
  _effects: mood 0.12; reputation 0.08000000000000002; flag +won_club_election; role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41_

- **1941.03** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.christmas-soup/serve-first-talk-later` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Josef先给Emil盛了满满一碗，扁豆和面团结结实实压在勺底，那块熏肉切开分了两份，锅里的吃食折合两克朗。药费的事一句没提，只问要不要再添点汤。Emil吃到一半才开口，说上次说话太冲；桌边多摆的那把椅子，最后有人坐下了。
  _effects: food; relation kin with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912; mood 0.050000000000000044_

- **1941.06** `vienna-life-overcrowded-flat/take-in-a-lodger` tags: housing, home, health, family
  Josef把厨房角落隔出一块，租给一个在附近上工的学徒。每月多四克朗，但煤、水和厕所都要排队；夜里有人咳嗽，Josef睡不踏实，第二天眼下发青，孩子也在床上翻来覆去，四克朗换来一屋子醒着的人。
  _effects: cash 4 (lodger rent); condition_

- **1941.07** `vienna-medical-treatment.sanatorium/ignore-diagnosis` tags: health, medical, family, money
  下午的活是搬纱锭，Josef蹲下去再站起来眼前发黑，扶墙站了一会儿。工友老约瑟夫把自己的水杯推过来，说去诊所开个假条吧，她摇头，说假条要扣钱。
  _effects: condition; mood -0.040000000000000036_

- **1941.10** `vienna-housing-move.moving-day/moving-day-cart` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money, household
  车夫把价钱咬得很死，Josef还到五克朗又添了二十赫勒，才说定跑一趟。Franz Huber 2扶着床架，车过路口时煤箱歪了一下，细灰撒在路面上。到了地方，东西卸在门厅，天已经大亮。
  _effects: cash -6 (雇马车搬家具一趟的工钱); residence; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33_

  Josef又把那天的顺序在脑子里走了一遍：先是门响，再是靴子，最后才是那声喊。细节还在，只是每讲一次，旁边就多出一点别人替你补上的颜色。
  _effects: state_parameter hs18-street-witness.certainty 77 -> 79_

- **1942.01** `state_tick:ad20-public-attention/the-attention-cycle/warms` tags: ad20-public-attention, public, attention, reputation, media, everyday, risk, opportunity

  _effects: state_parameter ad20-public-attention.attention 49 -> 45; state_parameter ad20-public-attention.sympathy 39 -> 45; state_parameter ad20-public-attention.opportunity 41 -> 45; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  _effects: mood -0.05999999999999994; flag +seeking_work; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-117; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-117_

  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44_

- **1942.10** `sm18-neighbor.night-light::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/watches-until-the-blind-moves` tags: neighbor, secret, night, window, suspicion, everyday, vienna, urban
  Josef连续三夜看见Karl Kraus家的地下室亮着灯，窗纸上有人影来回走，却从不见人从前门出来。第四夜，Josef提着煤桶假装去地窖，听见里面有另一个人的咳嗽声。
  _effects: state_add sm18-town-secret state:world-vienna-1912:586; knowledge “邻居家的地下室藏着一个没有登记的人，夜里有人影和咳嗽声”; mood -0.040000000000000036; flag +saw_the_neighbors_cellar_light; flag +carries_a_neighborhood_secret_

  _effects: flag +old_enemy_alliance; cash 2 (an advance on the joint commission); knowledge “借据背面还留着当年两个人的签名，墨迹已经发灰。”; mood 0.010000000000000009_
