  Josef本来只想说一句“那家人最近不太容易”，话到嘴边却带出了地下室。听的人没有追问，只把杯子放下；第二天，楼道里多了几道从门口扫到楼梯口的目光。
  _effects: state_parameter sm18-town-secret.pressure 35 -> 42; state_parameter sm18-town-secret.danger 25 -> 33; reputation -0.01999999999999999; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1943.01** `r13-fam.collect-funeral-money::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/collection-short` tags: task, social, funeral, neighbor, charity, grief, vienna, urban
  走到第四家，Josef的帽子还是轻的。有人说明天再给，有人说自家也刚办过丧事。Josef把帽子里的钱倒在Katharina Winter 2的桌上，数了两遍，还差一口棺材的钱；Katharina Winter 2说算了，用旧门板钉一个也行。
  _effects: cash -1 (端着一顶帽子走楼道); flag +funeral_collection_short; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41; knowledge “a plain coffin costs more than the building can raise”; condition_

- **1943.04** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/dentist-pull` tags: health, medical, family, money
  诊室在二楼，楼梯间飘着石炭酸味。医生把钳子举到灯下看了看，叫Josef攥紧扶手。Klara站在门边数墙上的价目表：拔牙两克朗五十赫勒，药棉五十赫勒，连消炎水另算。
  _effects: cash -3 (牙医拔牙费用); health 0.05999999999999999 (拔除病牙解除疼痛); mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1943.05** `major-crisis.rent-increase/tenant-meeting` tags: crisis, housing, rent, eviction, family, poverty
  巷口洗衣房的老板娘把几张椅子摆开，叫来半条街的租户。Josef把涨租单摊在桌上，逐条念出上面的数字；Klara站在门边，既没走，也没替房东说话。大家决定先集体迟交一个月，签名纸传到Josef手里时，纸上已经有一块油渍。
  _effects: cash -2 (paper, ink and tram fare for the tenants meeting); role; flag +rent_strike; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912; reputation 0.030000000000000027_

- **1943.09** `curse15-betrayal.ledger-share::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-witness-stays-quiet` tags: betrayal, money, family, trust, curse, secret, vienna, urban
  Anna Moser 2在办事处说了Josef一整个下午的坏话，从欠债说到那场没人看见的火灾。旁边的人没有替Josef说一句话。几天后，Josef发现自己的名字从互助会的名单上被划掉了，Anna Moser 2只说“我也要养家”。
  _effects: reputation -0.08000000000000002; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44; cash -1 (互助会停止发放的补贴); condition; flag +reputation_damaged_by_betrayal_

- **1944.01** `sm18-secret.market-rumor::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-rumor-comes-back-wrong` tags: secret, rumor, market, neighbor, reputation, consequence, vienna, urban
  Josef只在菜摊前说了半句“那家人地下室里”，第二天整条街都在传Friedrich Berg杀了人。Helene Huber一边称土豆一边问尸体埋在哪；Josef说不是这么回事，Helene Huber已经把这话当成了新的版本。
  _effects: state_parameter sm18-town-secret.pressure 42 -> 54; state_parameter sm18-town-secret.danger 33 -> 43; reputation -0.03; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71; knowledge “街上正在传邻居杀了人，这不是真的”; flag +started_a_false_rumor; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71_

- **1944.01** `state_tick:hs18-street-witness/memory-drift/blurs` tags: hs18-street-witness, witness, memory, history, testimony, rumor, risk, street
  Josef忽然想不起那个人先迈的是左脚还是右脚。邻居说自己记得清清楚楚，还补了一个细节；Josef听着，竟分不清那是自己看见的，还是后来听来的。
  _effects: state_parameter hs18-street-witness.certainty 80 -> 72; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1944.01** `state_tick:sm18-town-secret/the-secret-keeps/held-behind-the-teeth` tags: sm18-town-secret, secret, neighborhood, rumor, knowledge, risk, obligation, mystery
  半年里，Josef在楼梯口三次遇见那件不该提的事，每次都把话咽回去。秘密没有变轻，只是被放到了一个更深的抽屉里；Josef开始记得自己每一次沉默是在哪一层楼梯。
  _effects: state_parameter sm18-town-secret.pressure 54 -> 51; state_parameter sm18-town-secret.proof 55 -> 57; mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1944.01** `state_tick:wish-craft/craft-pressure/press-on` tags: wish-craft, wish, wish:craft, craft, work, mastery, state, state_tick
  Josef又把那件没实现的事翻出来想了一遍；这一次没有告诉任何人。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1944.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.sunday-hill/p9-social-daily-culture.sunday-hill.rain-turn-back` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:daily
  走到半路雨点砸下来，山道立刻变得溜滑。Josef和Otto Stein躲进路边的酒棚檐下，商量了几句，决定改乘电车回家，车票六十赫勒。Josef把带去的面包掰开分给同车躲雨的人，自己站在车尾，湿透的裤脚贴着腿，一路打着哆嗦；远足没走完，倒记住了一车陌生人的谢意。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (改乘电车回家的车票); food; health -0.03 (淋雨后穿着湿衣服乘车); mood -0.020000000000000018; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-27_

- **1944.10** `bc18-crime.inept-blackmail::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/mourning-card` tags: crime, blackmail, photograph, mistake, humiliation, absurd, reputation, vienna
  Rosa 2在葬礼上偷拍了一张Josef靠着棺材睡着的照片，说只要不给四克朗，就把照片寄给Josef的老板。Josef还没有凑钱，照片先被家里人选去做了悼念卡；亲戚们说，只有真正伤心的人才会累成这样。Rosa 2拿着底片站在门口，第一次不知道该威胁谁。
  _effects: cash 3 (相馆支付的悼念卡照片使用费); reputation 0.030000000000000027; relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002007:child; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1944.10** `vienna-legacy-worklife.union-office/union-office.post-ledger` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  窗外是维也纳第十五区的一条窄街，卖烤栗子的炉子冒着白烟。Josef贴完纸退后两步看，发现“欠”字被浆糊洇开了一点。老成员没说话，只把眼镜摘下来擦了又擦。
  _effects: reputation 0.04999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912; flag +union_ledger_public; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-128_

- **1945.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/wound-infection` tags: health, work, family
  Josef的伤口在几天后肿起来，手腕发热。医生说要切开引流，费用比上次更高；Josef看着账单，先问能不能分两次付。
  _effects: cash -14 (drainage and medicine); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:010244_

- **1945.01** `vienna-life-1945-liberation/survives` tags: war, liberation, 1945
  苏联士兵进城时，Josef躲在门后听见坦克履带压过石板路。几天后街口贴出告示，Josef去领了一份汤和一块面包。
  _effects: role; food; cash -2 (replacement documents)_

- **1945.01** `sm18-secret.protect-or-tell::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/lets-them-pass` tags: secret, neighbor, stairwell, choice, risk, consequence, vienna, urban
  Josef在楼梯间撞见第三个人：那人提着一只旧皮箱，见到Josef就把帽檐压低。Katharina Winter从门后出来，手还按在门把上。三个人站了几秒钟，谁也没有先说话；最后Josef侧过身，让他们过去。
  _effects: state_parameter sm18-town-secret.pressure 51 -> 61; state_parameter sm18-town-secret.danger 43 -> 48; state_parameter sm18-town-secret.proof 57 -> 62; flag +saw_the_third_person_on_the_stairs_

- **1945.01** `state_tick:hs18-street-witness/memory-drift/holds` tags: hs18-street-witness, witness, memory, history, testimony, rumor, risk, street
  Josef又把那天的顺序在脑子里走了一遍：先是门响，再是靴子，最后才是那声喊。细节还在，只是每讲一次，旁边就多出一点别人替你补上的颜色。
  _effects: state_parameter hs18-street-witness.certainty 72 -> 74_

- **1945.01** `state_tick:sm18-town-secret/the-secret-keeps/held-behind-the-teeth` tags: sm18-town-secret, secret, neighborhood, rumor, knowledge, risk, obligation, mystery
  半年里，Josef在楼梯口三次遇见那件不该提的事，每次都把话咽回去。秘密没有变轻，只是被放到了一个更深的抽屉里；Josef开始记得自己每一次沉默是在哪一层楼梯。
  _effects: state_parameter sm18-town-secret.pressure 61 -> 58; state_parameter sm18-town-secret.proof 62 -> 64; mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1945.01** `p10-family-tasks.arrange-childcare/arrange-childcare.neighbor` tags: adulthood, family, social, marriage, care, inheritance, task, childcare
  老太太接过孩子，用旧围裙兜着坐在窗边，说一克朗一周，六点送来六点接。Josef数出硬币，把奶瓶放在她灶台边。第三夜孩子还是哭醒，Josef抱着他在屋里走，天亮前靠着床沿打了个盹。
  _effects: cash -1 (每周付邻居老太太的照看费); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33_

- **1945.01** `vienna-life-overcrowded-flat/move-to-shared-tenement` tags: housing, home, health, family, household
  Josef把床架拆开，用绳子捆在借来的手推车上，搬进利奥波德城的合租房。押金和车钱花掉八克朗；新住处人多、墙薄，但至少每个人有一块能躺下的地方，Josef的腰在冷风里疼了一路，夜里却第一次不用侧着身睡。
  _effects: residence; cash -8 (deposit and cart)_

- **1945.02** `vienna-legacy-worklife.hours-ledger/hours-ledger.refuse` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  工头把表折成四折塞进围裙口袋，说“你会后悔的”。Josef当天下午去账房窗口排队，窗上的铁栅栏有股锈味。账房先生翻了半天蓝皮本子，最后从抽屉里数出八克朗硬币，一枚一枚推过台面。
  _effects: cash 8 (追回的拖欠计件工资); reputation -0.02999999999999997; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-133; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-133_

- **1945.05** `p9-gap-adult-unemployed.moving-job/moving-finished-paid` tags: adulthood, unemployment, casual_labour, poverty, daily, moving, neighbor
  楼梯窄得只容一个人侧身，Josef和Grete Huber一前一后抬着柜子，Franz Huber在楼下喊当心磕墙。床架拆成两截，捆在手推车上推到城外新住处，回来时天已经擦黑。
  _effects: cash 3 (帮鞋匠搬家的工钱)_

- **1945.12** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/corner-doctor` tags: health, medical, family, money
  施特劳斯医生用酒精棉擦手，把药粉倒进小纸袋，折口时指甲掐出印子。他说夜里别再喝醋水，那东西烧胃。Anton Winter把炉火拨旺，在灶上坐了一壶水，答应第二天替Josef去铺子里告假。
  _effects: cash -4 (医生出诊费和药费); health 0.05 (药粉和糖浆缓解症状); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-20_

  Josef又把那天的顺序在脑子里走了一遍：先是门响，再是靴子，最后才是那声喊。细节还在，只是每讲一次，旁边就多出一点别人替你补上的颜色。

  Josef本来只想说一句“那家人最近不太容易”，话到嘴边却带出了地下室。听的人没有追问，只把杯子放下；第二天，楼道里多了几道从门口扫到楼梯口的目光。

- **1946.01** `r13-civ.recover-at-home::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/neighbor-soup` tags: health, recovery, care, household, food, vienna, urban, postwar
  Alois Moser 2端来一碗兑了水的牛奶，顺手把Josef家的炉子捅旺。Josef靠在床头，听Alois Moser 2讲楼下谁也被传染了，谁家把病人送去了医院。人走后，屋里还留着一点煤烟和奶味。
  _effects: cash -1 (退烧后的第一碗粥); health 0.04 (退烧后的第一碗粥); flag +recovered_with_neighbor_help; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-86; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-86_

- **1946.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_no_damage` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Josef刚把锅端下灶，火苗就蹿了起来，Anton Bauer 2一脚踩灭地上的油布，顺手抄起沙箱里的沙子盖住灶口。两人都没受伤，但Anton Bauer 2的鞋底烧穿一个洞，那是唯一一双出门的鞋。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-66; mood -0.05; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-66_

- **1946.07** `vienna-legacy-worklife.sick-fund/sick-fund.fill-form` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  衙门走廊的地砖是黑白相间的，Josef站着站着就把重心从一只脚换到另一只脚。窗口里的办事员姓什么没人知道，只叫“三号窗”。表递进去时，里面说“等复核”，声音很平。
  _effects: relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-126; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-126_

  Helene Lechner趁Josef不在，把租约上的名字改成了自己。Josef回家时，门口多了一只不属于自己的箱子；Helene Lechner站在门里，说房东已经同意。
  _effects: relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002003:child; flag +home_taken_by_betrayal; state_add grief state:world-vienna-1912:675_

  Emil在咖啡馆角落坐下，要了两杯最便宜的咖啡，说地下室那个人是他妻子的弟弟，战后一直没有证件。Josef问为什么不去登记；Emil把杯子转了一圈，说登记表上有一栏叫“原籍”，他填不出来。

- **1947.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.pictures-or-concert/p9-social-daily-culture.pictures-or-concert.skip-both` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:culture
  Josef在影戏棚和音乐厅之间走了两趟，最后哪处都没进。Josef拐进常去的酒馆，花三十赫勒要了半升啤酒，听角落里有人拉琴，拉得不准但很响。Maria Lechner找到Josef时，桌上的杯子已经空了一半，省下的票钱还在口袋里叮当响；这一晚没看成戏，倒把半升啤酒喝出了几分自在。
  _effects: cash -0.3 (酒馆半升啤酒); mood 0.030000000000000027; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000659:child_

  Josef拎着鱼上楼，看见Grete Moser 2蹲在门口用刀刮锅底。Josef把鱼放在两家之间的窗台上，切了一半推过去。第二天门口多了一小袋面粉。
  _effects: cash -6 (买鱼); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-111; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-111_

  提问时间一到，Josef站起来，把请愿书上的三条逐条读出：房租上限、成年男子普选、十小时工时。Emil Kraus在台上让记录员一条条记下，礼堂里有人跟着低声重复那三个数目。
  _effects: reputation 0.04999999999999999; knowledge “市政厅旁的会议上，官员让记录员把三条请愿要求记进了正式记录”_

- **1948.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/compromise_split_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Josef把洗好的床单搭上院子里的公用晾衣绳，占了整条。Anton Bauer 2抱着一盆湿衣服下来，说自家孩子明天要穿干净衬衫上学。两人在绳子两头扯了一阵，最后Josef把床单对折，腾出一半，Anton Bauer 2用木夹子把衬衫一件件排上去。谁也没道歉，但第二天见面都点了头。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-66; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:011477_

  Josef在整理旧衣服时突然停下来。那天剩下的活没有做完。

- **1948.01** `state_tick:wish-craft/craft-pressure/press-on` tags: wish-craft, wish, wish:craft, craft, work, mastery, state, state_tick
  Josef又把那件没实现的事翻出来想了一遍；这一次没有告诉任何人。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1948.01** `r13-work.open-shop::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement::postwar-vacant-shop/lease-dispute` tags: commerce, shop, capital, licence, risk, vienna, urban, postwar
  Otto Bauer 2先答应租，等Josef把货搬进去，又说得加一笔“门面修缮费”。Josef把口头约定复述了一遍，Otto Bauer 2说没有纸就不算。货堆在走廊里过了一夜，第二天Josef把最贵的两箱搬回去，剩下的折价卖给了对街的Mitzi Moser。
  _effects: cash -5 (卷帘门上的第一块木牌); flag +work13_shop_lease_dispute; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-128_

- **1948.01** `vienna-church-community.tenement-prayer/rent-prayer-help` tags: faith, social, family, home
  Josef在楼梯间守夜时，邻居说起楼下老太太病重。
  _effects: condition; food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13_

- **1948.05** `ml18-lottery.same-numbers::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-newsagent-kept-the-ticket` tags: lottery, luck, money, family, regret, windfall, vienna, urban
  开奖后，Alois Moser 2从柜台下面把票拿出来，说这张是用赊账买的，按老规矩中奖一人一半。Josef在店里站到天黑，最后拿了三十五克朗；Alois Moser 2把另一半锁进铁盒，顺手把柜台上的欠账本翻到Josef那一页，划掉了最后一笔。回家路上，Gustav Lechner说这比不中强，Josef没有说话。
  _effects: cash 35 (the subject's half of the disputed lottery win); state_add ml18-sudden-fortune state:world-vienna-1912:729; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-86; flag +ticket_held_on_credit; knowledge “烟草铺老板把赊账买的彩票扣在柜台下面，直到开奖才拿出来。”; mood 0.010000000000000009; flag +sudden_fortune; reputation 0.020000000000000018; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1949.01** `ml18-charity.neighborhood-clinic::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-committee-buys-a-wagon` tags: charity, money, clinic, reputation, community, warm, vienna, urban
  捐款交出去三个月，诊所还没有开门，先来了一辆新的弹簧马车和一张桃花心木办公桌。Emil Kraus把账本摊在桌上，说委员会认为“体面的门面”也是诊所的一部分。Mitzi Lechner在门口骂了一句，Josef把账本翻到最后一页，发现自己捐的钱只剩一半。
  _effects: cash -30 (the clinic endowment was spent on furniture and a wagon); state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.principal 30 -> 0; reputation -0.04999999999999999; relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000168:child; condition; flag +clinic_funds_misused; knowledge “诊所的捐款先买了一辆弹簧马车和一张桃花心木办公桌。”; mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-129_

- **1949.01** `state_reaction:sm18-town-secret/scandal-touches-it` tags: sm18-town-secret, secret, neighborhood, rumor, knowledge, risk, obligation, mystery
  街口有人家出了丑事，围观的人转到这条巷子时，Josef发现自己也站在门口。Josef没有说任何名字，但回家以后，还是把窗帘拉得比平时早。
  _effects: state_parameter sm18-town-secret.pressure 79 -> 91; state_parameter sm18-town-secret.danger 64 -> 69; mood -0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:012496_

- **1949.01** `state_tick:sm18-town-secret/the-secret-keeps/a-sentence-slips` tags: sm18-town-secret, secret, neighborhood, rumor, knowledge, risk, obligation, mystery
  Josef本来只想说一句“那家人最近不太容易”，话到嘴边却带出了地下室。听的人没有追问，只把杯子放下；第二天，楼道里多了几道从门口扫到楼梯口的目光。
  _effects: state_parameter sm18-town-secret.pressure 91 -> 98; state_parameter sm18-town-secret.danger 69 -> 77; reputation -0.020000000000000018; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1949.01** `state_tick:grief/anniversary/overwhelmed` tags: grief, death, family, mind, state, state_tick
  Josef在整理旧衣服时突然停下来。那天剩下的活没有做完。
  _effects: state_parameter grief.years 2 -> 3_

- **1949.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.lending-library/p9-social-daily-culture.lending-library.volksbibliothek` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:knowledge
  Josef听Anton Bauer 2报出押金数目，把伸进内袋的手又抽了回来。问清免费阅览处的位置后，Josef穿过两条街，在 Volksbibliothek 的阅览室门口登记了姓名，坐在长桌边读了一下午；一个赫勒没花，只是不能把书带回家，天黑前就得还回去。
  _effects: mood 0.030000000000000027; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-66_

- **1949.01** `vienna-parish-aid.funeral-society/funeral-society.pay` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Josef数出八赫勒交给Grete Lechner，看她在蓝皮本上找到自家门牌，把这一周的格子划掉。Grete Lechner说上个月隔壁巷子的人走了，公账出了棺材和一场小弥撒，剩下的钱记在册尾。楼梯口的灯很暗，Josef盯着自己的名字看了两遍才转身。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (一周丧葬互助会会费); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-52; flag +funeral_society_member; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-52_

- **1949.02** `major-crisis.rent-increase/pay-the-increase` tags: crisis, housing, rent, eviction, family, poverty
  房东没有露面，只让看门人Mitzi Lechner把补款单塞进门缝：每月要多十四克朗，先交十八克朗（本月十四加四克朗手续费）才肯谈续租。Josef把准备买冬煤的钱数了两遍，还是把钱交了，收据上写的是“自愿捐助”。Mitzi Lechner低声说，不签的人已经有两家搬走了，楼下的煤价牌又换了数字。
  _effects: cash -18 (advance payment demanded with the rent increase); flag +rent_increase_accepted_

- **1950.01** `sm18-neighbor.wall-noise::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/helps-carry-the-wood` tags: neighbor, secret, noise, misunderstanding, comic, everyday, vienna, urban
  Josef干脆过去帮忙扶木板，才知道Helene Lechner在做一只带抽屉的小柜子，抽屉里放的是给孙女攒的硬币。两个人做到半夜，墙那边的锤声变成了两个人的。临走时，Helene Lechner塞给Josef一小袋钉子。
  _effects: relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002003:child; mood 0.050000000000000044; state_parameter sm18-town-secret.pressure 98 -> 90; skill; flag +helped_build_the_secret_dollhouse_

  Josef本来只想说一句“那家人最近不太容易”，话到嘴边却带出了地下室。听的人没有追问，只把杯子放下；第二天，楼道里多了几道从门口扫到楼梯口的目光。
  _effects: state_parameter sm18-town-secret.pressure 90 -> 97; state_parameter sm18-town-secret.danger 77 -> 85; reputation -0.020000000000000018; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1950.01** `state_expire:grief` tags: grief, death, family, mind, state, state_tick, state:grief
  Josef的「丧亲之痛」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove grief state:world-vienna-1912:675_

- **1950.01** `r13-fam.reconcile-sibling::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement::sibling-return-postwar/returned-brother` tags: task, family, sibling, estrangement, reconciliation, station, vienna, urban
  Anna Bauer从战俘营回来，瘦得军装空了一圈。Josef去车站接，两人先没有认出来；后来Anna Bauer叫了Josef小时候的绰号，Josef才把箱子接过来。回家路上，Anna Bauer说在营里最想的是家里的酸菜；Josef说今年腌得少，但还有一坛。
  _effects: cash -2 (车站长椅上的两个人); condition; flag +welcomed_returned_sibling; role; relation kin with person-anna-bauer; knowledge “the sibling dreamed of the family’s pickled cabbage in the c”_

- **1950.03** `vienna-legacy-worklife.union-office/union-office.enroll-first` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  来问入会的是个瘦高的年轻人，帽檐捏在手里，说自己在第十六区的织布厂做了半年。Josef把名册翻到空白页，蘸水笔尖在“住址”那栏停了一下，等他报出街名。老成员在角落里把账本合上，声音很轻。
  _effects: cash 1 (新会员的入会费先记在桌上); skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-144_

- **1950.11** `vienna-social-movement.recruitment/join-and-carry` tags: development, social_movement, organization, politics
  Josef在楼梯下面接到一张折成四折的传单。纸上没有署名，只写着下一次集会的地址。Josef去了，在昏暗的屋里报上姓名，成为第二区夜班出租合作社的一员，并答应负责自己所在街区的联络。
  _effects: organization_membership_

- **1951.01** `vienna-postwar-housing-1950s/stay-with-relatives` tags: adulthood, postwar, housing, family
  分房名单又往后推了一年，Josef一家搬进亲戚家的厨房隔间。Rosa Moser送来一块旧帘子当隔断；夜里有人起身，全屋都听得见。
  _effects: cash -3 (shared household costs); condition; mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9_

- **1951.01** `vienna-family-same-sex-male/private-arrangement` tags: family, intimacy, same_sex, persecution, lifecycle, partnership
  Josef和Friedrich Weiss 2在城边租了一间小屋，对外只说是同事合住。房租四克朗，两人不同时出门，信件用假名，夜里把窗帘拉严；Friedrich Weiss 2在门后放了一根木棍，Josef把两人的工票分开放，连咳嗽都压着声。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-155; mood 0.040000000000000036; cash -4 (separate room rent); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-155_

- **1951.01** `kernel-health.task-pharmacy-hunt::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/pharmacy-aspirin-substitute` tags: health, task, pharmacy, shortage, black_market, vienna, urban, postwar
  药房说没有货，Josef把处方叠好，去了下一家。天黑时手里只有一包阿司匹林和一瓶咳嗽糖浆；Grete Moser说总比空手强，把糖浆让给了孩子。回家后他把阿司匹林分成两半，糖浆瓶口用纸塞着，漏了一点。
  _effects: cash -8 (aspirin and cough syrup after a day of searching); condition; role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-103; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-103_

  Josef数出八赫勒交给Rosa Winter，看她在蓝皮本上找到自家门牌，把这一周的格子划掉。Rosa Winter说上个月隔壁巷子的人走了，公账出了棺材和一场小弥撒，剩下的钱记在册尾。楼梯口的灯很暗，Josef盯着自己的名字看了两遍才转身。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (一周丧葬互助会会费); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-12_

- **1951.09** `vienna-housing-move.sublet-room/sublet-daylight-only` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Josef和Friedrich Berg谈妥只出租白天，夜里不留宿，收两克朗。床仍归Josef家，钥匙只在白天给。Friedrich Berg说不碍事，他住的工棚不许带人，白天有个落脚的地方就够。Gustav Hofer听了点头，说这样少些闲话；到了傍晚，Josef把床铺重新收好，屋里照旧几口人。
  _effects: cash 2 (白天出租床位的钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1951.11** `vienna-adult-social.burial-fund/burial-fund.paid` tags: adulthood, social, work, family
  车间里管互助会的Josef Weiss在午休时把本子摊在Josef的工具箱上，说这个月还差最后一份月钱。Josef从马甲内袋摸出二十个克罗伊策，Josef Weiss当着他的面在本子上画了钩。
  _effects: cash -0.3 (缴纳丧葬互助会的月钱); reputation 0.03999999999999998_

- **1952.01** `sm18-object.tram-lost::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/accuses-the-conductor` tags: object, loss, child, tram, family, everyday, vienna, urban
  Josef认定是电车售票员把纸包收进了自己的口袋，在站台上拦住他，声音大得整条街都回头。售票员把票夹和口袋都翻出来，里面只有几张旧票和一只坏表。Franz Bauer站在后面，把脸埋进围巾里。
  _effects: state_add sm18-missing-object state:world-vienna-1912:799; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-116; reputation -0.030000000000000027; mood -0.040000000000000036; flag +accused_the_tram_conductor; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-116; flag +something_is_missing_

  Josef又把那件没实现的事翻出来想了一遍；这一次没有告诉任何人。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1952.01** `r13-work.repay-debt::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/asked-extension` tags: commerce, debt, credit, obligation, household, vienna, urban, postwar
  Josef只凑出五克朗，剩下的请求宽限到月底。Helene Hofer 2把五克朗收下，在还款簿上添了一行小字：逾期按周加息。Helene Lechner没有说话，只把日期记在自己的烟盒上。回家的路上，Josef算了一遍利息，发现月底要还的比今天更多。
  _effects: cash -5 (还款簿上的红格子); flag +work13_debt_extension_granted; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-149; knowledge “the creditor charges weekly interest on late payments”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-149_

- **1952.01** `vienna-social-movement.meeting/speak-and-organize` tags: development, social_movement, organization, meeting
  Josef在后屋的会议上报出自己街区的名字：谁家缺煤，谁被房东催租，谁知道哪间印刷厂还在招人。Die Fackel把这些事记进一本旧账本，散会时每个人带走一叠更小的纸条。
  _effects: skill; reputation 0.020000000000000018_

- **1952.01** `vienna-legacy-worklife.hours-ledger/hours-ledger.sign` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  印泥是赭红色的，像干了的面包瓤。Josef按完手印，工头把表举到窗边吹干，阳光里那张纸透出指印的轮廓。楼下第十区的街面传来运煤车的铁皮声，星期六下午突然显得很宽。
  _effects: cash -1 (星期六下午减少的计件工钱); mood 0.050000000000000044; flag +accepted_hours_reform; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-99_

- **1952.09** `vienna-parish-aid.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.help-wash` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Josef喝完汤没走，留下来帮修女把大锅刷净，又把木碗按大小摞好。厨房后头有半块面包和一碗剩汤，修女说是帮忙人的午饭。Gustav Hofer 2在门口等着一起回去，说下回还来，洗锅的活总有人要干。
  _effects: food; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-129; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1952.11** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.misdelivered-letter/misdelivered-letter.upstairs` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, letter
  Josef拿着信上了四层楼，敲了两户都不是，门牌是七号还是十七号已看不清。第三户的老太太说那人去年就搬去法沃里滕了。她把信交回门房，门房在登记簿上写下一个赫勒的墨水钱。
  _effects: mood 0.030000000000000027; reputation 0.020000000000000018_

- **1953.01** `state_tick:sm18-missing-object/another-drawer/a-receipt-in-the-lining` tags: sm18-missing-object, mystery, object, loss, memory, search, suspicion, everyday
  半年过去，Josef又翻了一遍那只装旧纽扣的铁盒，在盒盖夹层里摸到一小片发黄的收据。上面的日期和那件东西最后一次出现的那天对得上，但字迹已经淡得只剩半个印章。
  _effects: state_parameter sm18-missing-object.leads 5 -> 11; state_parameter sm18-missing-object.suspicion 45 -> 43; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1953.01** `r13-civ.clinic-first-visit::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/chronic-cough` tags: health, clinic, queue, diagnosis, institution, vienna, urban, postwar
  Josef在诊所走廊的第三张长凳上坐到中午，膝上压着挂号纸和一块湿手帕。Otto Bauer 2把听诊器贴上来不到半分钟，说是潮气和烟，开了褐色止咳糖浆，又让下周再来。药费从当天的菜钱里扣走，回家时篮子里只剩半棵卷心菜。
  _effects: cash -3 (诊所走廊的第三张长凳); condition; flag +clinic_first_visit; knowledge “the clinic doctor called the cough chronic bronchitis”_

- **1953.04** `hs18-cinema.newsreel::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/leave-early` tags: cinema, newsreel, neighbor, recognition, media, history, vienna, urban
  Josef在第二本胶片放到一半时起身，摸黑从侧门出去。Franz Huber 3在门口抽烟，问是不是片子太旧。Josef说不是，是座位太硬；街上的雨刚停，海报被泡得只剩半张脸。
  _effects: mood -0.010000000000000009; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-117; flag +left_newsreel_early_

- **1953.05** `vienna-parish-aid.rent-box/rent-box.refuse` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Josef说这个月还能撑，房租差的那几克朗打算替人洗衣补上，请Luise Moser把篮子提回去给更困难的人家。Luise Moser在门口停了一会儿，把一小块肥皂留在窗台上，说不是救济，是教区多出来的。门关上后，屋里只剩煤箱底刮出的响声。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2; flag +parish_refused_aid_

- **1953.06** `vienna-medical-treatment.sanatorium/sanatorium-go` tags: health, medical, family, money
  疗养院的护士接过行李，把床号写在小黑板上。Josef躺下时听见走廊尽头有人在放唱片，咳嗽没那么紧了。安娜的信第三天到，说花浇过了，房东没来催租。
  _effects: cash -12 (疗养院车票和住宿押金); health 0.12000000000000001 (疗养院松林空气和正规休养); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-116_

- **1953.06** `vienna-legacy-worklife.hours-ledger/hours-ledger.sign` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  老工人凑近看那张表，说这纸比工资袋还薄。Josef把拇指蘸进印泥，按下去时听见工头说了句“下一个”。学徒在旁边数少掉的铜板，数到四十赫勒时不出声了。

- **1956.03** `vienna-housing-move.flat-viewing/flat-viewing-too-damp` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Josef在单间里站了一刻钟，脚底发潮，墙纸底下透出一股霉味。看门人说刷一层石灰就好，Hermann Hofer 3却在门口轻轻咳了一声，提醒这背面没有阳光。Josef把定金收回口袋，谢过看门人，宁可多付三克朗，也不愿把湿气带进夜里。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-158; flag +still_looking_for_room; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-158_

  _effects: skill; cash -1 (买了一张站票); flag +cast_refused_

- **1957.01** `ml18-family.fortune-split::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-sibling-takes-it` tags: family, money, windfall, inheritance, conflict, dark, vienna, urban
  晚饭后的第三天，Josef去邮局取钱，发现存折上少了三十克朗。Anna Bauer在母亲的旧照片前承认拿了钱，说这些年照顾母亲的情分总该折成数目。Gustav Weiss去叫了警察；Josef站在两个最熟悉的人中间，听见楼下有人把留声机开得很响。
  _effects: cash -30 (the sibling took thirty crowns from the savings book); state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.principal 35 -> 5; state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 8 -> 11; relation rival with person-anna-bauer; condition; flag +family_fortune_stolen; knowledge “存折上少了三十克朗，取款单上的签名是模仿的。”_

- **1957.01** `r13-fam.visit-grave::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/wrong-grave` tags: task, family, grave, mourning, neighbor, grief, vienna, urban
  两人在坟场里绕了半个钟头，才找到那块刻着名字的石头。Katharina Winter 2忽然说不是这一块，自己记得旁边有一棵小槐树。Josef陪她一排排找，天快黑时，守墓人过来说小槐树去年就砍了；Katharina Winter 2站在空地上，把花放在了脚边。
  _effects: condition; flag +could_not_find_the_grave; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41; knowledge “the old tree by the grave was cut down last year”_

- **1912.04** `vienna-life-infant-feeding/fed-cows-milk` tags: infancy, food, household
  家里有人从街角奶店带回一小罐牛奶，Rosa Lechner兑水煮开，用小勺喂给Rosa。罐底有一层灰，奶店老板说这已经是最好的。
  _effects: cash -3 (cow milk); condition_

- **1913.05** `vienna-life-toddler-accident/safe-in-the-doorway` tags: infancy, accident, family, danger
  Alois Lechner在最后一刻抓住了Rosa的后领。孩子悬在两级台阶之间，鞋底蹭着地，手里还攥着那只线轴。Alois Lechner把孩子放回地上，蹲下来看了很久，才说：“再跑，我就把门关上。”
  _effects: mood -0.01999999999999999; skill_

- **1913.05** `iw18-infancy.first-word::ctx-vienna-1912::milieu-tenement/the-dead-grandmothers-name` tags: infancy, first_word, memory, family, wonder, eerie, vienna, urban
  Rosa扶着桌沿站起来，指着门口的空处，清清楚楚叫出一个名字。屋里没人教过这个词；Alois Lechner手里的铝勺停在半空。那是去年冬天埋掉的老人的名字，窗外正好有送煤的车经过，铁轮压过石缝。
  _effects: mood -0.03; knowledge “孩子说出的第一个词，是去年死去的祖母的名字。”; flag +first_word_dead_name_

- **1914.01** `vienna-life-toddler-first-words/first-word-more` tags: infancy, family, first, comic
  Rosa把空碗推回Alois Lechner面前，清清楚楚地说了一个字：“还要。”Alois Lechner愣住，随后把锅底最后一点糊刮进碗里。孩子学会了用一句话让世界再转一圈。
  _effects: skill; mood 0.03_

- **1915.01** `vienna-life-child-first-friend/shared-toy` tags: childhood, friendship, family, comic
  Otto Lechner在楼道里滚一只铁环，Rosa站在门口看。Otto Lechner把铁环推过来，说“你推一次，我推一次”。两个人从楼梯口推到院子里，铁环倒下的声音把午睡的大人吵醒了一排。
  _effects: relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000089:child; skill; mood 0.05_

- **1915.12** `p9-childhood-household.name-day-gift/old-shoes` tags: childhood, family, household, home, siblings, illness, neighbors
  报纸窸窸窣窣打开，露出一双旧鞋。Rosa认出鞋帮上的补丁，是弟妹穿剩下的。Rosa把脚伸进去，脚后跟还空着一指，脚趾却挤在前头。Alois Lechner看了看，说垫层布就好了，起身去柜子里翻布头。
  _effects: mood -0.02; relation kin with person-alois-lechner_

- **1916.01** `vienna-life-child-family-legend/stolen-inheritance` tags: childhood, family, secret, story
  Alois Lechner说，家里本来有一件东西，后来被“更会说话的人”拿走了。Rosa问是什么，Alois Lechner只说是一块表，表盖里刻着姓。孩子记住了“表盖里刻着姓”，却没有记住那个姓。
  _effects: knowledge “家里有一块表盖里刻着姓的表，被一个更会说话的人拿走了。”; flag +family_legend_watch_

- **1917.07** `r14-soc.childhood-oath::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/shared-last-bread` tags: childhood_friendship, childhood_oath, poverty, loyalty, neighbor, rural, agriculture, small_town
  面包房后门的泔水桶边，Otto Lechner捡起半块没沾灰的黑面包，掰成两半。Rosa把大的那半推回去，说自己早上吃过；其实Rosa的胃从昨夜就在叫。两人蹲在台阶上吃完，约好谁也不许把这件事说出去。多年后Rosa仍记得，那半块面包边上有股煤灰味。
  _effects: cash -1 (砖墙根下的铜纽扣); mood 0.05; flag +shared_last_bread; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000089:child_

- **1917.09** `p9-childhood-household.fever-night/hospital-deposit` tags: childhood, family, household, home, siblings, illness, neighbors
  围巾从Rosa的头一直裹到脚，Rosa Lechner抱着人下楼，在电车踏板上差点滑倒。急诊窗口先收押金，Rosa Lechner把内袋里的票子一张张抹平，数出六克朗。Rosa躺在观察室的窄床上，头顶的灯管嗡嗡响，Rosa Lechner的手一直按着被角。
  _effects: cash -6 (医院急诊押金和电车票); health 0.08 (在医院接受处理); mood -0.04_

  两个孩子在院子两头跑，一个递，一个夹，床单在风里鼓起来。最后一条挂好，Gustav Berg从厨房拿出面包，先给了自家孩子，再掰一块给Rosa。Rosa把面包塞进围裙口袋，回家后放在家里人的碗边，说自己在外面吃过了。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-gustav-berg; skill_

- **1918.08** `kernel-social.first-meeting::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/first-meeting.returned-bread` tags: social, friendship, first_meeting, childhood, neighbor, everyday, rural, agriculture
  Rosa把家里省下的半块面包递给Otto Lechner，Otto Lechner看了看，推回来，说母亲不许拿别人的东西。两人在门口站到上课铃响，只交换了姓名；面包被Rosa揣回口袋，硬得像一块砖。此后见面点头，再没有一起走过楼梯。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000089:child; role; knowledge “楼上的孩子家里不许收别人的食物。”_

- **1919.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-sold-out-potatoes` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  木窗“咔”地合上，Rosa的布袋还敞着口。伙计隔着板缝说下星期再来。Rosa在墙根站了一会儿，把硬币排在掌心数了两遍，走到旁边土豆摊前，挑了几个小的，让摊主当面过秤。家里人晚上把钱和土豆倒在桌上，重新数了一遍。
  _effects: food; mood -0.03; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-22; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4_

- **1920.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.mind-sibling/sibling-shelter-missed-river` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, sibling, care
  巷口的喊声第二次传来时，Rosa正用膝盖压住木盆边沿，给Maria Lechner挡穿堂风。Maria Lechner在旧布底下翻了个身，Rosa立刻停住，等呼吸又匀了才敢动。Rosa Lechner推门进来，先看见盆里睡着的Maria Lechner，再看见Rosa冻红的脚背，把灶上留的汤热了热。
  _effects: mood -0.02; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000659:child; relation kin with person-rosa-lechner_

- **1920.02** `vienna-childhood-friends.courtyard-race/won-the-race-lost-a-button` tags: childhood, social, school, street
  晒衣绳下挂着湿床单，孩子们绕着跑，鞋底把水甩到墙上。Rosa在第三圈超过Josef Bauer，手先碰到墙根。两颗玻璃弹珠进了Rosa口袋，外套第二颗纽扣却崩进下水道。Josef Bauer从二楼窗口骂了一句，扔下一根别针。
  _effects: mood 0.05; relation acquaintance with person-josef-bauer_

- **1921.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.fetch-coal/coal-half-credit` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, coal, errand
  “月底一起结。”掌柜说完，把半筐煤推到柜台外。Rosa用胸口顶住筐沿往家挪，煤灰落进领口，手心被麻绳磨得发亮。Alois Lechner在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang门口等到人，先看了看筐底，再看了看Rosa的手，把漏掉的碎渣扫进簸箕。
  _effects: food; relation kin with person-alois-lechner_

- **1921.06** `vienna-childhood-street.market-errand/errand-lost-coin` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  菜摊前的地面是湿的。Rosa弯腰捡土豆时，一枚铜板从指缝里滑出去，顺着水沟格栅掉了下去。Rosa把剩下的钱摊在柜台上，让摊主帮忙数，还是少了三十克鲁泽。晚上Rosa Lechner把钱一枚枚排开，问了三遍怎么少的，Rosa每答一次，声音就低一点。
  _effects: cash -0.3 (铜板掉进下水道); relation kin with person-rosa-lechner; mood -0.06_

- **1922.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.mind-sibling/sibling-carried-afternoon` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, sibling, care
  Anna Berg哭得打嗝，手指一直朝井台方向指。Rosa蹲下把Anna Berg驮上背，沿着墙根走，数砖缝给Anna Berg听：一、二、三……数到第四圈，背上的呼吸才匀了。天黑时Rosa的胳膊麻得抬不过肩，Rosa Lechner回来接过孩子，Rosa扶着门框甩了好一会儿手。
  _effects: skill; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000604:child; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7_

- **1922.01** `vienna-childhood-friends.courtyard-race/fell-on-wet-stones` tags: childhood, social, school, street
  晒衣绳上的水一滴一滴落在石板上。Rosa跑到第二圈，鞋底一滑，膝盖先着地。Gustav Berg从屋里端出半盆清水，蹲下冲掉伤口里的沙，又用手帕扎紧，说今天别跑了。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-gustav-berg_

- **1923.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.fetch-coal/coal-refused-nuggets` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, coal, errand
  Rosa空手站在窗口，听掌柜说“叫你Alois Lechner来”，耳朵一直红到脖子。后墙的渣堆冻得硬，Rosa用鞋尖刨开表层，把煤核一颗颗捡进围裙。回家路上碰到同巷的孩子，Rosa把纸袋往身后藏，进门才交给Alois Lechner。
  _effects: mood -0.04; food; skill_

- **1923.03** `vienna-youth-threshold.sibling-departure/quarrel-who-leaves` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, transition
  车票只够一个人走，晚饭桌上话越说越大声。Franz Lechner说该自己走，Rosa说自己也快够岁数了。母亲把汤勺往锅沿一磕，屋里静下来，最后Rosa摔门出去在楼梯上坐了半宿，直到楼上有人喊冷。
  _effects: relation kin with person-franz-lechner; mood -0.07_

- **1924.01** `p9-childhood-apprenticeship.ask-for-trial/silent-follow` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship, labour, training, family, task, task:work
  推车的轮子碾过石板，Rosa隔着两步跟在后面，一直跟到装订铺后门。Luise Moser卸纸时看了Rosa一眼，问“找活？”，Rosa说是，伸手去接最上面一捆。三捆纸搬完，手腕外侧已经肿起一条筋；Luise Moser说师傅今天不在，让改天再来。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2; flag +seeking_work; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2_

- **1924.09** `vienna-adolescence.brothers-torn-jacket/sew-it-yourself` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  Rosa在厨房找到一截粗线，颜色和外套差了一截。缝到一半，线打结，Rosa用牙咬断，重新来。Alois Lechner坐在对面，没问外套的事，只把汤碗推过来，说“先吃”。Rosa把最后一针打完，汤面已经结了一层薄皮。
  _effects: skill; food; relation kin with person-alois-lechner_

- **1925.01** `r13-edu.exam-eve::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/passed-entrance-exam` tags: education, exam, study, poverty, adolescence, reform, medical, schooling
  Rosa把煤油灯芯剪短，省下油熬到后半夜。代数题做错三遍，Rosa就用冷水拍脸，再把公式抄到手腕内侧。考试那天，监考把袖口翻了一遍，Rosa还是把最后一道题解完了。放榜那天，Rosa在人群后面站了很久，才敢往前挤。
  _effects: mood 0.06; flag +passed_entrance_exam; skill_

- **1925.02** `r3-teen.first-friend-group::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/admitted` tags: friendship, roof, belonging, betrayal, class, reform, medical, schooling
  屋顶上的暗号是两声口哨。Rosa吹对了一声，第二声卡在喉咙里，Josef Kral笑着替她补上。四个人分吃一袋偷来的栗子，壳扔到楼下，砸在Franz Weiss的雨棚上。
  _effects: mood 0.08; flag +joined_roof_group; role; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-53; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-53; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-28_

- **1925.04** `p9-childhood-adolescence.secret-in-the-stairwell/secret-mother-sets-place` tags: adolescence, friendship, identity, work, school, secret, church
  Rosa还是把这件事告诉了母亲。母亲没多问，在厨房案板旁多摆了一副碗筷，晚上让Elisabeth Bauer睡在炉边的旧垫子上，说先住下，别的事明早再想。
  _effects: relation friend with person-elisabeth-bauer; food; mood 0.02; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41_

- **1925.07** `yf18-runaway.bundle::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/buy-the-ticket-tonight` tags: runaway, escape, family, plan, night_train, reform, medical, schooling
  Rosa趁Rosa Lechner去上夜班，把储钱罐砸在窗台上。硬币滚进床缝，Rosa趴在地上摸到最后一枚，跑去南站买了一张最便宜的单程票；售票员问要不要往返，Rosa说不用，声音比自己想象中平静。
  _effects: state_add yf18-runaway-plan state:world-vienna-1912:99; cash -3 (the savings jar was broken for a ticket); flag +yf18_ticket_bought; mood 0.04; flag +yf18_runaway_plan_active_

- **1925.10** `r3-teen.parent-remarriage::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/accepts` tags: family, remarriage, stepfamily, grief, household, reform, medical, schooling
  Alois Lechner把漏雨的屋檐修好，又在门牌上添了自己的姓。Rosa没有叫他父亲，只在他修窗户时递钉子。晚饭桌上，Alois Lechner把最大的一块肉夹给Rosa，Rosa Lechner低头笑了。
  _effects: mood 0.04; flag +accepted_parent_remarriage; relation guardian with person-alois-lechner_

- **1926.01** `state_tick:yf18-runaway-plan/three-months-of-preparation/route-memorised` tags: yf18-runaway-plan, escape, youth, plan, home, travel, risk, state
  Rosa把去车站的路又走了一遍，记住了哪一段有煤气灯、哪一段住着巡夜的警察。回家后，Rosa在旧日历背面画了一条线，标出换乘的站名；那条线短得可笑，却比课本上的任何地图都清楚。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.preparation 35 -> 47; state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.suspicion 15 -> 17; mood 0.009999999999999995_

- **1926.01** `p9-childhood-youth-aspiration.save-for-books/installment-deal` tags: adolescence, education, aspiration, school, books, club, exam, task
  Gustav Moser把书翻到扉页，用铅笔写上日期和两克朗的欠额。Rosa把学生证放到柜台上的铁盒里，出门时回头看了一眼，证上那张照片拍得比本人精神。
  _effects: cash -1 (旧书首付); flag +book_debt_2kr; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-16; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-16_

- **1927.01** `state_expire:yf18-runaway-plan` tags: yf18-runaway-plan, escape, youth, plan, home, travel, risk, state
  Rosa的「床底下的包袱」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove yf18-runaway-plan state:world-vienna-1912:99; flag -yf18_runaway_plan_active_

- **1927.01** `r13-fam.help-homework::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/sums-finished` tags: task, family, school, homework, sibling, literacy, reform, medical
  Rosa把煤油灯往桌子中间挪，让Anna Berg看清算术本上的格子。孩子把七加八数了三遍，Rosa没有直接说答案，只把豆子一颗颗排在桌上。最后一页写完时，灯芯已经烧黑，Rosa才发现自己的作业还空着半页。
  _effects: flag +helped_sibling_with_homework; role; skill; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000604:child; skill_

- **1927.04** `yf18-dare.bridge-rail::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/fall-on-the-ice` tags: dare, bridge, river, friendship, risk, reform, medical, schooling
  Rosa走到第四步，鞋底在霜上滑了一下，整个人挂在栏杆外侧。Elisabeth Bauer抓住Rosa的手腕，Otto Lechner跑去叫桥头卖烤栗子的老人；Rosa被拉上来时，膝盖已经湿透，第二天走路一瘸一拐。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 45 -> 51; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 25 -> 15; relation friend with person-elisabeth-bauer; flag +yf18_bridge_scare; mood -0.03_

- **1927.05** `yf18-dare.prater-slide::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/ride-in-the-dark` tags: dare, prater, fairground, comic, friendship, reform, medical, schooling
  普拉特的木滑道在闭园后还亮着一盏值班灯。Rosa和Josef Moser从围栏底下钻进去，用一块麻袋垫着滑下来，木槽里的蜡味呛得人直笑。滑到底时，Ferdinand Bauer提着灯站在出口，问他们要不要再买一张票。
  _effects: mood 0.05; cash -1 (a late ticket paid to the watchman（其中1克朗记入债务）); reputation -0.009999999999999995; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-61; flag +yf18_prater_stunt; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-61; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-48_

- **1927.11** `vienna-adolescence.stolen-roll/caught-and-returned` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  Rosa的手刚碰到面包皮，身后就有人清了清嗓子。Josef Bauer站在晾衣绳下，手里还捏着夹子，什么也没问，只把手伸过来。Rosa把面包放回筐里，手背挨了两下，面包筐的盖子被Josef Bauer合上，发出一声闷响。
  _effects: reputation -0.06; relation acquaintance with person-josef-bauer; mood -0.1_

- **1927.12** `yf18-firstlook.tram-glove::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/return-it-at-the-gate` tags: first_love, love, tram, glove, youth, courtship, reform, medical
  下一站，Rosa追下车，把那只手套递还给Otto Lechner。Otto Lechner说了一声谢谢，手指在毛线手套里缩了一下；Rosa回到车上才发现自己坐过了两站，只好沿着环城路走回家。
  _effects: state_add yf18-first-awakening state:world-vienna-1912:157; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000089:child; mood 0.03; flag +yf18_first_awakening_active_

- **1928.01** `state_tick:yf18-first-awakening/half-year-of-glances/answered-glance` tags: yf18-first-awakening, love, youth, first_love, desire, school, awakening, state
  Rosa在教堂门口或学校走廊里又遇见Otto Lechner。这一次对方先点了头，Rosa准备好的整段话全忘了，只把手里的书换到另一只手上；回家后Rosa才发现，自己把书拿倒了。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 51 -> 56; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 15 -> 23; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.reciprocated false -> true; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000089:child; mood 0.03_

- **1928.01** `p9-childhood-youth-aspiration.save-for-books/bought-used-book` tags: adolescence, education, aspiration, school, books, club, exam, task
  书摊上那本《机械制图》被压在一摞旧画报下面，Rosa抽出来时抖落一层灰。Anton Winter把书翻到缺角那页，说三克朗拿走，他数出最后几枚赫勒，硬币在木板上叮当响。
  _effects: cash -3 (旧书摊买《机械制图》); skill; mood 0.05; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-20; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-20_

- **1928.01** `state_reaction:yf18-first-awakening/exam-week-distraction` tags: yf18-first-awakening, love, youth, first_love, desire, school, awakening, state
  考试周里，Rosa把课本摊在桌上，眼睛却一直看着窗外那条电车线。笔记的页边多了几个没有写完的名字，Rosa用橡皮擦掉，纸面起了毛。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 56 -> 53; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.talk 0 -> 2_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:003506_

- **1928.03** `r3-teen.police-caution::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::reform-parent-notice/cautioned` tags: police, caution, record, family, work, reform, medical, schooling
  Friedrich Hofer把Rosa的名字写进一本硬皮簿子，问一句写一句，最后让他按手印。Alois Lechner赶来时，Friedrich Hofer说带回去好好管教，这次不立案。走出门口，Alois Lechner的手一直攥着Rosa的袖子。
  _effects: reputation -0.039999999999999994; flag +police_caution_recorded; relation guardian with person-alois-lechner; knowledge “the police opened a caution file in the subject’s name”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-6_

  Rosa的成绩打动了本地学校，拿到一份覆盖学费的奖学金；家里第一次不用为这笔钱吵到半夜。
  _effects: state_add scholarship-holder state:world-vienna-1912:192; role_

- **1928.11** `yf18-body.danube-cabin::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/stay-behind-the-curtain` tags: body, desire, river, swimming, shame, first_love, reform, medical
  Rosa把浴巾裹到下巴，说自己怕水。Otto Lechner在门外笑，说去年还见Rosa从木塔上跳下去；Rosa没有解释，只隔着板缝看Klara把水撩到肩上。回家时，Rosa的泳衣还是干的，衣角被手攥得起了皱。
  _effects: state_add yf18-first-awakening state:world-vienna-1912:191; flag +yf18_stayed_dry; mood -0.01999999999999999_

- **1929.02** `vienna-social-movement.recruitment/read-and-decline` tags: development, social_movement, organization, politics

- **1929.03** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.misdelivered-letter/misdelivered-letter.opened` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, letter
  信封没封牢，Rosa在灯下把它掀开，里面是维也纳联合银行的催款单，欠了三十四克朗。她认出那名字是楼上常穿黑裙的女人，便把纸原样叠回去，用米汤把封口粘好，放到门房窗台上。
  _effects: mood -0.04000000000000001; flag +knows_upstairs_debt_

- **1929.06** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/subject_yields_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Rosa看Hermann Hofer盆里那几件小衬衫，把自家床单卷起来抱回屋，说等下午再晾。Hermann Hofer愣了一下，闷头把衣服挂好，收工回来时在Rosa门口放了一小包用旧报纸裹的洗衣碱。Rosa打开报纸，碱面里还掺着几粒粗盐。

  _effects: cash -2 (面包篮底下的戒指); role; interaction; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-76_

  Magistrat der k. k. Reichshaupt- und Residenzstadt Wien的人带来一台手压泵，说公署出三分之二，剩下的按户摊。Rosa把分摊的钱数念给Mitzi Lechner听，Mitzi Lechner说这比淘井便宜。装泵那天，孩子们围着铁管，等第一股水出来。

  四十七个弗朗茨·穆勒里，只有Rosa拿得出干净的房租簿。公证人Anton Winter把遗嘱念完，遗产是一辆灵车、两匹老马和十四克朗的欠账。Rosa没有钱下葬，先把灵车租给搬家的人；夜里跑环城大道，白天替人运钢琴。Franz Huber起初等着看笑话，后来开始给他介绍生意。

  铜像揭幕那天，Friedrich Hofer念错了男高音的名字，Helene Berg 2在台下把帽子捏变了形。没有人注意那张脸其实是照着Rosa的旧照片刻的；雕像很快成了约会的暗号，恋人们说“在假男高音下面等”。Rosa每天经过，偶尔替游客指路，从不纠正。

### 166 subject events

- **1912.02** `vienna-infant-household.laundry-day/sibling-watches` tags: infancy, health, family, home
  Josef Bauer在厨房的木盆里搓亚麻布，热水汽模糊了窗。Anna Bauer坐在摇床边的矮凳上晃拨浪鼓，Emil伸手去抓。等Josef Bauer晾完最后一块尿布，Emil已经在Anna Bauer的看护下睡熟，摇床没被蹬歪。
  _effects: relation kin with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912; health 0.020000000000000018 (被看护着，没磕碰); mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1913.01** `vienna-life-infant-first-smile/colic-night` tags: infancy, family, first, warm, injury
  夜里Emil哭了很久，Josef Bauer抱着孩子在屋里来回走，数着地板缝。天快亮时孩子终于打了个嗝，安静下来；Josef Bauer坐在床沿，发现自己还保持着走路的姿势。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; health -0.010000000000000009 (lost sleep)_

- **1914.01** `vienna-life-toddler-first-words/first-word-more` tags: infancy, family, first, comic
  Emil把空碗推回Josef Bauer面前，清清楚楚地说了一个字：“还要。”Josef Bauer愣住，随后把锅底最后一点糊刮进碗里。孩子学会了用一句话让世界再转一圈。
  _effects: skill; mood 0.03_

- **1914.12** `vienna-early-childhood.stairwell-fall/landlord-complaint` tags: childhood, family, home, street
  摔伤之后两天，房东女人在门厅拦住Josef Bauer，楼里的Anna Moser正提着菜篮站在门边。房东女人说楼梯间不许孩子嬉闹，铁扶手磕坏要赔。Josef Bauer把裹着布条的Emil往身后一拉，回了句“灯都没有，谁看得见台阶”。房东女人把话记在房租本边上，说下回修灯的钱从房租里扣。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-anna-moser; mood -0.07; flag +stairs_accident_noted_

- **1915.01** `vienna-life-child-family-legend/happy-legend` tags: childhood, family, secret, story
  Josef Bauer讲起自己小时候在乡下追一只白鹅，追到河边，鹅没追到，倒捡回一篮鸭蛋。Emil笑得从凳子上滑下来，Josef Bauer也跟着笑，说这个家不是只有欠账和葬礼。
  _effects: mood 0.04; mood 0.01999999999999999_

- **1915.01** `vienna-life-child-first-friend/shared-toy` tags: childhood, friendship, family, comic
  Josef在楼道里滚一只铁环，Emil站在门口看。Josef把铁环推过来，说“你推一次，我推一次”。两个人从楼梯口推到院子里，铁环倒下的声音把午睡的大人吵醒了一排。
  _effects: relation friend with person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912; skill; mood 0.049999999999999996_

- **1917.02** `vienna-early-childhood.fever-night/home-remedy` tags: childhood, family, home, street
  Emil烧得翻来覆去，Josef Bauer把醋倒进温水，用布蘸着擦手心、脚心，又去灶间煮接骨木汁。勺子送到嘴边，Emil偏头躲，Josef Bauer就等一会儿再喂。鸡叫时炉火压小，床脚用旧大衣堵住。两天后热度退了，嘴唇起了一层干皮。
  _effects: health 0.03 (土法退烧，热度渐退); mood -0.06_

- **1917.03** `vienna-childhood-street.washhouse-fever/fever-recover` tags: childhood, street, social, home, injury
  洗衣房里全是蒸汽和碱水味。Emil靠在墙边等Josef Bauer，湿衣服堆在脚边。夜里Emil开始发烧，Josef Bauer把湿布叠成长条，敷在额头，凉了再换。三天后热度退下去，Emil的嘴唇起了一层白皮，Josef Bauer的手指也裂了口子。
  _effects: health -0.03 (热病高烧); condition; relation kin with person-josef-bauer_

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-half-flour` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  面粉袋压在胸口，越抱越沉。Emil前面的大婶一直往后靠，Emil就把膝盖顶住对方的篮子，硬留出半步。买到半袋面粉后，Emil的鞋跟留在人堆里，找回来时鞋面全是泥。回家路上布袋换了两次肩，赤脚踩过石板时脚底发麻。
  _effects: food; skill_

- **1918.05** `vienna-childhood-friends.courtyard-race/won-the-race-lost-a-button` tags: childhood, social, school, street
  Emil跑到第二圈时鞋带松了，索性踩着鞋后跟跑。最后一圈，Emil从床单底下钻过去，先拍到墙。Maria Kral喘着气，把两颗弹珠拍进Emil手心。Emil低头才发现外套少了一颗扣子；窗口的Maria Kral扔下一根别针，说先别着。
  _effects: mood 0.05000000000000002; relation acquaintance with person-maria-kral_

- **1919.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.mind-sibling/sibling-shelter-missed-river` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, sibling, care
  河水那边传来同伴的笑声，Emil站在院门口听了听，还是回去把木盆扶正。Hermann Bauer睡醒哭了一声，Emil把最后半块面包掰碎泡进温水，一勺一勺喂。Josef Bauer回来时，Emil正蹲在盆边打瞌睡，手里还攥着喂饭的木勺。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000004; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000690:child; relation kin with person-josef-bauer_

- **1919.10** `vienna-childhood-street.cigarette-card/card-stolen` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  巷口有人跑过来，肩膀撞在Emil的胳膊上。烟画卡脱手，被风卷到路中间。Emil追了两步，鞋底在湿石板上打滑，手掌先着地。Hedwig从后面追上去，在拐角抓住那男孩的袖子，布条撕下来一截，人还是跑了。
  _effects: mood -0.08; relation friend with person-phase15-Hedwig-world-vienna-1912; condition_

- **1920.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-both-families` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  Rosa Moser把Emil拽到队伍前头，往自己身前塞，说“孩子占不了多大地儿”。窗口一次递出两包面包，Emil把邻居那包夹在胳肢窝，自己这包抱在胸前。到Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang门口，Emil蹲下把两包并排，按Rosa Moser说的份量分开，连找零的铜板也分成两小堆。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-21_

- **1921.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.mind-sibling/sibling-shelter-missed-river` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, sibling, care
  巷口的喊声第二次传来时，Emil正用膝盖压住木盆边沿，给Maria Bauer挡穿堂风。Maria Bauer在旧布底下翻了个身，Emil立刻停住，等呼吸又匀了才敢动。Josef Bauer推门进来，先看见盆里睡着的Maria Bauer，再看见Emil冻红的脚背，把灶上留的汤热了热。
  _effects: mood -0.02; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000585:child; relation kin with person-josef-bauer_

- **1922.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-half-flour` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  面粉袋压在胸口，越抱越沉。Emil前面的大婶一直往后靠，Emil就把膝盖顶住对方的篮子，硬留出半步。买到半袋面粉后，Emil的鞋跟留在人堆里，找回来时鞋面全是泥。回家路上布袋换了两次肩，赤脚踩过石板时脚底发麻。
  _effects: food; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-6; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10_

- **1922.01** `vienna-childhood-street.cigarette-card/card-trade` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  “三张换一张。”Hedwig把皇帝像在Emil眼前晃了晃。Emil数出三张攒了半年的烟画，一张张递过去。皇帝像到手还没捂热，Hedwig已经推开杂货铺的门，用同一张卡换了糖。Emil把卡举到光下看，觉得皇帝的脸有点歪。
  _effects: mood 0.04000000000000001; relation friend with person-phase15-Hedwig-world-vienna-1912_

- **1923.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.mind-sibling/sibling-falls-at-well` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, sibling, care
  土豆滚进墙角的煤灰里，Emil弯腰去追，再抬头时Hermann Bauer已经坐在井台边，裤腿湿了一片，哭声一抽一抽。Friedrich Hofer把孩子抱进灶间，Emil跟在后面，手指抠着门框。Josef Bauer回来没问经过，只把Emil的碗收走一半，说“看个人都看不住”。
  _effects: mood -0.06; relation kin with person-josef-bauer_

- **1923.06** `vienna-adolescence.brothers-torn-jacket/sew-it-yourself` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  外套腋下裂了一道口子，里布翻出来。Emil把袖口套在桌角上撑开，穿针时试了三次才引上线。针脚一针长一针短，像蜈蚣。Josef Bauer端着汤进来，站在桌边看了一会儿，把碗往Emil那边推了推，自己只留了半碗。
  _effects: skill; food; relation kin with person-josef-bauer_

- **1923.07** `p9-childhood-adolescence.church-club/club-follow-without-paying` tags: adolescence, friendship, identity, work, school, secret, church
  Emil口袋里没有十赫勒，却还是跟着队伍走到车站。修女在栅栏边拦住Emil，翻了翻名册，说没交钱的孩子不能上车；Mitzi Lechner在车厢门口回头看。
  _effects: reputation -0.03; relation acquaintance with person-mitzi-lechner; mood -0.03_

- **1923.10** `vienna-youth-threshold.sibling-departure/quarrel-who-leaves` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, transition
  车票只够一个人走，晚饭桌上话越说越大声。Anna Bauer说该自己走，Emil说自己也快够岁数了。母亲把汤勺往锅沿一磕，屋里静下来，最后Emil摔门出去在楼梯上坐了半宿，直到楼上有人喊冷。
  _effects: mood -0.07_

- **1924.01** `state_tick:wish-love/love-pressure/press-on` tags: wish-love, wish, wish:love, love, family, intimacy, state, state_tick
  Emil又把那件没实现的事翻出来想了一遍；这一次没有告诉任何人。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000004; flag +wish:love:pursuing_

- **1924.06** `r15-work.master-takes-on::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/turned-away` tags: work, craft, apprentice, trade, poverty, reform, medical, schooling
  Alois Bauer看了看Emil的手，说太小，拿不稳凿子。Emil回家没有提这件事，第二天去码头找零工。
  _effects: mood -0.07999999999999999; flag +apprenticeship_refused; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-42_

- **1924.12** `r1-fam.sibling-rivalry::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/rivalry.neither` tags: family, siblings, rivalry, apprenticeship, craft, reform, medical, schooling
  Anna Lechner 2看了看兄弟俩的手，说两个人的指节都不够硬，谁也不收。Josef Bauer求了半天，Anna Lechner 2答应让两人一起干杂活，管饭不给钱。Elisabeth Bauer干了三天就走了，Emil留下来扫地、磨刀，磨到第六个月，Anna Lechner 2才让他碰第一块好木料。
  _effects: cash 1 (同一把刨子); flag +worked_up_from_dogsbody; role; skill; relation acquaintance with person-elisabeth-bauer; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-46_

- **1925.01** `r13-edu.exam-eve::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/passed-entrance-exam` tags: education, exam, study, poverty, adolescence, reform, medical, schooling
  Emil把煤油灯芯剪短，省下油熬到后半夜。代数题做错三遍，Emil就用冷水拍脸，再把公式抄到手腕内侧。考试那天，监考把袖口翻了一遍，Emil还是把最后一道题解完了。放榜那天，Emil在人群后面站了很久，才敢往前挤。
  _effects: mood 0.06; flag +passed_entrance_exam; skill_

- **1925.01** `yf18-firstlook.tram-glove::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/let-friend-return-it` tags: first_love, love, tram, glove, youth, courtship, reform, medical
  Emil把手套交给Otto Lechner，让Otto Lechner替自己去还。Otto Lechner回来时说，Klara问是谁捡到的；Otto Lechner顺口报了一个名字，Emil听见那名字不是自己的，笑得比平时响。
  _effects: state_add yf18-first-awakening state:world-vienna-1912:91; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000089:child; mood -0.009999999999999995_

- **1925.03** `yf18-body.danube-cabin::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/stay-behind-the-curtain` tags: body, desire, river, swimming, shame, first_love, reform, medical
  Emil把浴巾裹到下巴，说自己怕水。Elisabeth Bauer在门外笑，说去年还见Emil从木塔上跳下去；Emil没有解释，只隔着板缝看Klara把水撩到肩上。回家时，Emil的泳衣还是干的，衣角被手攥得起了皱。
  _effects: state_add yf18-first-awakening state:world-vienna-1912:90; flag +yf18_stayed_dry; mood -0.020000000000000004; flag +yf18_first_awakening_active_

- **1925.04** `yf18-friend.two-tickets::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/confront-at-the-cloakroom` tags: first_love, friendship, betrayal, dance, tickets, reform, medical, schooling
  Emil在衣帽间堵住Elisabeth Bauer，问那两张票怎么到了Josef手里。Elisabeth Bauer说Emil从来没敢开口，自己只是替Emil把话说了；Emil把票根揉成一团，塞进Elisabeth Bauer的西装口袋，转身时撞翻了侍者的托盘。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 35 -> 40; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.talk 10 -> 20; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 10 -> 5; relation friend with person-elisabeth-bauer; relation friend with person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912; reputation -0.020000000000000004; mood -0.05000000000000002_

- **1925.10** `r3-teen.police-caution::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::reform-parent-notice/fined` tags: police, caution, record, family, work, reform, medical, schooling
  Anton Winter说按条例罚三克朗，交不出就通知学校。Josef Bauer从内衣口袋里摸出叠好的钞票，一张张数，数到第三张时手停了。回家后，那三克朗被写在厨房的账单上，旁边画了一道红线。
  _effects: cash -3 (派出所里的第一次签字); mood -0.08000000000000002; flag +fined_by_police; relation rival with person-josef-bauer; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-20_

  Emil在厨房里对着镜子练了七遍开场白，第二天却在离Klara三步远的地方拐进面包店。Emil买了一袋并不需要的盐，回家路上把开场白又想了一遍；这一次，连标点都更熟练了。

  Emil把包袱重新打开，衬衫上的褶子已经压平了。Emil把洗礼证明放回抽屉，去厨房烧了一壶水；Josef Bauer醒来时，桌上多了一杯茶，Emil说今天不想去上学。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; relation kin with person-josef-bauer; flag +yf18_unpacked_

- **1926.06** `vienna-adolescence.brothers-torn-jacket/sell-the-coat` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  巷口的风把外套下摆吹起来。旧货贩子闻了闻领子，说给两克朗。Emil把钱卷进袜筒，回家前在楼梯口站了一会儿。Josef Bauer发现衣钩空了，问了三遍，Emil都说没看见，脚趾在鞋里把硬币越踩越紧。
  _effects: cash 2 (卖掉哥哥旧外套); relation kin with person-josef-bauer; mood -0.03999999999999998_

  Emil回到学校，发现同班同学已经毕业，新班级里没人认识Emil。Emil在操场上站了一下午，最后没有进教室，跟着父亲去了工地。晚上Emil把课本放进箱底，没舍得扔。工地的工头看Emil年纪小，只让Emil搬轻的。课本压在最底下。
  _effects: mood -0.07; flag +dropout_confirmed_

  检查员来查佣工合同，Sozialdemokratische Arbeiterpartei Österreichs (SDAP)让Emil把手藏在围裙下面。Emil没有藏，摊开掌心，那块新疤还没有褪成白色。检查员看了一眼，又看了一眼Helene Huber，把名字写进本子。Sozialdemokratische Arbeiterpartei Österreichs (SDAP)说这是烫衣服不小心，谁家没有这种事。
  _effects: reputation 0.05; flag +reported_iron_burn_to_inspector; role; knowledge “the inspector recorded the burn and the household address”; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-29; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-19; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-32; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-29_

- **1929.02** `major-crisis.rent-increase/pay-the-increase` tags: crisis, housing, rent, eviction, family, poverty
  房东没有露面，只让看门人Josef把补款单塞进门缝：每月要多十四克朗，先交十八克朗（本月十四加四克朗手续费）才肯谈续租。Emil把准备买冬煤的钱数了两遍，还是把钱交了，收据上写的是“自愿捐助”。Josef低声说，不签的人已经有两家搬走了，楼下的煤价牌又换了数字。
  _effects: cash -18 (advance payment demanded with the rent increase); relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912; flag +rent_increase_accepted_

  _effects: cash -4 (医生出诊费和药费); health 0.05 (药粉和糖浆缓解症状); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-16_

  _effects: cash -6 (支付邻居照看工钱和煤钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-46; health 0.04 (白天有人按时喂药和换洗)_

### 187 subject events

- **1912.02** `vienna-life-infant-first-smile/neighbour-warning` tags: infancy, family, first, warm
  隔壁的女人探头进来，说孩子笑得太早，老人讲这不是好兆头。Josefine Hofer没有接话，只把Lena往怀里拢了拢；孩子却伸手去抓那女人围裙上的扣子，抓得不肯松。
  _effects: mood -0.009999999999999995; flag +early_smile_omen_

- **1913.01** `vienna-life-toddler-first-words/first-word-more` tags: infancy, family, first, comic
  Lena把空碗推回Friedrich Hofer面前，清清楚楚地说了一个字：“还要。”Friedrich Hofer愣住，随后把锅底最后一点糊刮进碗里。孩子学会了用一句话让世界再转一圈。
  _effects: skill; mood 0.03_

- **1915.03** `p9-childhood-household.supper-bowl/gave-potato-to-younger` tags: childhood, family, household, home, siblings, illness, neighbors
  Hedwig的碗先见了底，Lena把自己那块土豆拨过去，说“我不爱吃面的”。Friedrich Hofer没拆穿，转身去灶口添煤，回来时把半片香肠压在Lena的面包下面。锅底刮得响，Lena把香肠又分了一半给Hedwig。
  _effects: food; relation kin with person-phase15-Hedwig-world-vienna-1912; mood 0.03_

- **1915.12** `vienna-life-child-first-friend/stolen-marble` tags: childhood, friendship, family, comic
  Anton把Lena的玻璃弹珠藏进鞋里，被当场看见。Anton说只是“替你拿着”，Lena没有揭穿，也没有再拿出第二颗。两个人蹲在墙根，把一只蚂蚁看了很久，谁也没有先回家。
  _effects: relation friend with person-phase15-Anton-world-vienna-1912; skill; mood -0.01_

- **1916.01** `vienna-life-child-family-legend/lost-relative` tags: childhood, family, secret, story
  Friedrich Hofer在收拾箱子时翻出一张没有署名的照片，说那是家里一个“去了很远地方”的人。Lena问还回不回来，Friedrich Hofer把照片翻过去，说先把名字记住，别在饭桌上问。
  _effects: knowledge “家里有一张没有署名的照片，照片上的人去了很远的地方。”; flag +family_legend_photo; mood -0.01_

- **1916.07** `kernel-child.household-rules::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/edu-rules-feeds-youngest` tags: childhood, family, food, rules, everyday, rural, agriculture, small_town
  最小的弟妹在桌边哭，Lena把自己的半碗汤推过去，说在学校已经吃过。Friedrich Hofer看了Lena一眼，把自己的面包掰下一角，放回Lena碗里。那天晚上，Lena学会了把饥饿说成不饿。Lena把空碗推远了一点。
  _effects: relation kin with person-friedrich-hofer; skill; condition; flag +fed_youngest_sibling_

- **1917.09** `iw18-play.kite-on-roof::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/climbs-and-gets-it` tags: childhood, play, kite, roof, neighbor, comic, rural, agriculture
  风筝挂在修鞋铺的屋檐上，尾巴缠着铁皮烟囱。Lena踩着煤箱爬上矮墙，Anton在下面扶着箱子，不停地说“慢一点”。Lena把风筝扯下来时撕了一个角，落地时膝盖先着地，手里还举着那截竹骨。
  _effects: reputation 0.02; relation friend with person-phase15-Anton-world-vienna-1912; mood 0.04; flag +kite_from_roof_

- **1917.12** `iw18-home.first-memory::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/the-first-snow` tags: childhood, first_memory, fire, family, wonder, fear, rural, agriculture
  Lena清早醒来，窗台上积了一层白，玻璃上的冰花像羽毛。Friedrich Hofer还没生火，屋里冷得能看见呼吸；Lena把手指按在窗上，留下五个小洞，又隔着洞看楼下的送煤车。那天是Lena记得的第一个冬天。
  _effects: mood 0.04; flag +first_memory_snow_

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-dragged-docked-pay` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  “你看看这角。”Friedrich Hofer把床单拎起来，煤渣簌簌往下掉。Lena伸手去拍，反而把灰按进布纹里。Friedrich Hofer从工钱里数回两枚，只留一赫勒在Lena手心，让重洗那角。晚饭前Lena一直蹲在井台边，换了两盆水，指甲缝里全是黑泥。
  _effects: cash 0.01 (被扣后只剩一赫勒); mood -0.05; relation acquaintance with person-friedrich-hofer_

- **1919.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-half-flour` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  队伍从面包房门口拐进巷子，Lena把布袋夹在腋下，学着前面人的样子，用胳膊肘顶住后面人的篮子。窗口的伙计舀面粉时，Lena踮脚看着秤，直到半袋面倒进袋口。挤出人群时鞋跟被踩掉，Lena单脚跳了两步，在泥里摸回鞋，索性赤脚走回家。
  _effects: food; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-20_

- **1920.10** `vienna-childhood-friends.courtyard-race/fell-on-wet-stones` tags: childhood, social, school, street
  Lena想从床单底下钻过去，脚下一滑，膝盖磕在石板棱上。Otto Winter听见哭声出来，先看了看伤口，又回去端水。手帕扎得很紧，Lena试着站起来，被Otto Winter按回台阶上。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-otto-winter_

- **1921.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-half-flour` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  面粉袋压在胸口，越抱越沉。Lena前面的大婶一直往后靠，Lena就把膝盖顶住对方的篮子，硬留出半步。买到半袋面粉后，Lena的鞋跟留在人堆里，找回来时鞋面全是泥。回家路上布袋换了两次肩，赤脚踩过石板时脚底发麻。
  _effects: food; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10_

- **1922.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-dragged-docked-pay` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work

  _effects: cash 0.01 (被扣后只剩一赫勒); mood -0.05; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-6; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-6; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-30_

- **1923.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-both-families` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  Josef Bauer把Lena拽到队伍前头，往自己身前塞，说“孩子占不了多大地儿”。窗口一次递出两包面包，Lena把邻居那包夹在胳肢窝，自己这包抱在胸前。到Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang门口，Lena蹲下把两包并排，按Josef Bauer说的份量分开，连找零的铜板也分成两小堆。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-josef-bauer; skill_

  _effects: food; cash -4 (凑钱买面包); relation friend with person-phase15-Anton-world-vienna-1912_

- **1925.01** `r13-edu.exam-eve::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/passed-entrance-exam` tags: education, exam, study, poverty, adolescence, reform, medical, schooling
  Lena把煤油灯芯剪短，省下油熬到后半夜。代数题做错三遍，Lena就用冷水拍脸，再把公式抄到手腕内侧。考试那天，监考把袖口翻了一遍，Lena还是把最后一道题解完了。放榜那天，Lena在人群后面站了很久，才敢往前挤。
  _effects: mood 0.06; flag +passed_entrance_exam; skill_

- **1925.03** `vienna-adolescence.brothers-torn-jacket/sew-it-yourself` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  外套腋下裂了一道口子，里布翻出来。Lena把袖口套在桌角上撑开，穿针时试了三次才引上线。针脚一针长一针短，像蜈蚣。Friedrich Hofer端着汤进来，站在桌边看了一会儿，把碗往Lena那边推了推，自己只留了半碗。
  _effects: skill; food; relation kin with person-friedrich-hofer_

  Lena把手套塞进书包最里层，回家后放在枕头下面。第二天上学，Lena每隔一会儿就摸一下书包带，像在确认一件不能给家里看见的东西；手套上还留着电车木座的气味。
  _effects: state_add yf18-first-awakening state:world-vienna-1912:97; flag +yf18_kept_glove; mood 0.020000000000000004; flag +yf18_first_awakening_active_

- **1928.01** `r13-edu.boarding-school::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/admitted-to-dormitory` tags: education, boarding_school, residence, family, scholarship, reform, medical, schooling
  Lena把铁皮箱绑在自行车后座，骑了三十里到寄宿学校。舍监检查行李，把家里的腌菜罐没收，说会招老鼠。Lena在熄灯后偷偷把罐子从垃圾堆捡回来，藏在床底，第一顿饭就着腌菜吃完了两碗粥。第一个星期Lena把腌菜分给同屋，换回三张邮票和一支不漏水的钢笔。
  _effects: flag +boarding_school_admitted; education secondary_

- **1928.03** `vienna-youth-threshold.sibling-departure/take-over-bed-and-ledger` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, transition
  Anton走后，靠墙那张床空了出来，Lena把自己的铺盖搬过去，也接过那本记着欠账的旧账本：面包店三克朗、煤铺一克朗八十赫勒。晚上Lena坐在床沿一笔笔抄清，第一次知道家里的钱是怎么缺的。
  _effects: relation kin with person-phase15-Anton-world-vienna-1912; skill; mood -0.04999999999999999; cash -0.5 (替兄姐补齐欠煤铺的零头)_

- **1928.03** `yf18-firstlook.parish-dance::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/the-second-dance` tags: first_love, dance, church, courtship, jealousy, reform, medical, schooling
  舞卡上第一支曲子被Emil写走了。Lena在教会礼堂的木地板上踩错了两步，Emil说没关系，把手放回Lena背后；第三支曲子响起时，Hedwig在门口冲Lena比了个手势，Lena假装没看见。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 50 -> 58; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 15 -> 25; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.reciprocated false -> true; relation friend with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912; mood 0.03999999999999998_

- **1928.08** `yf18-body.danube-cabin::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/wade-in-first` tags: body, desire, river, swimming, shame, first_love, reform, medical
  多瑙河的浴棚按男女分开，木板缝里能看见对面的人影。Lena穿着借来的毛线泳衣，站在第三间门口不敢下水；Hedwig从水里冒出来，头发贴在额头上，说水不冷。Lena走进河里时，满脑子都是自己肋骨上那道疤。
  _effects: state_add yf18-first-awakening state:world-vienna-1912:189; health 0.019999999999999997 (cold river water and a long swim); mood 0.02999999999999997; flag +yf18_first_awakening_active_

- **1929.01** `state_tick:yf18-first-awakening/half-year-of-glances/answered-glance` tags: yf18-first-awakening, love, youth, first_love, desire, school, awakening, state
  Lena在教堂门口或学校走廊里又遇见Hedwig。这一次对方先点了头，Lena准备好的整段话全忘了，只把手里的书换到另一只手上；回家后Lena才发现，自己把书拿倒了。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 58 -> 63; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 25 -> 33; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.reciprocated true -> true; mood 0.03_

- **1929.01** `r13-edu.dropout-return::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/dropout-confirmed` tags: education, dropout, return, school, work, reform, medical, schooling
  Lena回到学校，发现同班同学已经毕业，新班级里没人认识Lena。Lena在操场上站了一下午，最后没有进教室，跟着父亲去了工地。晚上Lena把课本放进箱底，没舍得扔。工地的工头看Lena年纪小，只让Lena搬轻的。课本压在最底下。
  _effects: mood -0.06999999999999998; flag +dropout_confirmed; relation kin with person-friedrich-hofer_

- **1929.01** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.stove-and-line/stove-and-line.move-to-line-end` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Lena把自己泡着的衣服端到绳子最末一段，把靠近炉子的位置让给Grete Huber，条件是对方看着火，别让水烧干。Grete Huber点头，往炉膛里添了一块煤，说中午以前一定把炉子空出来。两家的盆就挨着放在窗下。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13; mood 0.02999999999999997; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13_

- **1929.08** `r1-cul.fashion-purchase::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/ruined-by-rain` tags: culture, fashion, shop, money, vanity, reform, medical, schooling
  Lena在橱窗前站了三个下午，最后数出十二克朗，把帽子捧回家。第一次戴出去就下雨，帽檐塌下来，像一只湿透的鸟；Elisabeth Bauer把自己的旧毡帽换给Lena，说这顶经得起雨。Lena把新帽子晾在炉边，第二天缩了一圈，再也戴不上，十二克朗只剩下一圈硬边。
  _effects: cash -12 (橱窗里的帽子); mood -0.06; condition; flag +hat_ruined_by_rain; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-16_

- **1929.10** `vienna-housing-move.sublet-room/sublet-daylight-only` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Lena和Mitzi Moser谈妥只出租白天，夜里不留宿，收两克朗。床仍归Lena家，钥匙只在白天给。Mitzi Moser说不碍事，他住的工棚不许带人，白天有个落脚的地方就够。Gustav Berg听了点头，说这样少些闲话；到了傍晚，Lena把床铺重新收好，屋里照旧几口人。
  _effects: cash 2 (白天出租床位的钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-49; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-49_

- **1930.01** `state_tick:yf18-first-awakening/half-year-of-glances/answered-glance` tags: yf18-first-awakening, love, youth, first_love, desire, school, awakening, state
  Lena在教堂门口或学校走廊里又遇见Hedwig。这一次对方先点了头，Lena准备好的整段话全忘了，只把手里的书换到另一只手上；回家后Lena才发现，自己把书拿倒了。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 63 -> 68; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 33 -> 41; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.reciprocated true -> true; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1930.01** `kernel-health.task-pharmacy-hunt::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/pharmacy-gives-up` tags: health, task, pharmacy, shortage, black_market, reform, medical, schooling
  Lena在柜台前站了很久，最后把处方留在那里，说等有货再来。回家路上经过医院，看见药房窗口也挂着“无货”；第二天，处方被雨水泡烂在口袋里。Lena把湿透的处方摊在炉边烤，字还是糊了。
  _effects: cash -1 (tram fare and a paper envelope for the prescription); condition; role; knowledge “医院药房也会挂“无货”；处方留在柜台，第二天下雨就泡烂了。”_

- **1930.02** `vienna-women-housework.coal-stove/coal-stove.cold-day` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  孩子放学回来搓着手说冷，Lena把仅有的半捧煤末压进炉底，火苗细得像灯芯。她没接Anna Bauer 2递来的煤块，只说等明天煤店开门。屋里哈气成雾，晚饭的土豆是凉的。
  _effects: mood -0.03999999999999998; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10_

- **1930.03** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_found_by_neighbor` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Lena傍晚从面粉厂下工回来，发现挂在腰带上的房门钥匙不见了，在楼梯间来回找了四趟。Marie Huber在二楼拐角的煤堆旁捡到那把铜钥匙，钥匙上缠着红线，一眼就认出来。Marie Huber敲开Lena的门，把钥匙拍在桌上，说自己眼神好使。
  _effects: relation friend with person-marie-huber; mood 0.04999999999999999_

- **1930.09** `vienna-legacy-worklife.sick-fund/sick-fund.fill-form` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  衙门走廊的地砖是黑白相间的，Lena站着站着就把重心从一只脚换到另一只脚。窗口里的办事员姓什么没人知道，只叫“三号窗”。表递进去时，里面说“等复核”，声音很平。
  _effects: relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-49; skill_

- **1930.10** `ad20-family-dinner::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/the-fourth-bowl` tags: family, dinner, everyday, obligation, public, grief, reform, medical
  Mitzi Lechner来得比约定早，手里提着一小袋栗子。Lena多摆了一副碗筷，又把汤里的肉往那只碗里拨了两块。饭吃到一半，Mitzi Lechner忽然说“你们家吃饭真安静”，Lena才发现自己一直在听楼上的脚步。
  _effects: food; mood 0.02999999999999997; relation acquaintance with person-mitzi-lechner; flag +family_dinner_guest_

  午休的哨子刚响，Alois Bauer夹着烟走到门口。Lena报上姓名、住址和干过的活，声音有点发紧。工头嗯了一声，说明早六点来，管一顿午饭，干得好再谈工钱。
  _effects: flag +trial_work_pending; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-42; mood 0.04999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-42; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71_

- **1931.01** `oj18-lift.stuck::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/blackout-lift` tags: lift, building, work, night, passenger, odd_job, suspense, reform
  停电来得毫无预兆，轿厢里只剩一盏应急蜡。Lena用手动闸把轿厢停在最近的楼层，又用肩膀顶开门；乘客一个个跨出去，最后一个人回头说“你该去当消防员”。Lena的胳膊酸了三天，管理处的维修单上却只写“正常”。
  _effects: reputation 0.04; cash 1 (blackout shift pay); state_add oj18-odd-apprenticeship state:world-vienna-1912:275; flag +lift_operator; flag +blackout_lift; knowledge “停电时轿厢里只剩一盏应急蜡，手动闸要停在最近的楼层。”; role; flag +odd_job_apprentice_

  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Rosa Hofer这周主动擦了黑板。Lena把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 57 -> 60; mood 0.01999999999999999_

  报纸的社会版把Lena家的门牌号和那笔钱的数目印在一起，标题是“一笔不该有的好运”。第二天门口多了两个陌生人，一个说是远亲，一个说代表一家孤儿院。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.public false -> true; state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 0 -> 2; reputation -0.020000000000000004; flag +windfall_went_public_

- **1931.08** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/neighbor_yanks_clothes` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Anna Bauer 2嫌Lena的床单滴水滴到Anna Bauer 2家衬衫上，一把扯下床单扔在院子的石板上。Lena下楼捡起来时，床单角沾了泥，重洗又要费肥皂。两人在院子当中对喊，楼上有人推开窗看热闹。Lena把床单重新搭上绳子时，故意把水拧在Anna Bauer 2那侧。
  _effects: relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10; cash -0.15 (重洗床单的肥皂钱)_

- **1931.09** `vienna-adult-social.burial-fund/burial-fund.paid` tags: adulthood, social, work, family
  车间里管互助会的Josef在午休时把本子摊在Lena的工具箱上，说这个月还差最后一份月钱。Lena从马甲内袋摸出二十个克罗伊策，Josef当着他的面在本子上画了钩。
  _effects: cash -0.3 (缴纳丧葬互助会的月钱); relation colleague with person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912; reputation 0.04_

  一个多年没走动的亲戚提着一盒点心上门，先问Lena的身体，再问那笔钱是怎么来的。临走时他说起小时候替Lena家垫过一冬天的煤钱，数字记不清了，但意思很清楚。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 2 -> 3; flag +windfall_claim_by_relative; mood -0.01999999999999999_

  Lena和Elisabeth Bauer在楼道里吵到半夜，最后因为下个月的房租又坐回同一张桌。谁都没有提和好，只把账本重新摊开；Elisabeth Bauer把煤钱推过来，Lena接过去数了两遍，手指碰到一起又分开，屋里的灯亮到很晚。
  _effects: mood -0.03_

  一年过去，钱没有变多，却也没有一下花完。Lena把一部分存进邮局储蓄所，一部分借给楼下的鞋匠；鞋匠每月送来一双补好的鞋，利息就夹在鞋垫下面。街口的杂货商开始把好面粉留到Lena来的时候。

- **1932.02** `vienna-parish-aid.rent-box/rent-box.food-only` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Lena只收下篮里的面包和豆子，把煤袋推回Maria Stein手里，说房租自己再想办法，请她们给介绍点零工。Maria Stein把煤放在走廊窗台，说礼拜四后院有人要拆旧木箱，一赫勒一捆，工钱不多但现结。Lena记下门牌，答应一早过去。

- **1932.10** `vienna-women-housework.coal-stove/coal-stove.borrow-fire` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  Lena端着一只旧锅去敲Elisabeth Bauer的门，想借几块烧红的炭。Elisabeth Bauer让她从自家炉膛里夹了一块，又抓了一小把引火的木片。Lena连声道谢，回来把炭放进炉栅，火是有了，屋里却还是冷得能呵出白气。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-elisabeth-bauer; food; mood 0.01999999999999999_

- **1932.10** `vienna-church-community.tenement-prayer/rent-prayer-help` tags: faith, social, family, home

  _effects: condition; food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17_

- **1932.12** `vienna-medical-treatment.sanatorium/home-rest` tags: health, medical, family, money
  安娜的汤锅外面包着旧毛巾，端进来时还烫手。Lena靠着枕头喝，喝一半咳一半。房东太太在走廊里故意大声说，肺病可不是闹着玩的，安娜探出头去，说医生讲了不传染。
  _effects: health 0.03 (在家休养但条件有限); food; cash -0.5 (房东要求的清洁费)_

  钱在一年里薄了一层：屋顶要修，姐夫来借，彩票站的老主顾说有个稳赚的买卖。Lena把账本翻到最后一页，发现写下的支出比记得的多出四克朗；窗外有人在敲铁皮，声音像在催账。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.principal 4 -> 0; state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.spent 0 -> 5; cash -1 (the account does not balance); mood -0.01999999999999999_

  _effects: cash -0.3 (缴纳丧葬互助会的月钱); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-76; reputation 0.04; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-76_

  _effects: cash 6 (补助金窗口的第二张表); flag +work13_unemployment_benefit_approved; knowledge “the benefit runs for twelve weeks and must be collected in p”_

- **1934.04** `vienna-legacy-worklife.union-office/union-office.enroll-first` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  来问入会的是个瘦高的年轻人，帽檐捏在手里，说自己在第十六区的织布厂做了半年。Lena把名册翻到空白页，蘸水笔尖在“住址”那栏停了一下，等他报出街名。老成员在角落里把账本合上，声音很轻。
  _effects: cash 1 (新会员的入会费先记在桌上); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-52; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-81; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-52_

  后厅的椅子不够，Lena 站在柱子旁听Karl Kraus 读一篇关于报纸谎言的文章。Karl Kraus 读到一半停下来，指出台下某位记者上周写的句子，那人把帽子压低。散场后，Lena 在账单背面抄下一句，墨迹被咖啡杯底印开。
  _effects: knowledge “Karl Kraus 在咖啡馆公开朗读时，会逐句引用报纸原文并指出其中的修辞和谎言。”; cash -2 (coffee and a seat at Café Central); mood 0.01999999999999999; skill_

  _effects: health 0.01 (没有久留，避开了病人的屋子); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-31; mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-31_

  Lena还是买下了报纸，回家用它包住一条鲱鱼。油墨沾在鱼皮上，连载的字迹倒着贴在盐粒里。晚饭时，隔壁的人问起今天报上写了什么，Lena说只记得价钱又涨了两格。
  _effects: food; cash -1 (bread and fish for dinner); mood 0.010000000000000009; flag +wrapped_fish_in_newspaper_

  _effects: state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 1 -> 2; mood -0.020000000000000018_

  _effects: cash 2 (白天出租床位的钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44_

  Friedrich Hofer把账本、印章和一把铜钥匙一起推到Lena面前，说铺子从今天起归Lena。账本最后一页记着欠供货商的钱，也记着三个老主顾的赊账。Hermann Huber站在柜台后面，等Lena开口；Lena先把印章收进内袋，再让Hermann Huber把今天卖出的货念一遍。

- **1937.01** `initiative:found_process:mortuary:person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44-evt:world-vienna-1912:004838`
  圣马克斯殡葬合作社在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang经过数年的招募、会议和互助，把分散的行动整理成公开纲领；这不是预先写好的历史，而是成员自己累积出来的运动。
  _effects: historical process 圣马克斯殡葬合作社的持续运动; historical development:initiative-mortuary:person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44-evt:world-vienna-1912:004838-1937 as organizer; historical development:initiative-mortuary:person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44-evt:world-vienna-1912:004838-1937 as organizer; knowledge “圣马克斯殡葬合作社的持续运动不是一次会议，而是我们这些年积累出来的持续组织。”; reputation 0.039999999999999994_

- **1937.01** `historical_phase`
  历史进程《圣马克斯殡葬合作社在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang经过数年的招募、会议和互助，把分散的行动整理成公开纲领；这》从 emerging 进入 mobilizing。

- **1937.07** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.sick-parent-night/hire-neighbor-daycare` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Lena请楼下的Anton Bauer 2白天来照看Friedrich Hofer，先付一周六克朗工钱，讲好每天另管两顿饭，煤钱也从这六克里出。Anton Bauer 2上午换床单，下午喂药，临走把便盆提到楼道口。Lena下工回来先摸煤箱，再在练习本上记下当天的饭钱和煤钱，看见Friedrich Hofer的嘴唇不再干裂。
  _effects: cash -6 (支付邻居照看工钱和煤钱); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-66; health 0.03999999999999998 (白天有人按时喂药和换洗); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-66_

- **1937.09** `vienna-life-anschluss-1938/resists` tags: politics, persecution, 1938
  Lena在登记桌旁说了句“这里以前不是这样”。Friedrich Hofer记下地址，当晚两个穿制服的人来敲门，把Lena带到警局，第二天放回来时脸上有伤。
  _effects: condition; role; cash -5 (fine and lost work)_

- **1937.11** `vienna-historical-social-movement.relief-network/open-the-books-again` tags: history, historical_process, social_movement, organization, politics
  Lena把账本摊在面粉袋上，一栏一栏念，念到某笔去向不明时屋里静了。老人把手套戴上又摘下，Gustav Bauer拿回自己那份登记，说先按墙上的数领。雪还在下，门一直开着。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-22; reputation 0.03; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-22_

  _effects: unemployment (laid off in the depression); cash -5 (lost wages); condition_

  Lena记得出门时钥匙还在，回来却没了。Anna Bauer说下午看见一个穿灰外套的男人在楼道里转悠，手里晃着一把铜钥匙。两人下楼查看，房门虚掩，抽屉被拉开，攒着买煤的六克朗不见了。Anna Bauer陪着Lena去街角警察岗亭报了案，警察记下灰外套，说会留意。
  _effects: cash -6 (被盗的买煤钱（其中6克朗记入债务）); relation friend with person-anna-bauer; mood -0.09999999999999998_

- **1943.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/subject_yields_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Lena看Emil Berg盆里那几件小衬衫，把自家床单卷起来抱回屋，说等下午再晾。Emil Berg愣了一下，闷头把衣服挂好，收工回来时在Lena门口放了一小包用旧报纸裹的洗衣碱。Lena打开报纸，碱面里还掺着几粒粗盐。
  _effects: relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001361:child; food_

  Lena在雪地里捡到一只缝线开裂的钱包，里面有三克朗、一张彩票和一张写着地址的纸。Lena按地址送回去，开门的老太太说钱包不是她的，却把彩票塞给Lena，说“你拿着吧，我这辈子没中过”。

- **1913.01** `vienna-life-infant-first-smile/smile-at-the-lamp` tags: infancy, family, first, warm
  Josefine Hofer把Anton抱到窗边，煤汽灯罩上有一圈晃动的光。孩子盯着看了很久，忽然笑出声，口水滴在Josefine Hofer的袖口上。屋里的人都停了一下，像是听见了什么了不起的消息。

- **1913.01** `iw18-infancy.first-word::ctx-vienna-1912::milieu-tenement/the-dog-gets-the-first-word` tags: infancy, first_word, memory, family, wonder, eerie, vienna, urban
  Anton追着炉边的狗爬了半间屋，忽然拍着地板喊出狗的名字。狗吓得钻到床下，Josefine Hofer笑得把水壶都放下了；邻居在门外问出了什么事。那天晚上，家里给狗多留了半块面包。
  _effects: mood 0.04000000000000001; food; flag +first_word_dog_name_

- **1913.06** `vienna-life-toddler-first-words/first-word-nonsense` tags: infancy, family, first, comic
  Anton说了一长串谁也听不懂的话，最后用力点头，像是在宣布一件已经决定的事。Josefine Hofer认真听完，也点了点头；孩子满意地爬走了，留下大人们互相看着，忍不住笑。
  _effects: mood 0.04000000000000001; skill_

- **1916.01** `vienna-life-child-family-legend/stolen-inheritance` tags: childhood, family, secret, story
  Friedrich Hofer说，家里本来有一件东西，后来被“更会说话的人”拿走了。Anton问是什么，Friedrich Hofer只说是一块表，表盖里刻着姓。孩子记住了“表盖里刻着姓”，却没有记住那个姓。
  _effects: knowledge “家里有一块表盖里刻着姓的表，被一个更会说话的人拿走了。”; flag +family_legend_watch_

- **1916.12** `vienna-life-child-first-friend/stolen-marble` tags: childhood, friendship, family, comic
  Hedwig把Anton的玻璃弹珠藏进鞋里，被当场看见。Hedwig说只是“替你拿着”，Anton没有揭穿，也没有再拿出第二颗。两个人蹲在墙根，把一只蚂蚁看了很久，谁也没有先回家。
  _effects: relation friend with person-phase15-Hedwig-world-vienna-1912; skill; mood -0.01_

- **1917.07** `statistical_infection`
  Anton在Haus der Tante in Klosterneuburg病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1917.09** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Anton的肺炎没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1917.12** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Anton的肺炎没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1918.01** `statistical_recovery`
  Anton熬过了这场肺炎，能坐起来喝一点汤，但身体还很虚。

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-dragged-docked-pay` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  “你看看这角。”Anna Moser把床单拎起来，煤渣簌簌往下掉。Anton伸手去拍，反而把灰按进布纹里。Anna Moser从工钱里数回两枚，只留一赫勒在Anton手心，让重洗那角。晚饭前Anton一直蹲在井台边，换了两盆水，指甲缝里全是黑泥。
  _effects: cash 0.01 (被扣后只剩一赫勒); mood -0.05; relation acquaintance with person-anna-moser; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-19_

  队伍从面包房门口拐进巷子，Anton把布袋夹在腋下，学着前面人的样子，用胳膊肘顶住后面人的篮子。窗口的伙计舀面粉时，Anton踮脚看着秤，直到半袋面倒进袋口。挤出人群时鞋跟被踩掉，Anton单脚跳了两步，在泥里摸回鞋，索性赤脚走回家。
  _effects: food; skill_

  木窗“咔”地合上，Anton的布袋还敞着口。伙计隔着板缝说下星期再来。Anton在墙根站了一会儿，把硬币排在掌心数了两遍，走到旁边土豆摊前，挑了几个小的，让摊主当面过秤。家里人晚上把钱和土豆倒在桌上，重新数了一遍。
  _effects: food; mood -0.03; skill_

- **1921.01** `vienna-childhood-friends.courtyard-race/won-the-race-lost-a-button` tags: childhood, social, school, street

  _effects: mood 0.05; relation acquaintance with person-josef-bauer_

- **1922.02** `vienna-childhood-street.cigarette-card/card-stolen` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  巷口有人跑过来，肩膀撞在Anton的胳膊上。烟画卡脱手，被风卷到路中间。Anton追了两步，鞋底在湿石板上打滑，手掌先着地。Mitzi从后面追上去，在拐角抓住那男孩的袖子，布条撕下来一截，人还是跑了。
  _effects: mood -0.08000000000000002; relation friend with person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912; condition_

- **1922.04** `vienna-childhood-friends.courtyard-race/won-the-race-lost-a-button` tags: childhood, social, school, street
  晒衣绳下挂着湿床单，孩子们绕着跑，鞋底把水甩到墙上。Anton在第三圈超过Hermann Kral，手先碰到墙根。两颗玻璃弹珠进了Anton口袋，外套第二颗纽扣却崩进下水道。Hermann Kral从二楼窗口骂了一句，扔下一根别针。
  _effects: mood 0.04999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-hermann-kral_

- **1922.11** `p9-childhood-household.strap-by-the-door/took-punishment` tags: childhood, family, household, home, siblings, illness, neighbors
  皮带落在掌心时，Anton的手缩了一下，又伸回去。挨完几下，Anton去墙角拿扫帚，把碎碗片扫进簸箕，瓷片碰着铁皮响。Friedrich Hofer把皮带挂回门后，坐在桌边点了半支烟，烟灰积了很长也没弹。
  _effects: mood -0.04999999999999999; relation kin with person-friedrich-hofer_

- **1923.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-both-families` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  队伍拐弯时，Josef Bauer朝Anton招手。Anton挤过去，替两家把面包领出来，纸包用不同的绳结系住。家里人晚上解开绳结，先掂了掂份量，再问价钱；Anton把每一包的面包数、找零和欠的铜板都说了一遍，家里人听完才把两包分别收进柜子。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-josef-bauer; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9_

  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne捏了捏Anton的虎口，又让Anton张开手指，说骨节还没长开，推硬木会伤手。Friedrich Hofer想再问一句，Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne已经转身去搬木料。Anton退到门槛外，听见刨子重新响起来，木花从门缝里卷到脚边。
  _effects: mood -0.08000000000000002; reputation -0.05; relation kin with person-friedrich-hofer_

  寄养父亲数了数橱柜里的面包，说多一张嘴就是多一份配给。Anna Kral来做回访时，他已经把Anton的行李放在门口。Anton抱着包袱站在雪里，听见屋里Friedrich Hofer在哭，门没有开。
  _effects: mood -0.14; condition; flag +returned_from_foster_placement; relation rival with person-friedrich-hofer; move place-wien-magistrat; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44_
