  Anton赶到时，阅览室门口挂着“盘点停开”的牌子。Anton在台阶上坐到天黑，把带来的笔记背了两遍，回家时鞋底湿透，第二天发起低烧。低烧退了以后，Anton再去，门口换了新牌子：阅览室每天只开三个钟头，Anton把借书证收进抽屉，等秋天再来。
  _effects: mood -0.03999999999999998_

- **1925.11** `r3-teen.police-caution::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-government::reform-parent-notice/fined` tags: police, caution, record, family, work, reform, medical, schooling
  Maria Hofer说按条例罚三克朗，交不出就通知学校。Friedrich Hofer从内衣口袋里摸出叠好的钞票，一张张数，数到第三张时手停了。回家后，那三克朗被写在厨房的账单上，旁边画了一道红线。
  _effects: cash -3 (派出所里的第一次签字); mood -0.08000000000000002; flag +fined_by_police; relation rival with person-friedrich-hofer; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-47_

  Anton的转学材料缺一张居住证明，Friedrich Hofer让Anton去区公所补。Anton跑了三趟，第三趟正赶上下雨，Friedrich Hofer看见Anton把湿透的纸一张张摊在柜台上，没再挑毛病，盖了章。Anton赶在开学前一天把名字写进新学校。那间教室在二楼，Anton第一天就找到了靠窗的座位，把旧课本摆好。
  _effects: flag +school_transfer_approved_

  Anton把借书证压在阅览室的台灯下，一页页抄书。Anton Winter过来提醒闭馆，Anton求了十分钟，把最后一章抄完。回家路上，Anton把纸条贴在胸口，怕雪打湿，进门先烤干手才敢展开。第二天Anton把抄本交给老师，老师翻了两页，说字比课文好看。
  _effects: flag +library_card; skill; knowledge “the library holds an 1890s survey of housing conditions”_

  第四天，Katharina Winter 2的回信从门缝底下塞进来，纸边被门槛磨毛了。Katharina Winter 2约Anton星期六早上在街角面包店门口见，说想买一个两赫勒的小圆面包，两个人分。Anna Bauer 2在楼道里撞见Anton读信，笑着问是不是好消息；Anton把信折回四折，塞进内袋贴着胸口。

- **1932.06** `vienna-legacy-worklife.union-office/union-office.write-off` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  窗外有人来问入会，接待的人一时不知道怎么开口，Anton替他说“进来坐”。老成员把账本拿回去，用一块旧布包好，放进抽屉最里面。抽屉关上的声音比平时响。
  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-6; flag +union_debt_written_off_

- **1932.11** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.remake-coat/remake-coat.left-on-chair` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, mending, clothing, household
  Anton只把外套的里衬拆了一半，就搁在椅背上。冬天天短，她每天回来都看见那件敞着口子的外套，想着明天接着弄，明天又总是有别的事。到开春外套还搭在那儿，呢面上落了一层灰。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; flag +unfinished_coat_

- **1933.01** `oj18-perfume.recipe::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-government/customer-allergy` tags: perfume, shop, recipe, theft, customer, work, odd_job, reform
  Maria Stein试了新到的紫罗兰香精，脖颈当天起了一圈红疹。Anton认出那批香精兑了工业酒精，追到门口把人叫回来，退了钱，又写了一张配方让药房配温和的。Maria Stein后来带来一篮李子，说是自家树上结的。
  _effects: cash -1 (refunded the harsh violet essence); reputation 0.03; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-6; skill; flag +warned_allergy; knowledge “那批紫罗兰香精兑了工业酒精，闻起来甜，擦在皮肤上会起疹。”_

- **1933.01** `state_tick:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/strange-year/blamed-for-the-noise` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  楼上住户投诉夜里有人拖椅子，管事的把巡查簿翻到Anton的那一页，说签字太潦草，看不出巡过几趟。Anton想辩解，却想起那晚确实在楼梯间多站了一刻钟；扣掉的工钱刚好够买一双不漏水的鞋。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 35 -> 19; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.reliability 55 -> 45; mood -0.050000000000000044; flag +odd_job_scare_

- **1933.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.cafe-newspaper/p9-social-daily-culture.cafe-newspaper.read-all` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:daily
  Anton把木架上的《新自由报》从头版读到讣告栏，中间续了两次水，第三杯 Melange 端上来时奶泡已经塌了，三杯一共六十赫勒。侍者把找零的铜板拍在桌上，没有催的意思；Anton折好报纸夹回木架，记住了议会辩论里的两个名字，出门时雨还没停。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (三杯 Melange 的咖啡钱); mood 0.050000000000000044; skill_

- **1933.01** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.sick-parent-night/hire-neighbor-daycare` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Anton请楼下的Katharina Winter白天来照看Friedrich Hofer，先付一周六克朗工钱，讲好每天另管两顿饭，煤钱也从这六克里出。Katharina Winter上午换床单，下午喂药，临走把便盆提到楼道口。Anton下工回来先摸煤箱，再在练习本上记下当天的饭钱和煤钱，看见Friedrich Hofer的嘴唇不再干裂。
  _effects: cash -6 (支付邻居照看工钱和煤钱); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7; health 0.04000000000000001 (白天有人按时喂药和换洗); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7_

- **1933.04** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_found_by_neighbor` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Anton傍晚从面粉厂下工回来，发现挂在腰带上的房门钥匙不见了，在楼梯间来回找了四趟。Friedrich Weiss在二楼拐角的煤堆旁捡到那把铜钥匙，钥匙上缠着红线，一眼就认出来。Friedrich Weiss敲开Anton的门，把钥匙拍在桌上，说自己眼神好使。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23; mood 0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23_

- **1933.05** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.window-argument/window-argument.peek` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, neighbor, overheard
  Anton下楼倒水，在楼梯口看见院子里站着一对夫妻，男人的围裙上还沾着面粉，女的攥着一张工钱条。她没插话，提着壶又上楼，关门时听见那女人说“一星期八克朗怎么够”。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; skill_

- **1934.01** `statistical_infection`
  Anton在Haus der Tante in Klosterneuburg病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1934.01** `oj18-perfume.recipe::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/sold-the-last-violet` tags: perfume, shop, recipe, theft, customer, work, odd_job, vienna
  一个穿旧军装的客人要买“紫罗兰”，说他的未婚妻战前就用这个。Anton翻出最后一瓶存货，瓶塞已经发黄；客人把瓶子贴在胸口，站了很久才问多少钱。Anton按旧价收了，找零时多放了一颗糖。
  _effects: cash 1 (sold the last bottle of violet); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-76; mood -0.030000000000000027; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.strangeKnowledge 15 -> 18; flag +sold_last_violet; knowledge “最后一瓶紫罗兰卖给了穿旧军装的客人，他说未婚妻战前就用这个。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-76_

- **1934.01** `r13-work.inherit-business::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/debts-came-with-it` tags: commerce, inheritance, family_firm, debt, succession, vienna, urban, crisis
  钥匙交到手里第二天，供货商就拿着欠条上门。Friedrich Hofer说那些账是铺子的，不是他的；供货商说铺子现在归Anton，账也归Anton。Anton把存货盘了一遍，能抵的抵，剩下十克朗写了还款计划；印章还在，但盖下去的第一份文件是欠条。
  _effects: cash -10 (父亲的账本和印章); condition; flag +work13_inherited_business_with_debts; role; employment Kaufmann @ taxi:person-maria-kral-evt:world-vienna-1912:000199_

  Anton的肺炎没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1934.07** `statistical_death/pneumonia` tags: death
  Anton在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang发起高烧，咳嗽越来越深。邻居帮忙去请医生，医生到时只留下药粉和一张账单；几天后呼吸变得很浅。Hedwig在场。
  _effects: death (肺炎); role; flag -odd_job_apprentice_

### 172 subject events

- **1913.01** `vienna-life-infant-first-smile/smile-at-the-lamp` tags: infancy, family, first, warm
  Josefine Hofer把Hedwig抱到窗边，煤汽灯罩上有一圈晃动的光。孩子盯着看了很久，忽然笑出声，口水滴在Josefine Hofer的袖口上。屋里的人都停了一下，像是听见了什么了不起的消息。
  _effects: mood 0.039999999999999994; health 0.010000000000000009 (a good day)_

- **1914.01** `vienna-life-toddler-first-words/first-word-door` tags: infancy, family, first, comic
  门外有人上楼，Hedwig忽然指着门说：“爸爸。”Josefine Hofer看了看空门口，没有纠正。那天晚上，孩子把家里每个人的名字都叫了一遍，叫错两个，却叫得很认真。
  _effects: skill; mood 0.01999999999999999; flag +first_word_door_

- **1915.04** `vienna-life-child-family-legend/lost-relative` tags: childhood, family, secret, story
  Friedrich Hofer在收拾箱子时翻出一张没有署名的照片，说那是家里一个“去了很远地方”的人。Hedwig问还回不回来，Friedrich Hofer把照片翻过去，说先把名字记住，别在饭桌上问。
  _effects: knowledge “家里有一张没有署名的照片，照片上的人去了很远的地方。”; flag +family_legend_photo; mood -0.01_

- **1916.01** `vienna-life-child-first-friend/imaginary-friend` tags: childhood, friendship, family, comic
  院子里没有别的孩子，Hedwig给墙上的裂纹起了一个名字，每天吃饭时给它留一个位置。家里的人没有纠正，只在摆碗时多放一只空碗；Lena从门口经过，问Hedwig在等谁。
  _effects: skill; flag +imaginary_friend; relation friend with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912_

- **1916.03** `statistical_recovery`
  Hedwig熬过了这场痢疾，能坐起来喝一点汤，但身体还很虚。

- **1916.05** `p9-childhood-household.fever-night/vinegar-compress` tags: childhood, family, household, home, siblings, illness, neighbors
  没有钱请医生，Friedrich Hofer把醋瓶拎到床边，拿两只碗轮换着兑水。Hedwig烧得嘴唇起皮，Friedrich Hofer用小勺喂了两口温水，又把冷布敷上去。鸡叫时热度退了一点，Hedwig睁开眼，看见Friedrich Hofer趴在床沿，手指还攥着湿布。
  _effects: health 0.03 (湿敷后退了烧); mood 0.02; relation kin with person-friedrich-hofer_

- **1917.08** `iw18-friend.chair-by-the-stove::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/the-adult-calls-it-a-phase` tags: childhood, play, imaginary_friend, solitude, family, wonder, rural, agriculture
  Hedwig说朋友今天不饿，Friedrich Hofer把盘子收走，说“等你长大就好了”。那天下午Hedwig没有再提朋友，只把空椅子推到墙角，又在椅背上搭了一条围巾。晚上熄灯后，Hedwig对着墙角小声说了很久的话。
  _effects: state_add iw18-imaginary-friend state:world-vienna-1912:28; mood -0.020000000000000004; flag +imaginary_friend_denied_by_adult; flag +imaginary_friend_active_

  Hedwig刚走到地窖最后一级台阶，Friedrich Hofer就从煤堆后面站起来，说“上去”。Hedwig转身时看见墙缝里露出一点油纸的角，回到厨房后，Friedrich Hofer把一碗汤推到孩子面前，谁也没有先开口。
  _effects: state_add iw18-child-secret state:world-vienna-1912:27; knowledge “大人从地窖墙缝里站起来，让我立刻上楼。”; relation kin with person-friedrich-hofer; mood -0.04; flag +caught_at_cellar_door; flag +child_secret_active_

- **1917.12** `kernel-child.first-errand::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/edu-errand-good-change` tags: childhood, family, market, money, errand, rural, agriculture, small_town
  Friedrich Hofer把两枚十字币放进Hedwig手心，让去街角买盐和半块面包。Hedwig把钱攥出汗，先数了一遍，又在柜台前重数一遍。Anna Moser把面包用纸包好，找回一枚铜币；Hedwig把铜币含在嘴里跑回家，咸味一直留到门口。

  Hedwig把铁盒端到桌上，说里面的钱对不上，可能是自己数错了。Friedrich Hofer把硬币倒出来，一枚枚排开，排到最后少了一枚；Friedrich Hofer没有问是谁拿的，只说“明天少买一次煤”。Hedwig低头把硬币又数了一遍，这次数得很慢。

- **1919.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-both-families` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  队伍拐弯时，Josef Bauer朝Hedwig招手。Hedwig挤过去，替两家把面包领出来，纸包用不同的绳结系住。家里人晚上解开绳结，先掂了掂份量，再问价钱；Hedwig把每一包的面包数、找零和欠的铜板都说了一遍，家里人听完才把两包分别收进柜子。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-josef-bauer; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10_

- **1919.01** `state_reaction:iw18-child-secret/a-school-note-reminds` tags: iw18-child-secret, childhood, secret, knowledge, guilt, family, fear, state
  Hedwig把老师要家长签字的纸条折成四折，忽然想起自己还有一件事没有告诉家里。纸条在口袋里放了一路，边角被手指捏得发亮。
  _effects: state_parameter iw18-child-secret.tellUrge 51 -> 55; state_parameter iw18-child-secret.weight 73 -> 75_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:001009_

- **1919.07** `statistical_infection`
  Hedwig在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1919.08** `statistical_recovery`
  Hedwig从肺炎中慢慢恢复，能下床喝水，但身体还虚弱。

- **1920.01** `state_expire:iw18-child-secret` tags: iw18-child-secret, childhood, secret, knowledge, guilt, family, fear, state
  Hedwig的「孩子藏在口袋里的事」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove iw18-child-secret state:world-vienna-1912:27; flag -child_secret_active_

- **1920.01** `state_expire:iw18-imaginary-friend` tags: iw18-imaginary-friend, childhood, play, imaginary_friend, solitude, imagination, secret, state
  Hedwig的「一个只有孩子看得见的朋友」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove iw18-imaginary-friend state:world-vienna-1912:28; flag -imaginary_friend_active_

- **1920.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-bread-with-friend` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  Anton Winter家的孩子站在绳子另一头，Hedwig递夹子时故意慢半拍，两个人把床单拉得没有一道褶。收工后Anton Winter端出半包隔夜面包，掰开时掉了一桌屑。Hedwig把分到的那块用围裙兜住，回家路上闻着麦味，硬是没咬。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-20; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-20_

- **1921.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-half-flour` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  队伍从面包房门口拐进巷子，Hedwig把布袋夹在腋下，学着前面人的样子，用胳膊肘顶住后面人的篮子。窗口的伙计舀面粉时，Hedwig踮脚看着秤，直到半袋面倒进袋口。挤出人群时鞋跟被踩掉，Hedwig单脚跳了两步，在泥里摸回鞋，索性赤脚走回家。
  _effects: food; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-27_

- **1922.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-bread-with-friend` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  两个孩子在院子两头跑，一个递，一个夹，床单在风里鼓起来。最后一条挂好，Hermann Hofer从厨房拿出面包，先给了自家孩子，再掰一块给Hedwig。Hedwig把面包塞进围裙口袋，回家后放在家里人的碗边，说自己在外面吃过了。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-32_

- **1923.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-sold-out-potatoes` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  “卖完了。”三个字从窗口里传出来，Hedwig后面的队伍立刻散了一半。Hedwig没走，趴在窗台上看伙计扫面屑，直到人家挥手赶人。旁边摊子的土豆带着泥，Hedwig用围裙兜住，一枚一枚数铜板，回家报账时连找零都说清了。
  _effects: food; mood -0.03; skill_

- **1923.03** `p9-childhood-adolescence.church-club/club-one-visit-only` tags: adolescence, friendship, identity, work, school, secret, church
  Hedwig去了一个下午，看那些旧歌本里夹着褪色的圣像卡，工厂来的几个年轻人在最后一排用手指敲长凳打拍子。第二个星期天Hedwig没再去，在院子里帮母亲晾被单。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-mitzi-lechner; mood 0.010000000000000009; skill_

- **1924.08** `r15-work.master-takes-on::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/signed-contract` tags: work, craft, apprentice, trade, poverty, reform, medical, schooling
  Katharina Huber把学徒契约摊在桌上，指给Hedwig看期限、工钱和出师考试。Hedwig签了名，第二天站到刨床前，手指上多了第一道木刺。
  _effects: relation employer with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-45; state_add apprenticeship-contract state:world-vienna-1912:68; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-45; education apprenticeship; role_

- **1925.01** `state_tick:apprenticeship-contract/workshop-year/learned` tags: apprenticeship-contract, education, craft, apprentice, work, state, state_tick
  Hedwig又学会了一道工序。师傅没有夸，只把下一块料推到面前。
  _effects: state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.progress 10 -> 30; skill; mood 0.01999999999999999_

- **1925.01** `r13-edu.boarding-school::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/admitted-to-dormitory` tags: education, boarding_school, residence, family, scholarship, reform, medical, schooling
  Hedwig把铁皮箱绑在自行车后座，骑了三十里到寄宿学校。舍监检查行李，把家里的腌菜罐没收，说会招老鼠。Hedwig在熄灯后偷偷把罐子从垃圾堆捡回来，藏在床底，第一顿饭就着腌菜吃完了两碗粥。第一个星期Hedwig把腌菜分给同屋，换回三张邮票和一支不漏水的钢笔。
  _effects: flag +boarding_school_admitted; education secondary_

- **1925.03** `yf18-friend.two-tickets::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/confront-at-the-cloakroom` tags: first_love, friendship, betrayal, dance, tickets, reform, medical, schooling
  Hedwig在衣帽间堵住Klara，问那两张票怎么到了Josef手里。Klara说Hedwig从来没敢开口，自己只是替Hedwig把话说了；Hedwig把票根揉成一团，塞进Klara的西装口袋，转身时撞翻了侍者的托盘。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 60 -> 65; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.talk 0 -> 10; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 5 -> 0; relation friend with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912; relation friend with person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912; reputation -0.02; mood -0.05_

- **1925.04** `yf18-firstlook.tram-glove::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/keep-it-in-the-satchel` tags: first_love, love, tram, glove, youth, courtship, reform, medical
  Hedwig把手套塞进书包最里层，回家后放在枕头下面。第二天上学，Hedwig每隔一会儿就摸一下书包带，像在确认一件不能给家里看见的东西；手套上还留着电车木座的气味。
  _effects: state_add yf18-first-awakening state:world-vienna-1912:92; flag +yf18_kept_glove; mood 0.020000000000000004; flag +yf18_first_awakening_active_

- **1925.05** `yf18-dare.bridge-rail::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/fall-on-the-ice` tags: dare, bridge, river, friendship, risk, reform, medical, schooling
  Hedwig走到第四步，鞋底在霜上滑了一下，整个人挂在栏杆外侧。Anton抓住Hedwig的手腕，Elisabeth Bauer跑去叫桥头卖烤栗子的老人；Hedwig被拉上来时，膝盖已经湿透，第二天走路一瘸一拐。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 65 -> 71; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 0 -> 0; flag +yf18_bridge_scare; mood -0.03_

- **1925.09** `yf18-dare.prater-slide::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/sell-the-story` tags: dare, prater, fairground, comic, friendship, reform, medical, schooling
  第二天，Hedwig在教室里把滑道说成三层楼高、底下是鳄鱼池。Elisabeth Bauer配合着补充细节，连Elisabeth Berg的灯都被说成探照灯；全班听得入神，只有后排的Josef问了一句“那你为什么裤子破了”。
  _effects: reputation 0.03; mood 0.03; relation friend with person-elisabeth-bauer; flag +yf18_prater_story; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-25_

- **1926.01** `state_tick:apprenticeship-contract/workshop-year/learned` tags: apprenticeship-contract, education, craft, apprentice, work, state, state_tick
  Hedwig又学会了一道工序。师傅没有夸，只把下一块料推到面前。
  _effects: state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.progress 30 -> 50; skill; mood 0.01999999999999999_

- **1926.01** `state_tick:yf18-first-awakening/half-year-of-glances/answered-glance` tags: yf18-first-awakening, love, youth, first_love, desire, school, awakening, state
  Hedwig在教堂门口或学校走廊里又遇见Elisabeth Bauer。这一次对方先点了头，Hedwig准备好的整段话全忘了，只把手里的书换到另一只手上；回家后Hedwig才发现，自己把书拿倒了。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 71 -> 76; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 0 -> 8; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.reciprocated false -> true; relation friend with person-elisabeth-bauer; mood 0.03_

- **1926.01** `r13-civ.first-aid-course::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/certificate-earned` tags: health, first_aid, course, skill, school, reform, medical, schooling
  Rosa Moser把一条绷带绕在Hedwig的小臂上，教怎么打结才不会勒住血管。Hedwig在同桌的手臂上练了三遍，最后一遍得到了点头。课程结束时，Rosa Moser发了一张油印证书，上面盖着红十字的章。
  _effects: flag +earned_first_aid_certificate; role; skill; knowledge “a bandage must be tight enough to hold but loose enough for ”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9_

- **1926.05** `r1-fam.sibling-rivalry::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/rivalry.neither` tags: family, siblings, rivalry, apprenticeship, craft, reform, medical, schooling
  Katharina Huber看了看兄弟俩的手，说两个人的指节都不够硬，谁也不收。Friedrich Hofer求了半天，Katharina Huber答应让两人一起干杂活，管饭不给钱。Anton干了三天就走了，Hedwig留下来扫地、磨刀，磨到第六个月，Katharina Huber才让她碰第一块好木料。
  _effects: cash 1 (同一把刨子); flag +worked_up_from_dogsbody; role; skill; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Anton-world-vienna-1912_

- **1926.12** `kernel-edu.friendship::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/edu-friend-moves-away` tags: adolescence, school, friendship, books, family, reform, medical, schooling
  Klara的父亲在车站找到活，全家要搬去另一区。临走前，Klara把一本旧书塞给Hedwig，扉页上写着地址。Hedwig把书读完，按地址写了信，信被退回来，上面盖着“查无此人”。那本旧书Hedwig一直留在书架上。
  _effects: relation_end friend with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912; knowledge “朋友搬走前留下的地址已经找不到人了。”; skill; flag +friend_moved_away; mood -0.05_

- **1927.01** `vienna-life-apprenticeship/kept-at-home` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship
  家里算了一笔账：学徒工钱扣掉车费和饭钱，剩不下多少。Hedwig留在家里接缝纫店的零活，顺便照看弟弟妹妹。
  _effects: role; cash 2 (home piecework)_

- **1927.01** `yf18-apprentice.press-guard::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/refuse-the-machine` tags: apprenticeship, work, danger, machine, injury, refusal, reform, medical
  教堂年历的订单催得紧，Magistrat der k. k. Reichshaupt- und Residenzstadt Wien把压印机的护板卸了，说这样进纸快一半。Hedwig看着那块铁板，说手会进去；Magistrat der k. k. Reichshaupt- und Residenzstadt Wien把工钱袋往桌上一扔，问Hedwig是不是不想出师了。Hedwig没有碰机器，那天下午的活由Katharina Berg接手。
  _effects: relation employer with person-gustav-berg; state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.progress 50 -> 45; state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.failures 0 -> 1; flag +yf18_refused_unguarded_press; mood 0.020000000000000004; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17_

- **1927.01** `state_tick:apprenticeship-contract/workshop-year/mistake` tags: apprenticeship-contract, education, craft, apprentice, work, state, state_tick
  Hedwig废掉了一块料，师傅扣了工钱。晚饭时谁也没提这件事。
  _effects: state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.failures 1 -> 2; cash -1 (wasted material); mood -0.04000000000000001_

- **1927.01** `state_tick:yf18-first-awakening/half-year-of-glances/answered-glance` tags: yf18-first-awakening, love, youth, first_love, desire, school, awakening, state
  Hedwig在教堂门口或学校走廊里又遇见Elisabeth Bauer。这一次对方先点了头，Hedwig准备好的整段话全忘了，只把手里的书换到另一只手上；回家后Hedwig才发现，自己把书拿倒了。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 76 -> 81; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 8 -> 16; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.reciprocated true -> true; relation friend with person-elisabeth-bauer; mood 0.03_

- **1927.01** `r13-edu.boarding-school::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/homesick-return` tags: education, boarding_school, residence, family, scholarship, reform, medical, schooling
  Hedwig在寄宿学校住了不到一个月，夜里总听见火车声，想起家里的炉子和妹妹。Hedwig给Friedrich Hofer写信，写了撕、撕了写，最后只寄出一张明信片。Friedrich Hofer来接Hedwig那天，Hedwig的箱子还没完全打开。回到家，Hedwig把铁皮箱放在床底，再没打开，腌菜罐却一直留在桌上。
  _effects: mood -0.04000000000000001; relation kin with person-friedrich-hofer_

- **1927.04** `r3-teen.first-rent::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::reform-rent-receipt/pays` tags: housing, rent, arrears, eviction, independence, reform, medical, schooling
  Otto Winter 2把收据铺在油布上，用蘸水笔写日期。Hedwig把硬币一枚枚码好，推到桌子对面，听见最后一枚碰到桌沿。Otto Winter 2点完，把收据撕下来，说下个月还是这一天。
  _effects: cash -6 (第一张房租收据); flag +paid_first_rent; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-54; knowledge “rent is due on the same day each month”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-54; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13_

- **1928.01** `yf18-firstlook.parish-dance::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/friend-cuts-in` tags: first_love, dance, church, courtship, jealousy, reform, medical, schooling
  Klara在第二支曲子前把Josef Moser请走，说Hedwig答应过要替她看管外套。Hedwig抱着一件不属于自己的大衣站在圣像下面，看他们在蜡烛光里转圈；回家路上，Hedwig把舞卡撕成小片，扔进多瑙运河。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 81 -> 86; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 16 -> 8; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.talk 10 -> 15; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-61; mood -0.04000000000000001; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-61_

  Hedwig从「学徒契约」转入另一段处境：the master ended the contract
  _effects: state_transition apprenticeship-contract -> failed-apprentice; flag +failed_apprenticeship; mood -0.08000000000000002; role_

  Hedwig只把Elisabeth Bauer的名字写在一张草稿纸背面，第二天那张纸就在教室后排传了一圈。有人把名字念出来，有人问Hedwig是不是要请人喝咖啡；Hedwig说那是表姐的名字，声音大得连自己都吓了一跳。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.talk 15 -> 27; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 8 -> 2; reputation -0.03; mood -0.030000000000000027_

  Hedwig跟着Elisabeth Bauer走进印刷厂后面的屋子，交了十赫勒会费，在借书簿上按了手印。他挑了一本讲蒸汽机的册子，封面烫金已经磨掉；管理员说下月十赫勒要按时交，书才能续借。
  _effects: cash -0.1 (工人青年会月费); skill; relation friend with person-elisabeth-bauer; flag +workers_youth_member; mood 0.03999999999999998_

  Maria Stein在俱乐部后间放了一张留声机，让Hedwig和Klara跳一支舞。窗帘没有拉严，对面楼里有人看见了；第二天，Hedwig的母亲问她昨晚是不是去了“那种地方”，Hedwig说去听了音乐。
  _effects: state_add ld18-public-desire state:world-vienna-1912:188; mood 0.03999999999999998; flag +danced_in_the_back_room; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-6; flag +public_desire_active_

- **1928.08** `r1-cul.fan-letter::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/hid-letter-in-bookshop` tags: culture, letters, fandom, literacy, ambition, reform, medical, schooling
  Hedwig没有寄信，把信塞进Otto Winter常去的书店，夹在一本旧书里。三个月后，书被卖掉，信也不见了；Otto Winter 2说那家书店上个月清过仓，旧书论斤卖。Hedwig去废纸站找了一下午，只找到半张写着我不敢寄的纸。
  _effects: cash -1 (寄给作者的信); mood -0.09999999999999998; condition; flag +hid_letter_in_bookshop; knowledge “the bookshop sold its old stock by weight”_

  故事又换了一个版本：有人说Hedwig是为了钱，有人说是为了爱，还有人说她只是喜欢看别人议论。Hedwig在咖啡馆听见第三种说法，没有更正，只把杯子往窗边挪了挪；阳光照进来，她第一次觉得这些版本都离自己很远。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-public-desire.rumorHeat 35 -> 31; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.spectacle 30 -> 32; mood 0.010000000000000009_

  _effects: skill; knowledge “a bandage must be tight enough to hold but loose enough for ”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-49_

- **1929.01** `oj18-cinema.reel::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/burned-frame` tags: cinema, film, projection, night, censorship, work, odd_job, reform
  胶片在片门里起火，火苗顺着观察窗舔进大厅。Hedwig没有先去拿灭火沙，而是把后排的孩子往侧门推；Alois Bauer用湿毯子捂住片盒，手背烧掉一层皮。第二天，Sozialdemokratische Arbeiterpartei Österreichs (SDAP)把烧黑的片盒留在柜台上，说“留着，提醒我们”。
  _effects: condition; reputation 0.05; state_add oj18-odd-apprenticeship state:world-vienna-1912:214; cash 1 (emergency pay for the fire shift); flag +cinema_fire; knowledge “硝酸片起火时，观察窗像一只被点燃的眼睛。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-42; role; flag +odd_job_apprentice_

- **1929.07** `vienna-church-community.tenement-prayer/rent-prayer-help` tags: faith, social, family, home
  Hedwig在楼梯间守夜时，邻居说起楼下老太太病重。
  _effects: condition; food; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Rosa-world-vienna-1912_

- **1929.08** `vienna-housing-move.sublet-room/sublet-daylight-only` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Hedwig和Elisabeth Lechner 2谈妥只出租白天，夜里不留宿，收两克朗。床仍归Hedwig家，钥匙只在白天给。Elisabeth Lechner 2说不碍事，他住的工棚不许带人，白天有个落脚的地方就够。Ferdinand Winter听了点头，说这样少些闲话；到了傍晚，Hedwig把床铺重新收好，屋里照旧几口人。
  _effects: cash 2 (白天出租床位的钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-37; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-37_

- **1930.01** `curse15-betrayal.name-on-the-list::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/denounced` tags: betrayal, politics, investigation, curse, denunciation, secret, reform, medical
  Rosa Moser把Hedwig在夜里说过的话整理成三页纸，交到了办事处。纸上没有一句是假的，但每一句都离开了原来的房间。第二天，Hedwig被叫去问话，问话的人已经知道该问什么。Rosa Moser在走廊里遇见Hedwig，点了头，像只是借过一支笔。
  _effects: state_add suspected-witchcraft state:world-vienna-1912:213; reputation -0.06; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9; flag +denounced_by_known_person; mood -0.04999999999999999_

- **1930.01** `state_reaction:suspected-witchcraft/another-name` tags: suspected-witchcraft, witchcraft, rumor, religion, legal, reputation, fear, supernatural
  Rosa Moser在指控Hedwig的人面前添了一句话，说Hedwig曾经在夜里念过谁的名字。那句话没有证据，但登记册上又多了一行。Hedwig后来才知道，添这句话的人就是Rosa Moser。
  _effects: state_parameter suspected-witchcraft.evidence 20 -> 30; reputation -0.03; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:004130_

- **1930.01** `state_expire:ld18-public-desire` tags: ld18-public-desire, desire, public, scandal, reputation, body, rumor, notoriety
  Hedwig的「被全城观看的欲望」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove ld18-public-desire state:world-vienna-1912:188; flag -public_desire_active_

- **1930.01** `state_tick:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/strange-year/blamed-for-the-noise` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  楼上住户投诉夜里有人拖椅子，管事的把巡查簿翻到Hedwig的那一页，说签字太潦草，看不出巡过几趟。Hedwig想辩解，却想起那晚确实在楼梯间多站了一刻钟；扣掉的工钱刚好够买一双不漏水的鞋。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 40 -> 24; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.reliability 65 -> 55; mood -0.04999999999999999; flag +odd_job_scare_

- **1930.01** `task-vienna-family-care.courtship-invitation/walk-with-sister` tags: family, care, courtship, siblings, inheritance, housing, task, task:family
  Hedwig把纸袋夹在腋下，问Emil星期天愿不愿去多瑙运河边走一段。对方低头理了理围裙，答应下来，还说明妹妹会陪着走前半段，到桥头就回家。Hedwig当场数出六个赫勒，在面包店买了两块小甜饼，说好两点在桥边等。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912; cash -0.06 (买了两块小甜饼)_

- **1930.03** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-demand-repair` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Hedwig没有立刻答话，先去敲Helene Winter的门。两人一起数落墙上的水渍和门框里灌进来的风，楼梯间的窗户修好了，自家墙皮还在剥落。Helene Winter提议一起把话带给看门人：先修好漏水的墙角，涨租的事再谈。Hedwig把纸重新折好，请他把话带到门外。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-helene-winter; mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +rent_dispute_

- **1930.07** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_burns_curtain` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Hedwig在公用灶台上热猪油，油星子溅到旁边晾着的床单上，火苗顺着床单爬上木隔板。Friedrich Weiss拎起水桶泼过去，火灭了，但床单烧掉半幅，隔板熏黑一片。房东扣了那户人家两克朗押金，那家的女人在楼道里哭了一下午。
  _effects: cash -2 (赔给房东的隔板熏黑损失); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23_

- **1930.09** `vienna-parish-aid.sick-call/sick-call.door-only` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Hedwig把药包和半块肥皂交给开门的孩子，嘱咐用水冲开、一天两次，就下了楼。楼梯间里孩子一直站在门口看着，手里攥着纸包，没关门。Hedwig走到二层又折回去，帮他把门带上，说自己家里也等着火。
  _effects: health 0.01 (没有久留，避开了病人的屋子); relation acquaintance with person-friedrich-hofer; mood -0.02999999999999997_

- **1931.01** `curse15-aftermath.the-inquiry::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/a-statement-is-taken` tags: witchcraft, investigation, reputation, rumor, legal, aftermath, reform, medical
  Gustav Berg把Hedwig叫进办公室，桌上摊着Elisabeth Lechner 2交来的三页证词。Hedwig逐条回答，Gustav Berg逐条记录，写到“是否曾诅咒他人”时停了很久。最后Hedwig被允许回家，但门口多了一个每月来一次的访客。
  _effects: state_parameter suspected-witchcraft.danger 25 -> 50; state_parameter suspected-witchcraft.evidence 30 -> 40; condition; reputation -0.05; relation acquaintance with person-gustav-berg; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-37; mood -0.03999999999999998_

  一年过去，Hedwig没有再点蜡烛，也没有再去那家后间。教堂的登记册翻到了新的一页，旧页边缘留着指甲划过的痕迹；街上的人开始讨论别的事。
  _effects: state_parameter suspected-witchcraft.evidence 40 -> 32; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1931.01** `r13-fam.repair-friendship::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/cup-refused` tags: task, social, friendship, repair, trust, reform, medical, schooling
  Anna Moser 2把杯子推回来，说摔坏的东西粘得再好也有缝。Hedwig在门口站了一会儿，把杯子放进外套口袋，杯沿磕在扣子上，响了一声。回家的路上，Hedwig绕开了两人常走的那条街。
  _effects: mood -0.12; flag +friendship_unrepaired; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44_

- **1931.02** `ad20-stranger-request::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/take-the-envelope` tags: stranger, envelope, request, everyday, obligation, risk, reform, medical
  Ferdinand Huber在巷口拦住Hedwig，把一个没有字的信封塞过来，说“明天中午之前，别打开”。Hedwig问里面是什么；Ferdinand Huber说“你不需要知道”。信封很薄，捏起来却像有一块硬东西。
  _effects: state_add ad20-private-bargain state:world-vienna-1912:274; cash 2 (payment for carrying the envelope); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-51; flag +envelope_taken; mood -0.010000000000000009; knowledge “信封很薄，里面像有一块硬东西。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-51; flag +private_bargain_active_

- **1931.04** `p9-social-courtship-friendship.fasching-dance/dance-cloakroom-scarf` tags: courtship, friendship, social, adulthood, reputation, leopoldstadt
  衣帽间里两条围巾缠在一起，Franz Stein那条被别人的大衣压住了。Hedwig弯腰把围巾抽出来递过去，说了自己每周六在哪家铺子做工。看管费加一杯热茶正好一克朗；Franz Stein把围巾接过去，没说谢，只问了一句铺子几点关门。
  _effects: cash -1 (衣帽间看管费与一杯热茶); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-79; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-79_

- **1931.09** `vienna-women-housework.wash-day/wash-day.send-out` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  洗衣妇把工服从报纸里抖出来，翻开领口看了看油垢，点头收下。Hedwig转身回屋，铜板在围裙口袋里磕出轻响。Anna Moser 2还在楼梯上嘟囔，说这钱不如买半斤猪油。
  _effects: cash -0.5 (请洗衣妇代洗工服); mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44_

  扁豆汤的香味从门缝飘出去，Franz Weiss敲了门。Hedwig掀开锅盖又盖上，说只够自家吃。Franz Weiss什么也没说就走了。夜里Hedwig听见隔壁有人用勺子刮碗底。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-28; mood -0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-28_

  Hedwig把洗好的床单搭上院子里的公用晾衣绳，占了整条。Friedrich Weiss抱着一盆湿衣服下来，说自家孩子明天要穿干净衬衫上学。两人在绳子两头扯了一阵，最后Hedwig把床单对折，腾出一半，Friedrich Weiss用木夹子把衬衫一件件排上去。谁也没道歉，但第二天见面都点了头。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:004512_

  _effects: cash -2 (诊所走廊的第三张长凳); flag +clinic_fee_recorded_as_debt; relation acquaintance with person-gustav-berg; knowledge “the outpatient fee can be recorded against next month’s wage”_

- **1932.07** `vienna-housing-move.flat-viewing/flat-viewing-too-damp` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Hedwig在单间里站了一刻钟，脚底发潮，墙纸底下透出一股霉味。看门人说刷一层石灰就好，Marie Huber却在门口轻轻咳了一声，提醒这背面没有阳光。Hedwig把定金收回口袋，谢过看门人，宁可多付三克朗，也不愿把湿气带进夜里。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-marie-huber; flag +still_looking_for_room_

  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; food; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  Anna Lechner 2提着药箱走了三里地，鞋上全是泥。进门后她先摸了摸炉子，说水不够热。Hedwig一趟趟往灶里添柴，听见Lena在里屋喊。孩子天亮前落地，Anna Lechner 2在门槛上坐了很久才站起来。
  _effects: cash -2 (夜里敲接生婆的门); flag +rural_midwife_walked_to_birth; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-46; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-55_

  Hedwig从一九〇四年的画报上抄下一段描写雨夜的话，把地名和人名换掉，寄给一家征文比赛。抄的时候，Hedwig告诉自己只借这一小段；寄出去之后，却在邮局门口站了很久，反复回想那段话里有没有一个词是别人一眼就能认出来的。

  Friedrich Hofer问起工钱和房租，Hedwig报了个数，又补了一句冬天活少。桌上一时只剩刀叉声。Hermann Hofer低着头把面包掰成小块，Friedrich Hofer往汤里又加了一勺盐，谁都没再开口；这顿饭把两个人的将来问成了一个没答完的数目。

  Anton Winter把押金收据和住院单钉在一起，让Hedwig去三楼东侧等床。走廊里全是折叠床，Alois Moser 2给Hedwig一条洗得发硬的毯子，说今晚先别喝水。Hedwig把鞋塞在枕头下面，听着隔壁的呻吟睡了过去。
  _effects: cash -8 (住院处窗口的押金); health 0.05999999999999999 (住院处窗口的押金); condition; flag +hospitalized_third_class; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-86; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-86_

- **1934.02** `vienna-market-household.friday-fish/friday-fish.share` tags: home, market, food, social
  Hedwig拎着鱼上楼，看见Otto Lechner蹲在门口用刀刮锅底。Hedwig把鱼放在两家之间的窗台上，切了一半推过去。第二天门口多了一小袋面粉。
  _effects: cash -6 (买鱼); food; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000089:child_

- **1934.04** `hs18-queue.figure::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/pretend-not-to-see` tags: historical_figure, queue, recognition, bread, witness, history, vienna, urban
  Hedwig把头转向橱窗，假装在看一双标价离谱的鞋。Friedrich Hofer从队伍旁走过，靴跟敲得很慢。Karl Kraus往前挪了一步，帽檐仍压着；两人之间隔着三个人和一整条街的沉默。
  _effects: state_add hs18-street-witness state:world-vienna-1912:373; knowledge “保持沉默也需要记住每一个细节。”; flag +ignored_historical_figure_in_queue; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1935.01** `vienna-family-divorce/reconcile-for-now` tags: family, marriage, divorce, housing, lifecycle, partnership
  Hedwig和Hermann Hofer在楼道里吵到半夜，最后因为下个月的房租又坐回同一张桌。谁都没有提和好，只把账本重新摊开；Hermann Hofer把煤钱推过来，Hedwig接过去数了两遍，手指碰到一起又分开，屋里的灯亮到很晚。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1935.01** `fm17-confession.velvet-box::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/a-penitent-with-a-ledger` tags: faith, confession, suspected_witchcraft, investigation, priest, secret, doubt, vienna
  Hedwig没有说自己施了咒，只说害怕。Karl Kraus听完，从丝绒匣里取出一张空白纸，问Hedwig要不要给地方法官写一封信，把一切说清楚。Hedwig摇头；他把纸放回去，说：“那就先活着，别一个人待着。”
  _effects: mood 0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person-karl-kraus; state_parameter suspected-witchcraft.evidence 25 -> 22; flag +fm17_confession_kept_close_

- **1935.01** `state_tick:suspected-witchcraft/rumor-season/whispers-fade` tags: suspected-witchcraft, witchcraft, rumor, religion, legal, reputation, fear, supernatural
  一年过去，Hedwig没有再点蜡烛，也没有再去那家后间。教堂的登记册翻到了新的一页，旧页边缘留着指甲划过的痕迹；街上的人开始讨论别的事。
  _effects: state_parameter suspected-witchcraft.evidence 22 -> 14; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  _effects: state_parameter fa17-plagiarist.exposure 6 -> 18; state_parameter fa17-plagiarist.guilt 30 -> 38; reputation -0.03; mood -0.050000000000000044_

  _effects: state_parameter hs18-street-witness.certainty 75 -> 67; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1935.01** `kernel-health.task-house-call::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::house-call-medical-students/house-call-medical-teaching` tags: health, task, house_call, care, poverty, vienna, urban, crisis
  医院派来一名实习医生，身后跟着两个学生。他们把Hedwig的胸口听了个遍，最后请来上级；药费减半，但Hedwig被当成了讲课的病例。学生们走后，实习医生把听诊器在手里捂了很久，才敢再贴上来。
  _effects: cash -4 (reduced fee for a teaching-house call); health 0.03 (examined by a teaching team); condition; role; knowledge “教学出诊会有实习医生和学生轮流听诊，最后请上级定方案，药费可以减半。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18_

- **1935.03** `vienna-parish-aid.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.take-and-carry` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  汤是豆子熬的，表面浮着几粒油星，气味把整条巷子都占了。Maria Stein排在前面，回头替Hedwig看着位置，嘴里数着还剩几个人。铁罐递过去时烫手，Hedwig用围裙角垫着。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-6; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1935.09** `vienna-legacy-worklife.hours-ledger/hours-ledger.refuse` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  学徒在旁边小声说，拒绝按手印的人名字会被单独抄在一张纸上。Hedwig没接话，把八克朗换成一克朗的硬币，数给家里时硬币在桌上滚了两圈才停。屋里的煤油灯芯剪得太短，光只够照亮手边的桌面。
  _effects: cash 8 (追回的拖欠计件工资); reputation -0.03; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17_

  _effects: cash -2 (临时找住处与搬家的费用); relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-36; reputation -0.04000000000000001; flag +rent_book_taken; condition; mood -0.09999999999999998; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-36_

  _effects: condition; state_parameter cursed-by-wish.triggers 0 -> 1; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  _effects: state_parameter under-investigation.progress 10 -> 25; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000563:child; flag +claimed_repair_records_burned; knowledge “谎话最怕被问月份。”; mood -0.050000000000000044_

  _effects: state_parameter suspected-witchcraft.danger 20 -> 30; reputation -0.04000000000000001; cash -1 (绕远路增加的车钱); mood -0.040000000000000036_

  _effects: cash -0.3 (自己那一杯咖啡); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-22; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-36; mood 0.010000000000000009_

  _effects: health -0.03 (屋里整天没生旺火，受冻); relation acquaintance with person-marie-huber_

- **1936.02** `vienna-medical-treatment.sanatorium/sanatorium-go` tags: health, medical, family, money

  _effects: cash -12 (疗养院车票和住宿押金); health 0.12000000000000001 (疗养院松林空气和正规休养); relation friend with person-friedrich-hofer_

  Hedwig看Anna Bauer盆里那几件小衬衫，把自家床单卷起来抱回屋，说等下午再晾。Anna Bauer愣了一下，闷头把衣服挂好，收工回来时在Hedwig门口放了一小包用旧报纸裹的洗衣碱。Hedwig打开报纸，碱面里还掺着几粒粗盐。
  _effects: relation friend with person-anna-bauer; food_

  Friedrich Hofer把Hedwig叫进办公室，桌上摊着Alois Moser 2交来的三页证词。Hedwig逐条回答，Friedrich Hofer逐条记录，写到“是否曾诅咒他人”时停了很久。最后Hedwig被允许回家，但门口多了一个每月来一次的访客。
  _effects: state_parameter suspected-witchcraft.danger 25 -> 50; state_parameter suspected-witchcraft.evidence 12 -> 22; reputation -0.04999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-friedrich-hofer; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-86; mood -0.040000000000000036_

- **1939.01** `state_tick:suspected-witchcraft/rumor-season/new-accuser` tags: suspected-witchcraft, witchcraft, rumor, religion, legal, reputation, fear, supernatural
  又一个人站了出来，说自家的母牛在Hedwig经过之后不再产奶。Hedwig没有辩解，因为辩解本身也需要被登记。Hedwig开始绕远路去市场，每月多出几枚铜币的车钱。
  _effects: state_parameter suspected-witchcraft.danger 50 -> 60; reputation -0.03999999999999998; cash -1 (绕远路增加的车钱)_

- **1939.01** `state_expire:under-investigation` tags: under-investigation, legal, police, investigation, risk, state, state_tick, state:under-investigation
  Hedwig的「被调查」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove under-investigation state:world-vienna-1912:464_

- **1939.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.singing-club/p9-social-daily-culture.singing-club.ask-fare` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:social
  Hedwig向Josef问清下个月郊外演唱会的车费由谁出，Josef说团里只贴一半，另一半要团员自己凑。Hedwig把四十赫勒硬币在指间转了两圈，最终没有放进收款盒，说等发了工钱再来；这一来一去，也算清了半票、酒棚和车钱各是多少。出门时Mitzi Moser正扛着啤酒桶进后厅，两人侧身让过。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912; skill_

- **1939.06** `vienna-legacy-worklife.sick-fund/sick-fund.fill-form` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  表格有四栏，第四栏要写“工种及所属行会”。Hedwig问工友平时怎么称呼自己的活，工友靠在机器旁边说“就是上油”。办事员的印章是木柄的，按下去时咔的一声。
  _effects: relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33_

- **1940.01** `df18-curse.name-in-the-door::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/name-and-date` tags: curse, name, door, omen, neighbor, dark, supernatural, vienna
  Hedwig在自家门框上看见一行新刻的字，是自己的名字，下面还有当天的日期。刀痕很浅，像是用钥匙划的。Anton Winter说不是他，Hedwig也没有再问。那天夜里，Hedwig把门闩插了两遍，听见楼梯上有脚步声停了一下。
  _effects: knowledge “门框上出现了我的名字和当天的日期。”; flag +df18_name_carved_on_door_

- **1940.01** `state_expire:suspected-witchcraft` tags: suspected-witchcraft, witchcraft, rumor, religion, legal, reputation, fear, supernatural
  Hedwig的「被疑巫术」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove suspected-witchcraft state:world-vienna-1912:457_

- **1940.01** `p9-social-relationship-initiative.introduce-two-people/let-them-talk` tags: courtship, friendship, family, social, initiative, housing, task, task:social
  星期天下午窗边三把椅子，介绍只用了两句，随后就任两人自己聊。老友讲印厂的油墨味，缝纫女工讲机针怎么断。有人只管叫侍者续咖啡，三杯一共九十赫勒，坐在旁边听了一下午。
  _effects: cash -0.9 (三杯咖啡和给侍者的小费); relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000699:child; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-31; skill; mood 0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-31_

- **1940.01** `vienna-historical-social-movement.public-meeting/stay-and-tidy-chairs` tags: history, historical_process, social_movement, organization, politics
  Hedwig整场没上台，散会后留下把折叠椅叠好，扫掉地上的瓜子壳。Klara帮着把借来的煤气灯还给店主，顺手记下谁走得最早。Wiener Psychoanalytische Vereinigung的人问Hedwig为何不发言，Hedwig说先把椅子还齐。
  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1940.02** `vienna-legacy-worklife.union-office/union-office.write-off` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  账本的纸边已经发毛，Hedwig翻到那一页时，手指沾上一层灰。有人说“三十四克朗够买两袋面粉”，没人接话。最后举手表决，赞成销账的手比反对的多两只，老成员把手放下去时，椅子又晃了一下。
  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9; flag +union_debt_written_off; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-102_

- **1941.01** `vienna-family-childbirth/daughter-born` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, birth, state, state:raising-child, raising-child
  Hedwig在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang生下一个女婴，取名Luise Bauer。接生的人收六克朗，Otto Bauer在走廊烧水，邻居借来一只摇篮；Hedwig起身时眼前发黑，三天后腰还像断了一样，孩子哭声响亮，把屋里的冷气都顶了回去。
  _effects: birth female; cash -6 (midwife fee（其中6克朗记入债务）); state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:547_

- **1941.01** `curse15-notice.the-second-illness::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/calls-it-a-fever` tags: curse, notice, illness, fear, knowledge, supernatural, vienna, urban
  医生说只是换季的烧，Hedwig便也这么说。第二次退烧后，Hedwig把那张写满日期的纸烧了，灰倒进花盆。花盆里的植物活了下来，Hedwig因此更愿意相信巧合，也不再去数下一个十一天。
  _effects: mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1941.01** `r13-civ.new-district-lodging::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/key-money-demanded` tags: social, housing, lodging, landlady, district, vienna, urban, crisis
  Anna Bauer收了房租，又说得再付四克朗的“钥匙钱”，否则前一个房客的家具不搬走。Hedwig在门口站了一会儿，还是数了钱。进屋后才发现，床底下塞着前房客的一只破靴子。
  _effects: cash -10 (新城区门牌下的招租纸（其中10克朗记入债务）); mood -0.020000000000000018; flag +lodging_key_money_paid; knowledge “the previous lodger’s furniture was held for key money”_

- **1941.10** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_scald_hand` tags: home, social, family, housing, injury
  Hedwig在合租房走廊的公用灶台上煮土豆，转身取盐时袖子扫翻了铁锅，滚水浇在左手背上。Luise Moser从隔壁冲出来，扯下自己的围裙裹住烫伤处，又跑下楼叫来住在二楼的接生婆。接生婆用蛋清和旧亚麻布包扎，嘱咐三天不能沾水。
  _effects: health -0.03 (左手背烫伤); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2_

- **1941.10** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-pay` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Hedwig把通知念给Josef听，Josef摇头说是楼梯间那几块新玻璃的账。Hedwig去楼下杂货铺换开一张五克朗的纸币，回屋一枚一枚摆开数，煤箱底多留了二十赫勒。
  _effects: cash -2 (按月多付两克朗房租（其中2克朗记入债务）)_

- **1942.01** `vienna-family-childbirth/daughter-born` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, birth, state, state:raising-child, raising-child
  Hedwig在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang生下一个女婴，取名Berta Bauer。接生的人收六克朗，Otto Bauer在走廊烧水，邻居借来一只摇篮；Hedwig起身时眼前发黑，三天后腰还像断了一样，孩子哭声响亮，把屋里的冷气都顶了回去。
  _effects: birth female; cash -6 (midwife fee（其中6克朗记入债务）); state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:565_

- **1942.01** `pc20-wish.literal-return::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/curse-lifted` tags: curse, wish, ordinary_life, second_chance, love, work, literal, irony
  Hedwig在饭桌上把那件旧事讲完，没有求原谅，也没有再许愿。Otto Bauer听完，把汤勺放回锅里，说这顿饭终于只是顿饭；那天夜里，Hedwig第一次没有梦见门被推开。
  _effects: state_remove cursed-by-wish state:world-vienna-1912:seed:person-phase15-Hedwig-world-vienna-1912:cursed-by-wish; state_add pc20-second-chance state:world-vienna-1912:566; health 0.03 (a night without the old dream); flag +curse_lifted_in_ordinary_life; knowledge “那顿饭没有许愿，也没有求原谅，诅咒却没有再回来。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-91; flag +second_chance_open_

- **1942.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/learns` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Hedwig把Luise Bauer的旧课本包上书皮，又在本子上签了名。Luise Bauer今年学会了自己系鞋带，也学会了在饭桌上说“不”；Hedwig听着，觉得屋子比去年更满。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 20 -> 28; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 45 -> 42; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1942.01** `r13-civ.donate-clothes::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/coat-donated` tags: social, charity, clothing, poverty, household, vienna, urban, crisis
  Hedwig把父亲留下的厚呢外套从箱底翻出来，掸掉樟脑味，叠好交给Klara。Klara摸了摸袖口，说这料子还能穿三年，就在收据上写了“男式冬衣一件”。回家时，箱子空出一角。
  _effects: reputation 0.06; flag +donated_winter_coat; knowledge “the coat was logged as a man’s winter coat”_

- **1942.08** `r1-fam.landlord-harassment::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/landlord.water-strike` tags: social, housing, landlord, harassment, tenants, vienna, urban, crisis
  Hedwig没有交那笔“钥匙钱”，Friedrich Berg就把总水阀关了。三天后，Hedwig在院子里支起一口锅，把自家的水分给全楼。Friedrich Berg来的时候，院子里站满了人，谁也没有说话；他站了一会儿，自己把水阀打开了。
  _effects: cash -2 (房东的万能钥匙（其中1克朗记入债务）); reputation 0.10000000000000003; flag +water_shutoff_answered_with_a_shared_pot; role; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001282:child; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71_

- **1942.10** `ad20-former-employer::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-door-is-open` tags: work, employer, return, everyday, stigma, public, vienna, urban
  k. u. k. Landwehr-Infanterie, Wiener Ergänzungsbezirk在门口认出Hedwig，先问家里怎么样，又问这些年在哪。Hedwig说换了几份工；k. u. k. Landwehr-Infanterie, Wiener Ergänzungsbezirk点点头，说“有空来坐”。两个人都知道这不是一句工作邀请，但谁也没有把话挑明。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +old_employer_cordial; cash 1 (a small gift from the old employer)_

- **1943.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/compromise_split_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Hedwig把洗好的床单搭上院子里的公用晾衣绳，占了整条。Luise Moser抱着一盆湿衣服下来，说自家孩子明天要穿干净衬衫上学。两人在绳子两头扯了一阵，最后Hedwig把床单对折，腾出一半，Luise Moser用木夹子把衬衫一件件排上去。谁也没道歉，但第二天见面都点了头。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:009188_

- **1943.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/clinic-treatment` tags: health, work, family
  Hedwig在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Hedwig把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.05 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages（其中8克朗记入债务）)_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:009188_

- **1943.01** `vienna-family-childbirth/son-born` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, birth, injury, state, state:raising-child
  Hedwig在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang生下一个男婴，取名Emil Bauer。工友凑的钱抵了一部分，家里还是从糖罐里数出六克朗给接生的人；Otto Bauer把消息带到工厂，回来时把唯一的摇篮重新刷了一遍，Hedwig躺在床上出了一身虚汗。
  _effects: birth male; health -0.05 (childbirth); cash -6 (midwife fee（其中6克朗记入债务）); state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:583_

- **1943.01** `hs18-blackout.cellar::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/hide-the-coal` tags: blackout, cellar, coal, neighbor, denunciation, history, vienna, urban
  Hedwig在地窖最里面看见两桶煤，桶盖上压着Mitzi Lechner家的旧毯子。Mitzi Lechner说这是给病人留的；楼上的巡逻队正在敲第一扇门。Hedwig把毯子重新铺平，又把自家的空桶挪到前面。
  _effects: state_add hs18-street-witness state:world-vienna-1912:584; relation acquaintance with person-mitzi-lechner; flag +hid_neighbor_coal; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1943.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/struggles` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Hedwig被叫到学校，老师说Berta Bauer这学期总在课上看窗外。回家的路上，Hedwig没有骂，只把买好的铅笔又数了一遍；晚饭时，两个人都吃得很少。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 20 -> 15; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 45 -> 50; cash -2 (extra lessons and replacement pencils（其中2克朗记入债务）); mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1943.01** `state_tick:pc20-second-chance/kept-word/small-relapse` tags: pc20-second-chance, second_chance, reconciliation, return, failure, trust, debt, hope
  Hedwig晚了半个钟头，进门时先解释路上的电车，又解释口袋里的收据。对方没有追问，只把已经凉掉的汤端回厨房；Hedwig听见锅盖碰在灶台上，知道这次不是天气的问题。
  _effects: state_parameter pc20-second-chance.credibility 55 -> 48; state_parameter pc20-second-chance.suspicion 25 -> 33; state_parameter pc20-second-chance.debt 25 -> 28; mood -0.030000000000000027_

  Hedwig把派工单从门缝底下捡起来，上面写着“疫后复工，逾期除名”。她把白线擦掉半截，走到街口又停住，因为岗亭里坐着人。回家后，Hedwig把派工单钉在墙上，像钉一张欠条。
  _effects: cash -1 (门框上的石灰线（其中1克朗记入债务）); mood -0.07000000000000006; flag +quarantine_job_threatened; knowledge “the employer threatens to strike quarantined workers from th”_

- **1943.01** `state_reaction:raising-child/school-note` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_reaction
  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Emil Bauer这周主动擦了黑板。Hedwig把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 20 -> 23; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  Elisabeth Lechner 2把合同推过来，说只要她们每星期跳三场，就付双倍的钱。Hedwig问要不要改舞步，Elisabeth Lechner 2说不用，越像现在这样越好。Luise Hofer看着合同上的数字，先笑了，又有点想哭。
  _effects: state_add ld18-public-desire state:world-vienna-1912:613; cash 2 (the first week of the scandal contract); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-37; mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +scandal_contract; flag +public_desire_active_

- **1943.12** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/drops-and-endure` tags: health, medical, family, money
  夜里疼醒时，Hedwig摸黑往棉球上又滴药水，手抖得洒在枕头上。Maria Bauer第二天一早来敲门，看见肿脸就说别硬撑了，可Hedwig只是摆摆手，说再熬一天也许就好了。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (牙痛药水); condition_

  Hedwig在《新维也纳日报》的失物栏里登了三行小字，花了四十个铜板。第二天，一个卖旧货的人说在电车终点站见过那样的铜牌。Gustav Hofer放学后跟着Hedwig去找，一路上把书包带绕在手指上，绕得发白。

  Hedwig又把那天的顺序在脑子里走了一遍：先是门响，再是靴子，最后才是那声喊。细节还在，只是每讲一次，旁边就多出一点别人替你补上的颜色。
  _effects: state_parameter hs18-street-witness.certainty 90 -> 92_

- **1944.01** `state_tick:ld18-public-desire/the-story-changes-hands/a-new-version` tags: ld18-public-desire, desire, public, scandal, reputation, body, rumor, notoriety
  故事又换了一个版本：有人说Hedwig是为了钱，有人说是为了爱，还有人说她只是喜欢看别人议论。Hedwig在咖啡馆听见第三种说法，没有更正，只把杯子往窗边挪了挪；阳光照进来，她第一次觉得这些版本都离自己很远。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-public-desire.rumorHeat 50 -> 46; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.spectacle 65 -> 67; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1944.01** `r13-edu.correspondence-course::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/cannot-read-lessons` tags: education, correspondence, adult_education, literacy, mail, vienna, urban, crisis
  Hedwig读到第三课就跟不上，题目里的“比例”和“折旧”都认不全。Hedwig把讲义压在箱底，直到邮差来问续费，才发现已经欠了三期。钱退不回来，Hedwig把没拆的信封当柴烧了。第二年，Hedwig在厂里听人念夜校的招生简章，才知道那门课还在办。
  _effects: cash -3 (邮包里的第七课（其中1.6克朗记入债务）); mood -0.07000000000000006_

- **1944.10** `statistical_death/childbirth` tags: death
  Hedwig生产后一直发热，腹部疼痛。接生的人说已经排干净了，但几天后高热不退，Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang的家里人连夜去请医生，仍没赶上。Otto Bauer在场。
  _effects: death (产后感染与出血); flag -second_chance_open; flag -something_is_missing; flag -public_desire_active_
